pin and the audit HEAD. Every citation rev 11 ADDS — `GET /products/{product}` with its `withSum` aggregate and `ProductData` formatter, the batch list's eager load and the POS-batch suggestion envelope with its FEFO computation, the counting creation/seed/assignment path with the counter-facing transformers and the shipped `BlindCountingTest` precedents, the rebalance threshold columns with `LocationScopeResolver`, and the stock-threshold writer — was opened and re-verified at this HEAD before it was written; the rows gate r10's citation audit marked wrong, incomplete or internally stale are corrected in place (change log). Every citation is a full path from the ERP repo root (`apps/api/`, `apps/web/`, `apps/pos/`, `packages/`, `docs/`, `.claude/`, vendor files under `apps/api/vendor/`, pnpm store files under `node_modules/.pnpm/`), repeated in full on every occurrence, with no ellipsis, no brace expansion and no bare `:line` continuation. Citations describing code this lane will WRITE are labelled "(edit target; today: <current code>)". Mobile citations name the sibling repo `/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile` (HEAD `51e3445`, re-checked).

> **Amendment A-1 (2026-09-12, S1 fix rounds 2–3, gate r3 inventory I-12/I-15/I-16):** §5.4's `summary{…}` shape is superseded. The shipped `TransferReconciliationSummaryData` carries only `lines`, `lines_with_discrepancy` and `freight_uncapitalized`; the six quantity totals (`total_sent`, `total_received`, `total_damaged`, `total_written_off`, `total_returned`, `total_remaining`) are NOT part of the contract because mixed-unit quantities are never summed. The same rule governs the immutable `StockTransferReceivedV1` / `StockTransferClosedV1` events (no cross-line quantity totals). S2–S4 must not re-add them; ruling recorded in plan rev 10 §7.12.

## Rev 11 change log (the single r10 MAJOR — completeness of the R5 surface inventory and of T9's executable presence controls — the r10 MINOR baseline pin with its structural closure sentence, the rows gate r10's rev-9 closure table left NOT CLOSED, the citation-audit rows, and the audit/false-positive/Preserve blocks)

| Finding | Disposition | Rev-11 anchor |
|---|---|---|
| **r10-M1, sub-item 1** — `GET /products/{product}` is absent from the R5 catalogue and from T9, although the T9 actor already holds `products.view` and the response emits a stock aggregate. The route is `Route::get('products/{product}', [ProductController::class, 'show'])` on `can:products.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:58-60`); `show()` adds `->withSum('stockLevels as stock_quantity', 'quantity')` to the product query (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:304-319`), `ProductData::fromModel` copies it through `stockQuantity()` as a scale-4 decimal string (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:131`, formatter at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:166-183`) and the DTO is returned whole (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:356-374`) | CLOSED by enumeration AND by an executable presence control. §5.0's R5 residual list gains item **10** naming the route, the `withSum` aggregate and the DTO formatter; §5.0's surface table gains **row 25**; §5.0b gains an **R5** response-shape row stating that the whole `ProductData` is present with `stock_quantity` as the company-wide `withSum` at scale 4, and that the cost members are nulled for this actor by `withoutCostFields()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:145-155`, applied at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:360-366`) because `U_blind` lacks `pricing.view_cost_prices`. T9 gains step **6t-c** — `GET /products/{P}` asserted 200 for `U_blind` with `assertSentinelsPresent(body, ['7391.4517'])` on `stock_quantity`, the same figure step 6t-a asserts on `totals.quantity` of `GET /products/{P}/stock-levels` and for the same reason (the L1 row is drawn to `0.0000` by the TransferOut of initiation, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:571-595`, and the new L3 row is created at `0.0000`) | §5.0 R5 list item 10, §5.0 row 25, §5.0b R5 product-show row, §10.1 table P row 6t, §10.1 step 6t, §10.1 step 1 |
| **r10-M1, sub-item 2** — the batch family names five endpoints but the response-shape table describes only batch show, batch stock and product-batch-stock. `GET /batches` resolves through `BatchRepository::getByCompany`, which eager-loads `batchStock` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Infrastructure/Persistence/BatchRepository.php:89-119`, the eager load at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Infrastructure/Persistence/BatchRepository.php:116-119`) and returns `BatchResource::collection` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:83-100`), so the list emits `total_quantity`, `available_quantity` and the per-location `batch_stock[]` triple (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Resources/BatchResource.php:39-40,55-60`). `GET /pos/products/{productId}/batches` emits a DIFFERENT envelope — `suggestions[].quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionDTO.php:27-41`, the quantity at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionDTO.php:33`) inside `suggestions`/`fully_fulfilled`/`shortfall`/`total_quantity_suggested` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionResultDTO.php:23-35`) | CLOSED. §5.0 R5 list item 8 now names the list's eager load and the POS suggestion envelope with those citations instead of stopping at show/stock/product-batch-stock, and §5.0 row 23 names all five with their emitters. §5.0b gains **two** new R5 rows — one for `GET /batches` (per-location `batch_stock[]` obtained by eager loading, plus the two roll-up accessors) and one for `GET /pos/products/{productId}/batches` (the suggestion envelope, with its two REQUIRED query parameters `location_id` and `quantity`, validated at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:295-306`) | §5.0 R5 list item 8, §5.0 row 23, §5.0b two new R5 batch rows |
| **r10-M1, sub-item 3** — counting reconciliation is claimed as a T9 presence assertion in §5.0/§5.0b while §10.1's own table records it as not exercised. The two statements cannot both be true | CLOSED by the FIRST option the gate allows — an executable control, not a downgrade of the claim. Step **5d** creates a real counting document at L2 through the shipped write path: as `U_admin`, `POST /inventory/countings` (`can:inventory.adjust`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:272-274`) with `scope_type: 'location'`, `scope_filters.location_ids: [L2]`, `include_zero_stock: false`, `requires_count_2: false`, `count_1_user_id: U_other`. `InventoryCountingService::create` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:73-120`) calls `generateCountingItems` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:133-169`), whose seeds copy `stock_levels.quantity` verbatim into `theoretical_qty` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:277-302`, the copy at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:297`) over the `quantity > 0` rows of the scoped location (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:505-559`, the predicate at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:558`), and `createAssignments` registers `U_other` as counter 1 (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:564-578`). Because the document is created AFTER step 5b, the three snapshots ARE the R5 control values: P `7391.4517`, PB `1017.2500`, PC `88.0000`. T9 gains step **6s-c** (as `U_blind`, `GET /inventory/countings/{C}/reconciliation` on `can:inventory.view`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:312-314`, controller `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:185-209`, payload `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingReconciliationPayloadBuilder.php:61-106` with `theoretical_qty` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingReconciliationPayloadBuilder.php:76`) asserting all three present, and step **6s-d** (as `U_other`, the counter-facing `counter-view` and `items/to-count`) asserting the same three ABSENT together with the key `theoretical_qty` at any depth — the existing blind-counting control, whose shipped precedents are `apps/api/tests/Feature/Inventory/BlindCountingTest.php:187-202`, `apps/api/tests/Feature/Inventory/BlindCountingTest.php:207-219` and `apps/api/tests/Feature/Inventory/BlindCountingTest.php:261-276`. No activation is required: neither counter endpoint has a status predicate (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:1053-1073`) and reconciliation only resolves the counting for the company (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:189`), so `POST /inventory/countings/{C}/activate` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:299-301`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:699`) is deliberately NOT called, and `replay_preview` stays `null` because the preview service returns `[]` outside `pending_review` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingReplayPreviewService.php:50-59`). §5.0 row 22 and the §5.0b counting row now name 6s-c/6s-d instead of the wrong step 6s, and §10.1's "not exercised" table row is replaced by the executable one | §5.0 row 22, §5.0b R5 counting row, §10.1 step 1, §10.1 step 5d, §10.1 table P row 6s-c/6s-d, §10.1 steps 6s-c and 6s-d |
| **r10-M1, sub-item 4** — rebalance presence is threshold-conditional and the step asserted only HTTP 200, which does not prove the stock-position fields stay visible | CLOSED by making the row deterministic in the fixture and asserting field VALUES. `StockRebalanceQueryService::rebalance` reads its thresholds from the `stock_levels` row itself — `min_quantity` and `max_quantity` are selected beside `quantity`/`reserved` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:36-49`) — and emits a product only when the in-scope cells yield BOTH a deficit and a surplus (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:61-115`, the pair requirement at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:92-94`). Since `U_blind`'s membership is L2-only and the controller intersects the request with `LocationScopeResolver::resolve` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMatrixController.php:51-61`, resolver at `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationScopeResolver.php:31-67`), a single in-scope location can never produce that pair — so step 1 adds a second in-scope location **L3** (company A, no movement of any kind, `U_blind`/`U_reconcile` members of L2 AND L3) and step 5d writes the two thresholds through `PUT /inventory/stock-levels/thresholds` (`can:inventory.adjust`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:66-68`, controller `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockLevelController.php:82-99`, writer `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockThresholdService.php:15-67`, which CREATES the absent L3 row at `quantity '0.0000'` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockThresholdService.php:45-51`): P at L3 `min_quantity '100.0000'`, P at L2 `max_quantity '1000.0000'`. Step **6t-b** now asserts 200 AND exactly one row, with `deficits[0] = {location_id: L3, available: '0.0000', min_quantity: '100.0000'}` and `surpluses[0] = {location_id: L2, available: '7391.4517', max_quantity: '1000.0000', excess: '6391.4517'}` — the on-hand sentinel asserted as a VALUE on the rebalance surface. PB and PC stay out of the payload by the same pair rule (each has one in-scope cell with a positive balance and no threshold, so the no-threshold branch produces a donor and no receiver, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:78-91`) | §5.0 row 21, §5.0b R5 rebalance row, §10.1 step 1, §10.1 step 5d, §10.1 table P row 6t, §10.1 step 6t |
| **r10-M1, sub-item 5** — T9's batch step exercised only show, stock and product-batch-stock, omitting the batch list and the POS-batch response that §5.0 names | CLOSED. Step **6u** becomes five requests: 6u-a `GET /batches/{B1}/stock`, 6u-b `GET /batches/{B1}`, 6u-c `GET /products/{PB}/batch-stock`, **6u-d `GET /batches?product_id=PB`** and **6u-e `GET /pos/products/{PB}/batches?location_id=L2&quantity=2000.0000`**, each asserted 200 for an actor holding NO batch read permission (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php:22,24,42,45,48`) and each asserted to contain `1017.2500` — in 6u-d as the `batch_stock[]` entry for L2 obtained by the repository's eager load, in 6u-e as `suggestions[0].quantity` and `total_quantity_suggested`. 6u-e requests MORE than the lot holds so the suggested quantity is the lot's own availability rather than an echo of the request (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:143-185`, the cap at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:176-178`), which makes the disclosure of the lot position the thing being asserted; its `shortfall` is the non-sentinel `982.7500`. Step 1 states that B1 is active, un-recalled and carries a FUTURE `expiry_date` so the FEFO predicates return it (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:81-112`). | Gate r10 "Preserve" (eleven items, `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-t2-t3-spec-codex-gate-r10.md:84-94`) | Intact — the paragraph below quotes every one of them verbatim. Rev 11 adds only enumeration and positive controls: no masking rule is relaxed, no permission is widened (`U_blind`'s permission set is unchanged; the fixture's new reach comes from an extra LOCATION membership, not an extra permission), no guarantee sentence is broadened, and the `S`/`R` separation is preserved with `R` gaining no member | — |
| Gate r10 "Owner decisions required" — none; OD-1, close authority, OD-4 and R5 are applied rulings | Carried unchanged. §12 is byte-identical to rev 10 and rev 11 opens no product question. The step letters added in this revision are sub-letters of the existing 6s/6t/6u block precisely so that every "6p–6u" range statement in the document — §5.0, §10.1 and §12 — stays literally correct without editing §12 | §12 unchanged |

Nothing under the Preserve list of gate r10 changed. Quoted in full, that list requires the fix round to preserve: "The narrowed document-only blind guarantee and accepted R5 visibility of destination on-hand, available and lot-stock positions, including the multi-receiver `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` composition"; "§5.10 masking of carrying-transfer movement quantities for every receiver, with terminal and non-transfer history intact"; "The corrected Table-P arithmetic and the separation of forbidden `S` from required-present `R` values"; "Odoo-style partial receipt, hard over-receipt refusal, coarse `partially_received`, destination membership without assignment, and blind refusal of quantity-less completion"; "Both close dispositions, with `return_to_source` restoring source stock without GL and write-off using the existing shrinkage bridge"; "Quantity-based remainder in exactly the three aggregate readers, completed-row backfill, cancelled exclusion, scale-4 decimal strings and no floats"; "Immutable existing events, stored header/per-line receipt facts, receipt-id aggregate anchoring, after-commit integration dispatch and worker independence from `CompanyContext`"; "Header-first concurrency, company-wide idempotency keys, payload-hash mismatch 422, reserved `sys:` keys and PG partial uniqueness"; "Reconcile and close permissions seeded to manager/admin, independently grantable, with close requiring both"; "One receipt writer with `/complete` delegation, generated entity DTOs and mobile/web receiver projections blind by construction"; and "DB notifications and web bell first; transfers before PO receiving; freight residual/no-journal and confirmed multi-receiver attribution" (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-t2-t3-spec-codex-gate-r10.md:84-94`). Every one of those eleven items is carried unchanged into rev 11. The narrowed document-only guarantee is untouched word for word (§5.0) and R5 gains three MORE recorded surfaces rather than losing any; §5.10's masking paragraph is not edited at all; the `S`/`R` separation is preserved exactly — `S` is unchanged and `R` keeps its three members `7391.4517`, `1017.2500` and `88.0000`, with the new fixture figures (`100.0000` and `1000.0000` thresholds, the `6391.4517` rebalance excess, the `982.7500` FEFO shortfall, the `2000.0000` FEFO request) deliberately chosen outside both sets so no existing assertion changes meaning; the Table-P arithmetic rows gate r10 marked VERIFIED are byte-identical. The additions of rev 11 are of exactly two kinds, both of which the gate asked for: RECORDS (three response shapes and one surface-table row that rev 10 omitted) and POSITIVE CONTROLS (five new or strengthened T9 assertions on stock positions the owner ruled visible). No masking, authorization or write path is changed by this revision, and the only fixture widening is a second in-scope LOCATION for the blind actor — L3, which holds no product movement, appears in no movement or entry-exit feed, and exists solely so the rebalance surface can emit the deficit/surplus pair its algorithm requires.

Vocabulary line (convention 11): Transfer, Transfer receipt, In-transit remainder, Discrepancy, Close, Blind receiving, Receiver view, Reconciliation, Visibility version — glossary rows in §2 (`docs/glossary.md` has no "Stock operations" section today; headings at `docs/glossary.md:13,22,32,48,67,80`); Receiver note ticketed (D2).

## 0. Industry baseline (benchmark-first — convention 10, `docs/conventions/10-BENCHMARK-FIRST-SPECS.md:37-56`)

Flow: inter-site stock transfer — ship, receive at destination, handle short/over/damaged, close the remainder (write-off or return), blind receipt, notify, audit. Reference systems: Odoo 17/18, ERPNext v15, Dolibarr (sources in the GL benchmark note; doc-page reads).

| # | Guarantee | Odoo | ERPNext | Dolibarr | AutoERP today (path:line @ 324b8a2c7) | Gap | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | Two-step transfer: ship, then destination confirms received quantities | Transit location, receipt at destination | Add to Transit → Receive at Warehouse | PO-only reception | `store` = initiate = decrement source + `in_transit` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:86,514,602`); `complete` takes no quantities (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:317-383`) | MISSING | MATCH (Q1) — `POST /stock-transfers/{id}/receive`, receipt document (§3.2) |
| B2 | Partial receipt / remainder | Backorder prompt | Remainder stays in transit | Partial PO reception | PO receipts only (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/GoodsReceiptService.php:1231-1234`) | MISSING | MATCH Odoo backorder — remainder stays in transit (`partially_received`); receive again or close |
| B3 | Short / over / damaged | Backorder or scrap; over by editing Done | Rejected qty + warehouse; over allowance % | Correct Stock | PO refuses over-receipt (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/GoodsReceiptService.php:326-345`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/GoodsReceiptFailureReason.php:9`); transfer line table has no discrepancy columns (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:74-94`) | MISSING | Q2: over REFUSED (typed 422); damaged at receipt with reason; short confirmed only at close with reason + alert |
| B4 | Blind receiving — what exactly is hidden. The reference implementation is **Oracle Retail Store Inventory Operations Cloud, Transfer Receiving**: "If configured for blind receiving, the expected inbound quantities will not display", containers being "received blind, without you knowing the expected quantity", with expected-vs-received compared after the receipt is confirmed and the store user keeping the normal inventory dialogs — i.e. the control is DOCUMENTARY, never a mask on the site's own on-hand (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:9`, reading at `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:16`). Racklify ("expected quantities, purchase order totals, or other confirming details"), DataDocks (anti-anchoring against confirmation bias) and the Odoo community request to hide the demand quantity on a receipt line agree; none hides on-hand (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:10-12`) | Not native | Not native | Not native | Counting is blind by construction (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/InventoryCountingController.php:334-377`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:54-74,161-177`); cash (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:58`); PO mobile types expose expected (`/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src/features/receiving/types.ts:58-73,98-109`) | MISSING (deliberate DIVERGE) | Q3: `company_fraud_settings.blind_receiving` (default false); transfers first, PO second (T-3b). **Scope MATCHES Oracle (owner ruling, `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`)**: what is hidden is the expected/sent/remaining quantity of the DOCUMENT being received, on the mobile receipt screen (the primary receiving surface, §9) and on the web receipt surfaces (§8); destination on-hand, available and lot stock stay visible under the existing permission model, and current-stock delta inference is accepted residual R5 (§5.0) |
| B5 | Notifications | Chatter | Doc-event notifications | Agenda | None for transfers; DB-notification module read-only (`apps/api/app/Modules/Notification/Presentation/routes.php:19-30`); bell by `type` (`apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:20-26,45-71`) | MISSING | Q4: DB channel + web bell; push later (§7) |

| B11 | Second company | Per company | Per company | Per entity | Transfer number unique per company (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:66`); GRN per company (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_04_100000_create_goods_receipts_tables.php:27-30`) | — | MATCH — document keys carry `company_id`; S-matrix |
| B12 | Second location | Per picking | Per warehouse | Per warehouse | Destination is a transfer attribute (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:45`) | — | MATCH — S-matrix |
| B13 | Correction / reversal | Return picking | Cancel + amend | Manual | Stock-adjustment document refuses `damage`/`write_off` on every batch-tracked product (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockAdjustmentDocumentService.php:520-531`) | MISSING | DIVERGE v1: under-report → further receipt; over-report → no `void` (ticketed); receipt rows immutable (§1) |

Domain norm: blind receiving is a WMS control against confirmation bias — the receiver counts without seeing the ASN quantity; the system compares afterwards and routes variances to a supervisor.

Second-of-everything (convention 09, `docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md:37-50`): the §10 S-matrix covers every new writer on all three axes. No `CATALOGUE_TABLES` entity is touched (`docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md:30-35`): the three receipt tables are documents, and the ratchet excludes primary/id keys (`docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md:54-59`), so relationship-scoped uniques without `company_id` are legitimate (list in §3.2; r3-m3).

## 1. Problem, definitions, scope

**Problem.** A transfer is decremented at source on `store` and incremented at destination by a quantity-less `complete` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:346-383`): the receiver cannot say "11 of 12 arrived, one broken". No receipt document exists, so nothing can be blind; the three in-transit readers derive incoming from `status = in_transit` alone (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:137-164,217-245`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:390-408`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:117-127`) and cannot express a partial receipt. Nobody is told a transfer is coming or arrived short.

**Definitions (M2 r1 — two different things).**
- **Open remainder**: per line, `sent − received − damaged − written_off − returned > 0` while the transfer is `in_transit` or `partially_received`. No reason, no alert, `has_discrepancy` false.
- **Confirmed discrepancy**: (a) **damaged** units declared on a receipt — reason required, alert at receipt; (b) **short** units confirmed by a **close** — write-off or return, reason required, alert at close. Shortness is never asked of a receiver (D2). A shortage confirmed at close is a **transfer-level fact**: its event actor is the closer, and it is never attributed to any receiver as that receiver's own report (§4.4, confirmed attribution ruling).

**In scope.** T-2: receipt document (per-line received/damaged, lot grain); remainder stays in transit; close with `disposition: write_off | return_to_source` (OQ-1) under reconcile + close authority; quantity-based in-transit derivation; stored header + per-line events with a real aggregate anchor; `complete` as the zero-discrepancy delegate for non-blind actors only. T-3: `blind_receiving`; receiver view by construction; server-side blind closure over every reachable TRANSFER-DERIVED surface incl. the POS feeds, the replenishment feeds and the two movement feeds (§5.0, §5.9, §5.10) — current-stock positions are deliberately NOT closed and are accepted residual R5 under the owner's ruling (§5.0, `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`); advisory `visibility_version` (§4.5); reconciliation; the mobile contract, in which the mobile receipt screen is the primary receiving surface and must be blind by construction (§9). T-4-min: two DB notifications (§7).

**Out of scope** (ticket each): push/Expo; `ReplenishmentRequestedNotification`; mobile screens (M-1); T-3b implementation; inter-company transfers (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:96-101` still refuses); over-receipt tolerance; alert threshold (v1 = any); **receipt `void`/reversal** — an over-reported receipt has no reversal in v1: a supervisor corrects on-hand with a stock-adjustment document (which refuses destructive reasons on every batch-tracked product, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockAdjustmentDocumentService.php:520-531` — such products go through the lot write-off path), while receipt rows, counters, reconciliation and terminal state stay as posted; **receiver note** (D2 ticket T-2b); **analytics read model / dashboard** (T-4 or later — this lane guarantees the stored facts and ships the reference query, §4.4); shrinkage account provisioning (exists: `apps/api/app/Modules/Accounting/Application/Services/InventoryVarianceAccountProvisioner.php:162-169`, purpose `InventoryShrinkageExpense`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Accounting/Domain/Enums/SystemAccountPurpose.php:65-68`); a sidebar `permissions` (any-of) prop (§8); a client-side purge guarantee for pre-flip data (§4.5, R4).

## 2. Vocabulary — glossary rows (`docs/glossary.md`, new section "Stock operations", same lane)

| Term | Definition | Table / module | Canonical surface | Synonyms |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Transfer** | Intracompany movement of stock from a source to a destination location: source decremented on initiate (`in_transit`), destination incremented by one or more transfer receipts. | `stock_transfers`, `stock_transfer_lines`, `stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations` / Inventory | Stock → Transfers; writers `StockTransferService` (initiate/cancel) + `StockTransferReceiptService` (receive/close; `complete` delegates) | transfert, stock transfer |
| **Transfer receipt** | Posted, immutable document recording what a destination physically received from one transfer at one moment (per line received, damaged, lots, reason), or a close, or a legacy completion backfilled from pre-receipt history. | `stock_transfer_receipts`, `stock_transfer_receipt_lines`, `stock_transfer_receipt_line_lots` / Inventory | Transfer detail → Receive; `POST /stock-transfers/{id}/receive` | réception de transfert |
| **In-transit remainder** | Per line/lot: `quantity − received − damaged − written_off − returned`; the only definition of "incoming" for stock readers; positive only in `in_transit`/`partially_received`. | derived; `StockTransferLine::REMAINDER_SQL` + `StockTransfer::CARRYING_STATUSES` | Matrix/location "incoming"; detail "remaining" (visibility-gated) | reste à recevoir, backorder |
| **Discrepancy** | Confirmed variance: damaged (at receipt) or short (at close). Carries `TransferDiscrepancyReason`, constrained per action. Over-receipt refused. An open remainder is NOT a discrepancy. | columns on `stock_transfer_receipt_lines` | Receive dialog; close dialog; reconciliation | écart, variance |
| **Close** | Terminal supervisor action (`inventory.transfers.reconcile` AND `inventory.transfers.close`) on a transfer with an open remainder, one disposition for the whole remainder: write-off (GL shrinkage) or return to source (no GL). Writes a receipt row of kind `close`. | `stock_transfers.status/closed_*/freight_uncapitalized`, `stock_transfer_receipts.kind = close` | Transfer detail → Close; `POST /stock-transfers/{id}/close` | clôture, close backorder |
| **Blind receiving** | `company_fraud_settings.blind_receiving` (default false): when on, a destination user without `inventory.transfers.reconcile` or source access cannot learn the expected/remaining quantity of an open transfer line from any TRANSFER, RECEIPT, REPLENISHMENT, MOVEMENT/ENTRY-EXIT or NOTIFICATION surface, cannot post a quantity-less completion, and cannot close (§5.0). The control is documentary (owner ruling `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`, benchmark `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:9,16`): destination on-hand, available and lot-stock POSITIONS stay visible and the delta they expose is accepted residual R5 (§5.0). | `company_fraud_settings.blind_receiving` / Compliance | Settings → Fraud & controls | réception à l'aveugle |
| **Receiver view** | Receiver-facing projection: identity, lines, lot identity, own prior receipts — never sent/expected/remaining, never sender-authored text or cost fields. Built only by `TransferReceiverPayloadBuilder`. | projection / Inventory | `GET /stock-transfers/{id}/receiver-view` | vue réceptionnaire |
| **Reconciliation** | Supervisor comparison sent vs received/damaged/written-off/returned/remaining per line and lot with reasons. Consumers: counting (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingDiscrepancyReportService.php:21`) and transfers (`TransferReconciliationService`). | projection / Inventory | `GET /stock-transfers/{id}/reconciliation` | rapprochement |
| **Visibility version** | Monotonic integer per company, initialised to 1 by the company-creation writer (I8) and bumped atomically on every fraud-settings controller write after that; carried by every transfer/incoming payload; ADVISORY — clients use it to discard cached transfer/incoming data on a mismatch (§4.5). | `company_fraud_settings.visibility_version` / Compliance | payload field `visibility_version` / `meta.visibility_version` | — |
