## 3. Domain model and schema contract

### 3.1 New columns and the migration guard recipe (r2-M7 / r3-M2 / r4-M2 — promise narrowed twice)

**Transactional fact.** `Migrator::runMigration` wraps `up()` in `$connection->transaction($callback)` whenever the schema grammar supports transactions and `$migration->withinTransaction` is true (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Migrations/Migrator.php:448-451`); `PostgresGrammar::$transactions = true` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Schema/Grammars/PostgresGrammar.php:18`, base default `false` at `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Schema/Grammars/Grammar.php:31`); `Migration::$withinTransaction = true` by default (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Migrations/Migration.php:19`) and none of this lane's migrations overrides it. Tenant migrations run per tenant DB through `tenants:migrate` (`.claude/context/architecture.md:70`, `docs/architecture/database.md:45`). On PostgreSQL a failing statement rolls the whole migration back: **a half-migrated PG schema cannot exist**.

**Logging fact (r4-M2).** `Migrator::run` selects only files absent from the migrations repository (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Migrations/Migrator.php:124-159`, `pendingMigrations` rejects every logged name at `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Migrations/Migrator.php:150-158`), and `runUp` logs a migration only AFTER `runMigration` returned (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Migrations/Migrator.php:252-258`). Consequences: (a) a rolled-back migration is unlogged, so the next `tenants:migrate` re-selects and re-executes it — that is the ONLY automatic recovery this spec claims; (b) a logged migration is never re-executed, so `tenants:migrate` does NOT repair post-commit drift (a column, constraint or index dropped by hand after the migration committed). The rev-4 S3 "drop one added column, one CHECK, one index, rerun → identical" test is deleted.

**Manual recovery for post-commit drift (documented, not automated).** An operator writes a NEW forward migration using the recipe below (its guards make re-creating the missing object a no-op everywhere else) and runs `tenants:migrate`; `tenants:run migrate:rollback` is never used for this lane's migrations because `down()` drops the receipt tables and their rows. The runbook line goes into `docs/handoff/PROMOTION-CHECKLIST-*` (§11 step 4).

**Recipe (each migration of §11 step 1).** (1) New tables: one `Schema::create` carrying the `uuid('id')->primary()` and every NOT NULL column (precedents `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:29-30`, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_04_100000_create_goods_receipts_tables.php:14-16,34-36`), guarded by `Schema::hasTable`; no `constrained()`/`unique()` inside the closure. (2) Added columns on existing tables: `Schema::hasColumn` per column (precedent `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_08_10_100000_add_inventory_valuation_mode_to_companies.php:32,65`); every added column has a DEFAULT or is nullable (list below), so the add is valid on populated tables. (3) FKs and CHECKs: named constraints via `ALTER TABLE <table> ADD CONSTRAINT <name> <definition>` only after `SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conname = ?` returns nothing (precedent `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_08_10_100000_add_inventory_valuation_mode_to_companies.php:43-52`; pgsql-only as `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_08_10_100000_add_inventory_valuation_mode_to_companies.php:39-41`). (4) Indexes: `CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX IF NOT EXISTS` (precedent `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_04_24_000002_add_z_report_alert_type_unique_index_to_fraud_alerts.php:39`). On the SQLite test lane (grammar transactions off) the per-statement guards make a rerun of an unlogged, partially applied SCHEMA migration (§11 steps 1a–1d) converge. **No such claim is made for the row-level backfill (§11 step 1e) on SQLite** (r6-m2): its recovery after an interruption is a PostgreSQL-only property — the whole `up()` rolls back, so a receipt header without its lines cannot persist and the unlogged migration simply re-runs — whereas on SQLite an interruption between the header insert and its lines would leave a row the "no receipt yet" predicate skips on rerun; there the dev-lane test asserts single-pass idempotency only (T11). S3 (§10) asserts rerun idempotency and catalog identity after a clean rerun only.

`stock_transfer_lines` (today `quantity decimal(15,4)`, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:86`; cast `decimal:4`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferLine.php:56-62`): `quantity_received`, `quantity_damaged`, `quantity_written_off`, `quantity_returned` — `decimal(15,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`. Named CHECK `stock_transfer_lines_received_within_sent`: each ≥ 0 AND `received + damaged + written_off + returned <= quantity`.

`stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations` (`quantity decimal(15,4)`, unique `(stock_transfer_line_id, batch_id)`, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_06_05_121000_create_stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations_table.php:25,28`): the same four columns + CHECK `stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations_received_within_sent`.

`stock_transfers`: `status` stays `string(20)` (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:42`; `closed_with_writeoff` = 20 chars). New nullable `closed_by_user_id` (FK users, nullOnDelete as `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:56-57`), `closed_at timestampTz`, `close_disposition string(20)`, `close_reason string(32)`, `close_note text`; new `freight_uncapitalized decimal(15,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` (§6.4; same scale as `transfer_cost` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:49` and `allocated_transfer_cost` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:88`, so `transfer_cost = Σ allocated + freight_uncapitalized` holds exactly — confirmed freight ruling).

`company_fraud_settings` (table `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_12_23_160000_create_company_fraud_settings_table.php:16-36`, uuid PK at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_12_23_160000_create_company_fraud_settings_table.php:17`, unique `company_id` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_12_23_160000_create_company_fraud_settings_table.php:35`): `blind_receiving boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false` and **`visibility_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`** (§4.5). Surfaces that MUST change in the same commit (r1-M6, r2-m1): `$attributes` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:53-64`), `@property` block (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:36`), `$fillable` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:108-120`), `casts()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:131-144`), `getDefaults()` + its array-shape PHPDoc (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:165-185`), `defaultsForVertical()` array-shape PHPDoc (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:195-200`); DTO `CompanyFraudSettingsData` ctor/`fromModel`/`fromDefaults` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Application/DTOs/CompanyFraudSettingsData.php:44,75,105`); controller validation (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:108-123`), write (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:146-149`) and `reset` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:194-203`). `visibility_version` is never client-writable (not in the validation list, not in `$fillable`; `reset` does not reset it).

### 3.1b Model contract — decimal strings end to end (r5-M4)

**Why casts, not the driver.** The precision contract's storage tier requires an Eloquent `decimal:N` cast on every money/quantity model property so reads return canonical strings (`docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:15-20`, cast rule at `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:19`), and its service tier forbids `(float)$model->decimalProp` and native arithmetic on those values (`docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:22-28`, forbidden list at `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:28`). PostgreSQL returns `numeric` as a string, but the SQLite test lane returns numeric affinity values, so the cast is the only portable guarantee. The PHPStan guard `ForbidFloatCastOnDecimalProperty` resolves the owning class and fires only on properties carrying a `decimal:*` cast (`apps/api/app/PHPStan/Rules/ForbidFloatCastOnDecimalProperty.php:20-35`, node type `Cast\Double` at `apps/api/app/PHPStan/Rules/ForbidFloatCastOnDecimalProperty.php:46-48`) — an un-cast column is invisible to it. Precedents for the exact shape: `StockTransferLine` declares `@property numeric-string $quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferLine.php:25-27`) with `'quantity' => 'decimal:4'` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferLine.php:56-63`); `StockTransferLineBatchAllocation` likewise (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferLineBatchAllocation.php:21,46-52`); `StockTransfer` casts `transfer_cost` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransfer.php:85-96`, `'transfer_cost' => 'decimal:4'` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransfer.php:91`); `CompanyFraudSettings` mixes `'integer'`, `'boolean'` and `'decimal:4'` casts (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:131-150`).

| Model (edit target / new) | PHPDoc `@property` (all `numeric-string` unless stated) | `$fillable` additions | `casts()` additions |
|---|---|---|---|
| `StockTransferLine` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferLine.php:18-35,42-51,56-63`) | `quantity_received`, `quantity_damaged`, `quantity_written_off`, `quantity_returned` | the same four (the receipt service writes them through `update()`; the initiate path never sets them) | each `=> 'decimal:4'` |
| `StockTransferLineBatchAllocation` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferLineBatchAllocation.php:15-28,35-41,46-52`) | the same four | the same four | each `=> 'decimal:4'` |
| `StockTransfer` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransfer.php:59-80,85-96`) | `freight_uncapitalized`; plus `string\|null $closed_by_user_id`, `Carbon\|null $closed_at`, `TransferCloseDisposition\|null $close_disposition`, `TransferDiscrepancyReason\|null $close_reason`, `string\|null $close_note` | all six | `'freight_uncapitalized' => 'decimal:4'`, `'closed_at' => 'datetime'`, `'close_disposition' => TransferCloseDisposition::class`, `'close_reason' => TransferDiscrepancyReason::class` |
| `StockTransferReceipt` (new, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/StockTransferReceipt.php`) | no quantity columns; `int $sequence`, `bool $is_blind`, `bool $has_discrepancy`, `TransferReceiptKind $kind`, `TransferCloseDisposition\|null $disposition`, `TransferReceiptStatus $status`, `Carbon $received_at` | every §3.2 column except `id` | `'sequence' => 'integer'`, `'is_blind' => 'boolean'`, `'has_discrepancy' => 'boolean'`, enum casts for `kind`/`disposition`/`status`, `'received_at' => 'datetime'` |
| `StockTransferReceiptLine` (new) | `quantity_received`, `quantity_damaged`, `quantity_written_off`, `quantity_returned`, `quantity_sent_snapshot`; `bool $is_lot_tracked`; `TransferDiscrepancyReason\|null $discrepancy_reason` | every §3.2 column except `id` | the five quantities `=> 'decimal:4'`, `'is_lot_tracked' => 'boolean'`, `'discrepancy_reason' => TransferDiscrepancyReason::class` |
| `StockTransferReceiptLineLot` (new) | the four quantities; `int $batch_id` | every §3.2 column except `id` | the four `=> 'decimal:4'`, `'batch_id' => 'integer'` |
| `CompanyFraudSettings` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:36,108-120,131-150`) | `bool $blind_receiving`, `int $visibility_version` | `blind_receiving` only (`visibility_version` stays non-fillable, §3.1) | `'blind_receiving' => 'boolean'`, `'visibility_version' => 'integer'` |

**Accessors and DTOs.** No model accessor, service method or DTO member for these columns is typed `float` or returns one: `StockTransferLine::remainingQuantity(): string` and `StockTransferLineBatchAllocation::remainingQuantity(): string` are `bcsub` chains at `QuantityScale::SCALE` (`apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/QuantityScale.php:20`); `StockTransferReceiptData`, `StockTransferReceiptLineData`, `StockTransferReceiptLineLotData`, `TransferReconciliationData`, the two transfer builders' line/allocation arrays and `CompanyFraudSettingsData::$visibility_version` (`int`) are constructor-promoted `public string $quantity_received` … / `public int $visibility_version` members, exactly as `GoodsReceiptLineData` types `received_qty`/`free_qty` as `public string` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/DTOs/GoodsReceiptLineData.php:20-21`). DTO builders take the cast attribute as-is (`$line->quantity_received`, already a string) — never `(string) (float)`; sums use `bcadd` at scale 4. **TypeScript.** `php artisan typescript:transform` emits `string` for a PHP `string` member — `GoodsReceiptLineData` is generated as `received_qty: string; free_qty: string;` (`packages/shared/types/generated.d.ts:1095-1096`) — so every new quantity/money member lands as `string` and `visibility_version` as `number`; the web/POS/mobile clients keep them as strings (`<QuantityInput>`, `formatQuantity`, `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:63-67`). Guards: `ForbidFloatCastOnDecimalProperty` (armed by the casts above), `ForbidHardcodedBcmathScale`, ESLint `no-parsefloat-on-money` (`docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:187-191`). T8 asserts the wire types.

### 3.2 Three new tables

`stock_transfer_receipts` — one row per posted receipt, close, or legacy completion: `id uuid PK`, `tenant_id uuid` (plain indexed, no FK — rationale `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:31-36`), `company_id` FK companies, `transfer_id` FK stock_transfers restrictOnDelete, `receipt_number string(80)`, `kind string(20)` (`TransferReceiptKind`), `disposition string(20) null` (non-null iff `kind = close`), `status string(20)` (`TransferReceiptStatus`), `sequence smallint` (1-based per transfer), `is_blind boolean`, `has_discrepancy boolean`, `idempotency_key string(128) NOT NULL` (r6-M2: the prefix `sys:` is a reserved server namespace — the only keys in it are `sys:complete:{transfer id}` of §5.6 and `sys:legacy-completion:{transfer id}` of §11 step 1e; a client key starting with `sys:` is refused by rule A6 of §5.1 before any lookup, so a client can never pre-empt them under the company-wide unique; the unique index itself is unchanged), `payload_hash char(64) NOT NULL`, `received_by_user_id` FK users restrictOnDelete, `received_at timestampTz`, `notes text null` (receiver-authored), `timestampsTz`. Uniques **with** `company_id` (document identity per company): `(tenant_id, company_id, receipt_number)`, `(tenant_id, company_id, idempotency_key)`. Unique **without** `company_id` (parent-scoped by uuid, r3-m3): `(transfer_id, sequence)`. Indexes: `(tenant_id, company_id, transfer_id)`, `(transfer_id, received_at)`. CHECK `stock_transfer_receipts_close_has_disposition`: `(kind = 'close') = (disposition IS NOT NULL)`.

`stock_transfer_receipt_lines`: `id`, `receipt_id` FK cascade, `transfer_line_id` FK, `tenant_id`, `company_id`, `product_id`, `variant_id null`, **`is_lot_tracked boolean NOT NULL`** (r3-M3), `quantity_received`, `quantity_damaged`, `quantity_written_off`, `quantity_returned` (`decimal(15,4)`), `quantity_sent_snapshot decimal(15,4)` (audit only, never projected to a blind actor), `discrepancy_reason string(32) null`, `discrepancy_note text null`, `in_movement_id uuid null`, `scrap_movement_id uuid null`, `return_movement_id uuid null`, `timestampsTz`. Unique without `company_id` (parent-scoped): `(receipt_id, transfer_line_id)`; partial uniques on each movement id (`WHERE <movement column> IS NOT NULL`) (uniqueness precedents `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_04_100000_create_goods_receipts_tables.php:61-70`, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_08_08_120000_create_stock_adjustments_tables.php:123-139`; written with `IF NOT EXISTS` here). CHECKs: all four ≥ 0; `received + damaged + written_off + returned > 0`; `(written_off > 0 OR returned > 0) → received = 0 AND damaged = 0`; **`stock_transfer_receipt_lines_lot_lines_have_no_parent_movement`: `is_lot_tracked = false OR (in_movement_id IS NULL AND scrap_movement_id IS NULL AND return_movement_id IS NULL)`**. Service invariant (T3/T15): on a lot-less line each movement id is non-null iff its quantity is > 0.

`stock_transfer_receipt_line_lots`: `id`, `receipt_line_id` FK cascade, `batch_allocation_id` FK, `tenant_id`, `company_id`, **`batch_id unsignedBigInteger NOT NULL`** (r6-m3 — the width `foreignId()` produces and `product_batches.id` has: `$table->id()` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_01_05_150000_create_product_batches_table.php:14`; sibling `foreignId('batch_id')` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_06_05_121000_create_stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations_table.php:24`; explicit `unsignedBigInteger('batch_id')` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_08_08_120000_create_stock_adjustments_tables.php:101-104`; never `integer`; FK to `product_batches` added in the guarded pass of §3.1 step 3; PHP `int` + `integer` cast of §3.1b unchanged), the same four quantities, the three movement ids (same partial uniques; **authoritative for lot-tracked lines — exactly one movement id per lot row per movement kind with quantity > 0**). Unique without `company_id` (parent-scoped): `(receipt_line_id, batch_allocation_id)`. CHECKs: all four ≥ 0; **`stock_transfer_receipt_line_lots_positive`: `received + damaged + written_off + returned > 0`** (an all-zero lot row is rejected at the request layer and can never reach the table — r5-M1). Every UNIQUE and CHECK of the three tables has a named request or service rule in the §5.1 validation table; the database constraint is the last line of defence, never the first.

Numbering: `DocumentNumberingService::generateForKey($tenantId, $companyId, 'stock_transfer_receipt', 'TRR')` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Document/Domain/Services/DocumentNumberingService.php:24-39`; precedent `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/GoodsReceiptService.php:272-277`), `TRR-{YYYY}-{NNNN}` per company. Legacy completions: `TRR-LEGACY-{transfer_number}` (unique per company because `transfer_number` is; hence `string(80)`).

### 3.3 Enums (rule 9)

- `TransferStatus` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/TransferStatus.php:17-20`) gains `PartiallyReceived = 'partially_received'`, `ClosedWithWriteoff = 'closed_with_writeoff'`, `ClosedReturned = 'closed_returned'`. `isTerminal()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/TransferStatus.php:22-25`) → Completed | Cancelled | ClosedWithWriteoff | ClosedReturned; `canBeCompleted()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/TransferStatus.php:32-35`) → InTransit | PartiallyReceived; `canBeCancelled()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/TransferStatus.php:37-40`) unchanged; new `canReceive()`, `canBeClosed()`, `isCarrying()` → InTransit | PartiallyReceived; `label()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/TransferStatus.php:42-50`) extended. `packages/shared/types/generated.d.ts:1240` regenerates.
- `TransferReceiptKind`: `Receipt = 'receipt'`, `Close = 'close'`, `LegacyCompletion = 'legacy_completion'` (backfill only — never written by the service, never carried by an event; M2). `TransferCloseDisposition`: `WriteOff`, `ReturnToSource`. `TransferReceiptStatus`: `Posted` (single case).
- `TransferDiscrepancyReason` (r3-M4): `ShortShipped`, `LostInTransit`, `DamagedInTransit`, `Other`; **no `movementReason()`** — the movement reason derives from the action (§6.1). `allowedFor(TransferReceiptKind $kind): bool` — `Receipt` accepts `DamagedInTransit | Other` (a receiver declares physical damage, never shortness — D2); `Close` accepts all four. Outside the set → 422 `DISCREPANCY_REASON_INVALID`.
- `TransferReceiptFailureReason` (typed 422, mirrors `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/GoodsReceiptFailureReason.php:9-21`): `OverReceipt`, `LotOverReceipt`, `UnknownLot`, `LotRequired`, **`LotNotAllowed`** (lot rows on a lot-less line), **`LotSumMismatch`** (Σ lot received ≠ line received or Σ lot damaged ≠ line damaged), **`LotTrackingMismatch`** (r7-m3 — the shipment grain the SERVICE derives from the transfer line's batch allocations (§5.1 rule 5: "the line has ≥ 1 batch allocation") contradicts the product's current `requires_batch_tracking` flag, i.e. rule B8 of §5.1; `is_lot_tracked` is not a request field and nothing "submitted" is compared), `LineNotOnTransfer`, `NothingToReceive`, `DiscrepancyReasonRequired`, `DiscrepancyReasonInvalid`, `IdempotencyKeyReused`, `DispositionRequired`, `BlindRequiresCountedReceipt` (B1), `ValuationModeUnsupported` (M3) — the three bold cases are r5-M1. `message()` returns a static sentence with no numbers. State errors keep `TransferStateException` → `INVALID_TRANSFER_STATE` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockTransferController.php:389-402`).
- `StockMovementReferenceType` gains `StockTransferReceipt = 'stock_transfer_receipt'` (precedent `apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/Enums/StockMovementReferenceType.php:72`) — scrap/write-off movements only. `TransferIn`/`TransferOut` keep `reference_type = StockTransfer::class` as `markMovementAsTransfer` writes (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:745-752`).
- `TransferActorRole` (event payload only): `Receiver = 'receiver'`, `Closer = 'closer'` (§4.2).

### 3.4 State machine

```
draft ─initiate─▶ in_transit ─receive(partial)─▶ partially_received ─receive(rest)/complete*─▶ completed
                     │  │                                 │
                     │  └─receive(all)/complete*─▶ completed │
                     │                                     └─close(write_off)†─▶ closed_with_writeoff
                     ├─close(write_off)†─▶ closed_with_writeoff      └─close(return_to_source)†─▶ closed_returned
                     ├─close(return_to_source)†─▶ closed_returned
                     └─cancel─▶ cancelled        (partially_received: cancel REFUSED 422 INVALID_TRANSFER_STATE)
draft ─cancel─▶ cancelled     (* complete only when canSeeExpected — §5.6; † close only with reconcile AND close — §5.2)
```
Terminal rule kept from `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:386-394`: status persisted BEFORE `capitalizeTransferCost` so the in-transit re-read in `recordCostAdjustment` does not double count. Concurrency model: the header `lockForUpdate` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:734-742`) SERIALISES writers, it does not invalidate the second one — whichever request runs second sees the first's state and is judged by `canReceive()/canBeClosed()` on that state (r2-M1; T7b/T7c).

### 3.5 In-transit remainder — three readers move from status-based to quantity-based (preserved; r3-m2 shape)

Shared symbols: `StockTransferLine::REMAINDER_SQL = '(stock_transfer_lines.quantity - stock_transfer_lines.quantity_received - stock_transfer_lines.quantity_damaged - stock_transfer_lines.quantity_written_off - stock_transfer_lines.quantity_returned)'` and `StockTransfer::CARRYING_STATUSES = [TransferStatus::InTransit->value, TransferStatus::PartiallyReceived->value]` (a cancelled transfer restocks source, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:452-481`, with counters still 0 — the status predicate stays necessary). The three readers are NOT rooted in a `StockTransfer` Eloquent builder, so no Eloquent scope is applied there; each root replaces its status equality with `whereIn('stock_transfers.status', StockTransfer::CARRYING_STATUSES)` and its `SUM(quantity)` with `SUM(REMAINDER_SQL)`:
- `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php` — root `DB::table('stock_transfer_lines')->join('stock_transfers', 'stock_transfer_lines.transfer_id', '=', 'stock_transfers.id')` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:139-140` and `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:223-224`): predicate at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:144` and `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:228`; aggregate at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:156` and `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:233`; rounding stays FLOOR (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:160-164,242`).
- `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php` — same query-builder root (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:396-397`): predicate at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:402`; aggregate inside the `selectRaw` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:404`; rounding stays HALF_UP (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:379-382`) — not harmonised in this lane.
- `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php` — Eloquent root `StockTransferLine::query()->join('stock_transfers', 'stock_transfers.id', '=', 'stock_transfer_lines.transfer_id')` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:119-120`): predicate at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:124` (`whereIn` on the same constant); the `->sum('stock_transfer_lines.quantity')` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:125` becomes `->selectRaw('COALESCE(SUM('.StockTransferLine::REMAINDER_SQL.'), 0) AS remainder')->value('remainder')`; `bcadd` at the working scale (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:127`).
`StockTransfer::scopeCarryingInTransit()` (Eloquent, `whereIn` on the same constant) exists for the transfer list/reconciliation queries only. Guard: `TransferInTransitReadersUseRemainderTest` asserts, for exactly the three reader files, (a) no literal `TransferStatus::InTransit` and (b) both `REMAINDER_SQL` and `CARRYING_STATUSES` present; liveness fixture per `docs/conventions/08-DETECTOR-LIVENESS.md`. `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:441` is outside the scan set.

### 3.6 Invariants (each a test in §10)

I1 Per line and per lot: `received + damaged + written_off + returned ≤ sent` (named CHECK + service `bccomp` at `QTY_SCALE = 4`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:63` is the constant; the assertion is new code).
I2 Company-owned quantity = on-hand + in-transit (`WeightedAverageCostService::companyOwnedQuantity`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:100-127`). Initiate: unchanged. Receipt: unchanged. Damage/write-off: TransferIn +1 then scrap −1 at destination, −1 remainder → **−1 exactly once**. Return: unchanged.
I3 Batch-tracked lines: every received/damaged/written-off/returned lot ⊆ the line's shipped allocations; lot sums = line grain; missing `lots` → `LOT_REQUIRED`; parent movement ids NULL, lot movement ids authoritative (r3-M3).
I4 A receipt is immutable; **for every transfer**, each line's and allocation's four counters = Σ of its receipt lines / receipt lots (including the `legacy_completion` receipt of a pre-feature completed transfer — §11 step 1e); reconciliation asserts; drift = data bug (M2).
I5 Carrying invariant: `in_transit`/`partially_received` ⇔ Σ remainder > 0 (`in_transit` ⇔ no receipt row yet); `completed` ⇔ Σ remainder = 0 with `written_off = returned = 0`; `closed_*` ⇔ Σ remainder = 0 with the matching close counter > 0. `cancelled` is outside this invariant (counters 0, reader scope excludes it).
I6 Idempotency: `(tenant_id, company_id, idempotency_key)` unique; equal `payload_hash` → stored receipt (200); unequal hash or different `transfer_id` → `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED`. Keys are company-wide, not per transfer, so every server-generated key lives in the reserved `sys:` namespace and no client-supplied key may start with `sys:` (rule A6 — r6-M2).
I7 Freight identity: `transfer_cost = Σ lines.allocated_transfer_cost + freight_uncapitalized` at 4 dp on every terminal transfer (§6.4).
I8 Visibility version: the row is INITIALISED at `visibility_version = 1` by exactly one writer outside the controller — `CompanyFraudSettingsService::ensureForCompany()` (`firstOrCreate`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Application/Services/CompanyFraudSettingsService.php:18-24`), invoked by `EnsureFraudSettingsOnCompanyCreated` on `CompanyCreated` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Listeners/EnsureFraudSettingsOnCompanyCreated.php:17-20`, wired in `$listen` at `apps/api/app/Providers/EventServiceProvider.php:69-73`); it writes `getDefaults()` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/CompanyFraudSettings.php:165-185`, which does not carry the column, so the column DEFAULT 1 of §3.1 applies), emits no `ReceivingControlsChangedV1`, and no seeder writes `company_fraud_settings` (r6-m1). After initialisation, every committed CONTROLLER mutation of the row (`update`, `reset` — §4.5, which also covers the no-row case through its own `insertOrIgnore` at version 1 followed by the bump to 2) advances `visibility_version` by exactly 1 (atomic `UPDATE … + 1 … RETURNING`, under the row lock — §4.5); every `blind_receiving` flip has exactly one `ReceivingControlsChangedV1` whose `aggregate_version` equals the value RETURNED by that write; concurrent writers serialise on the row (T19, T19b).

---

## 4. Event sourcing (B3, owner pattern-detection requirement)

New stored events extend `App\Shared\Domain\Events\DomainEvent` (`apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/Events/DomainEvent.php:16`, Spatie `ShouldBeStored`), template `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Events/GoodsReceived.php:18-42`. Payloads are scalars, 4-dp numeric strings and lists of such (serialised to `stored_events.event_properties jsonb`, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_11_30_102448_create_stored_events_table.php:17`).

### 4.0 Persistence mechanism and aggregate anchor (B3)

**Fact (why `event()` is not enough).** Spatie's wildcard subscriber stores a `ShouldBeStored` event via `$this->repository->persist($event)` with NO uuid (`apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/EventSubscriber.php:34-38`); the repository writes `aggregate_uuid` from its `$uuid` argument only (`apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/Repositories/EloquentStoredEventRepository.php:101,118-121`) and never calls `aggregateRootUuid()`. Our `DomainEvent` constructor uuid (`apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/Events/DomainEvent.php:27-41`) therefore never reaches the row on direct dispatch, and its `metaData()` override (`apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/Events/DomainEvent.php:46-59`) also keeps Spatie's `MetaData::AGGREGATE_ROOT_UUID` out of `meta_data`. Spatie's own aggregate-root path is the only one that passes a uuid (`apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/AggregateRoots/AggregateRoot.php:135-145` → `persistMany($events, $this->uuid())`).

**Mechanism.** `StockTransferReceiptService` constructor-injects `Spatie\EventSourcing\StoredEvents\Repositories\StoredEventRepository` (interface, singleton-bound to `config('event-sourcing.stored_event_repository')` = `EloquentStoredEventRepository` at `apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/EventSourcingServiceProvider.php:76` and `apps/api/config/event-sourcing.php:74`). Inside the receipt transaction, after the rows are written, it calls `persist($headerEvent, $receiptId)` then `persist($lineEvent, $receiptId)` per line — **aggregate uuid = the receipt id for the header AND its line events** (one stream per receipt; `retrieveAll($receiptId)` returns the whole document, `apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/Repositories/EloquentStoredEventRepository.php:40-51`). Before persisting, the service sets `setAggregateRootVersion(1)` on the header and `2..N+1` on the lines in body order (`apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/ShouldBeStored.php:68-73`, read at `apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/Repositories/EloquentStoredEventRepository.php:121`), so the unique `(aggregate_uuid, aggregate_version)` (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_11_30_102448_create_stored_events_table.php:23`) makes a second stream for the same receipt id impossible. After each `persist`, `$storedEvent->handle()` runs sync projectors/reactors exactly as the subscriber does (`apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/EventSubscriber.php:36-37`; there are none in `apps/api/app/` today — the only aggregate-root file is the empty base `apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/AggregateRoot.php:17-20`). The events are NOT passed through `event()`: the subscriber would store a second, anchor-less copy. Spatie's `dispatch_events_from_aggregate_roots` is `false` (`apps/api/config/event-sourcing.php:147`) and stays so.

**Listeners.** In-process consumers get a plain, non-stored `StockTransferReceiptPosted` (`Dispatchable`, like `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Events/StockTransferInitiated.php:9-11`) dispatched in `DB::afterCommit` (precedent `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:406,492,608`): `receiptId, transferId, tenantId, companyId, kind, disposition|null, hasDiscrepancy, linesWithDiscrepancy, actorUserId, destinationLocationId, receiptNumber, transferNumber`. Wired via `Event::listen` in `InventoryServiceProvider::boot` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Providers/InventoryServiceProvider.php:89-96` style) to `NotifyOnTransferDiscrepancy`. `EventServiceProvider::$listen` (`apps/api/app/Providers/EventServiceProvider.php:145-148`) is not used for the stored classes.

### 4.1 Header events (one per posted document; aggregate uuid = `receiptId`, version 1)
- `StockTransferReceivedV1` (`inventory.stock_transfer.received.v1`): `receiptId, transferId, tenantId, companyId, transferNumber, receiptNumber, sequence, sourceLocationId, destinationLocationId, receivedByUserId, isBlind, hasDiscrepancy, previousStatus, newStatus, lineCount, totalReceived, totalDamaged, receiptNotes|null, idempotencyKey, payloadHash, freightUncapitalized` (non-zero only when this receipt is the terminal one), `lineEventVersions: list<int>`, `occurredAt`.
- `StockTransferClosedV1` (`inventory.stock_transfer.closed.v1`): `receiptId, transferId, tenantId, companyId, transferNumber, receiptNumber, sequence, sourceLocationId, destinationLocationId, closedByUserId, disposition, closeReason, closeNote|null, previousStatus, newStatus, lineCount, totalWrittenOff, totalReturned, idempotencyKey, payloadHash, freightUncapitalized, lineEventVersions, occurredAt`.

### 4.2 Per-line event — `StockTransferReceiptLineRecordedV1` (`inventory.stock_transfer.receipt_line.recorded.v1`; aggregate uuid = `receiptId`, versions 2..N+1)
Fields: `receiptLineId, receiptId, transferId, transferLineId, tenantId, companyId, kind, disposition|null, sequence, sourceLocationId, destinationLocationId, actorUserId, actorRole` (`receiver` for `kind = receipt`, `closer` for `kind = close` — r2-M6), `isBlind, productId, variantId|null, isLotTracked, quantitySent, quantityPreviouslyReceived, quantityPreviouslyDamaged, quantityReceived, quantityDamaged, quantityWrittenOff, quantityReturned, quantityRemainingAfter, discrepancyReason|null, discrepancyNote|null, inMovementId|null, scrapMovementId|null, returnMovementId|null` (**all three NULL when `isLotTracked`**, r3-M3), `lots: list<{receiptLotId, batchAllocationId, batchId, batchNumber, quantityReceived, quantityDamaged, quantityWrittenOff, quantityReturned, inMovementId|null, scrapMovementId|null, returnMovementId|null}>` (**empty when not lot-tracked; one entry per lot row, each id non-null iff its quantity > 0**), `occurredAt`.

### 4.3 Replay and atomicity contract
- **Replay**: from the three event classes alone, in `stored_events.id` order, a rebuild reproduces `stock_transfer_receipts` (all columns except `created_at/updated_at`, which the rebuilder sets to `occurredAt`; `status` = `posted`), `stock_transfer_receipt_lines` (incl. `is_lot_tracked` and the parent movement ids from the scalar fields), `stock_transfer_receipt_line_lots` (row ids and movement ids from `lots[]`), the four counters on lines and allocations, `stock_transfers.status/closed_*/freight_uncapitalized`. Receipts of kind `legacy_completion` have no stream: the rebuilder skips receipt ids without events and T15 asserts those rows are untouched. Stock levels are rebuilt by the movement stream, as today; `StockMovementRecordedV2` is not a replay source for this document.
- **Atomicity**: `persist()` writes the `stored_events` row synchronously (`apps/api/vendor/spatie/laravel-event-sourcing/src/StoredEvents/Repositories/EloquentStoredEventRepository.php:130-134`) inside the receipt transaction, so rows and events commit or roll back together. Movement events stay `DB::afterCommit` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:181-207,325-350`) and are not part of this replay set — the receipt events carry the movement ids themselves.
- Compatibility (rule 8): `StockTransferInitiated`/`StockTransferCancelled` unchanged; plain `StockTransferCompleted` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Events/StockTransferCompleted.php:9-26`) fires on the `completed` transition whichever path caused it.
- Replenishment settlement stays on initiate (`apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Application/Listeners/SettleRequestsOnTransferInitiated.php:22-44`, wired `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Providers/ReplenishmentServiceProvider.php:18-20`); no listener on close (OQ-2).

### 4.4 Pattern-detection reference query (owner requirement; attribution CONFIRMED — `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:106`)
Attribution rules: (1) per-line receipt events record exactly what each receiver posted (received/damaged, their identity, `actorRole = receiver`, blind flag); (2) a shortage discovered at close is a transfer-level fact — its line events carry `actorRole = closer` and are never counted as the closer's "mis-receiving" nor attributed to the last receiver; (3) the query is scoped per company and measures, per receiver, (a) damage reported vs the company baseline and (b) supervisor-confirmed variance on lines that receiver posted (receipt-line events joined to the close outcome on the same `transferLineId`); a line posted by several receivers counts for each of them with `shared_line = true`. Because `event_class` is indexed and `event_properties` is jsonb (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_11_30_102448_create_stored_events_table.php:16-17,20`), no new table is needed:
```sql
WITH e AS (SELECT event_properties AS p FROM stored_events
           WHERE event_class = 'App\Modules\Inventory\Domain\Events\StockTransferReceiptLineRecordedV1'
             AND created_at >= now() - interval '90 days'),
postings AS (SELECT p->>'companyId' company_id, p->>'actorUserId' user_id, p->>'transferLineId' line_id,
                    (p->>'quantityDamaged')::numeric damaged FROM e WHERE p->>'actorRole' = 'receiver'),
receipts AS (SELECT company_id, user_id, line_id, COUNT(*) postings, SUM(damaged) damaged
             FROM postings GROUP BY 1, 2, 3),
closes AS (SELECT DISTINCT p->>'transferLineId' line_id
           FROM e WHERE p->>'actorRole' = 'closer'
             AND ((p->>'quantityWrittenOff')::numeric > 0 OR (p->>'quantityReturned')::numeric > 0)),
baseline AS (SELECT company_id, AVG((damaged > 0)::int) damage_rate FROM receipts GROUP BY 1)
SELECT r.company_id, r.user_id, COUNT(*) lines_posted, SUM(r.postings) postings,
       AVG((r.damaged > 0)::int) damage_rate, b.damage_rate company_damage_rate,
       COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE c.line_id IS NOT NULL) lines_later_confirmed_short,
       bool_or((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM receipts r2 WHERE r2.line_id = r.line_id) > 1) shared_line
FROM receipts r JOIN baseline b USING (company_id) LEFT JOIN closes c USING (line_id)
WHERE r.company_id = :company_id GROUP BY 1, 2, b.damage_rate ORDER BY lines_later_confirmed_short DESC, damage_rate DESC;
```
**Grain (r5-m2).** The metric is LINE-based, as its column names say: `receipts` collapses every posting by the same receiver on the same transfer line into ONE row (`GROUP BY company_id, user_id, line_id`) before any rate is computed, so `lines_posted`, `damage_rate`, the company baseline and `lines_later_confirmed_short` each count a `(receiver, transfer line)` pair once however many partial receipts that receiver posted on it; `postings` is reported beside it for volume. `damaged` per pair is the SUM over the receiver's postings (a receiver who declares damage on any of their postings for a line has damaged that line). `closes` is `DISTINCT` per line so the left join cannot multiply rows. `shared_line` is true when more than one receiver posted on the line. T16 pins the shape, including a repeated-partial-receipt fixture. A T-4/later lane turns this into a read model + `fraud-alerts` rule.

**Close outcome covers BOTH dispositions (r7-m1).** §1 defines a confirmed discrepancy (b) as short units confirmed by a close — write-off OR return to source — and the per-line close event stores `quantityWrittenOff` and `quantityReturned` side by side (§4.2). The `closes` CTE therefore matches a line when EITHER is positive, and the output column is named `lines_later_confirmed_short` rather than "written off": a remainder returned to source is exactly as much a supervisor-confirmed shortage against the receiver's postings as one written off, and counting only the write-off disposition would let a company that always returns short remainders show a flat zero. `DISTINCT` per line still collapses a line closed once into one row, and a line can carry only one disposition (§5.2: one disposition for the whole remainder), so no line is double-counted.

### 4.5 Visibility version, the settings event, and what it does NOT guarantee (r3-B3, r4-B1, r4-M3)

- **Server truth (r4-M3).** `company_fraud_settings.visibility_version` starts at 1: the company-creation writer of I8 (`CompanyFraudSettingsService::ensureForCompany()`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Application/Services/CompanyFraudSettingsService.php:18-24`, via `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Listeners/EnsureFraudSettingsOnCompanyCreated.php:17-20`) creates the row at the column default and emits no settings event (r6-m1); the version moves only through the controller path that follows. Every write of the row — `FraudSettingsController::update` (today `updateOrCreate` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:146-149`) and `reset` (today `updateOrCreate` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:200-203`) — moves into ONE `DB::transaction` executing, in order: (1) `DB::table('company_fraud_settings')->insertOrIgnore([... CompanyFraudSettings::getDefaults(), 'id' => (string) Str::orderedUuid(), 'company_id' => $companyId, 'created_at' => now(), 'updated_at' => now()])` — PG `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Query/Builder.php:4132`, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Query/Grammars/PostgresGrammar.php:371`; the `HasUuids` id generator at `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Eloquent/Concerns/HasUuids.php:16` is bypassed by the query builder, hence the explicit id) against the unique `company_id` (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_12_23_160000_create_company_fraud_settings_table.php:35`) — this replaces `updateOrCreate`'s unguarded create race; (2) `CompanyFraudSettings::query()->where('company_id', $companyId)->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail()` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Query/Builder.php:3292`; PG `FOR UPDATE`, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Query/Grammars/PostgresGrammar.php:355-358`) — concurrent writers for the same company now serialise here; `$before = $settings->blind_receiving` is read from the locked row; (3) `$settings->fill($validated)->save()` (`reset`: fill with `Arr::except(CompanyFraudSettings::getDefaults(), self::REFUND_EXPOSURE_KEYS)` as today); (4) **one atomic bump**: `$row = DB::connection()->selectOne('UPDATE company_fraud_settings SET visibility_version = visibility_version + 1 WHERE id = ? RETURNING visibility_version', [$settings->id], false)` (`Connection::selectOne($query, $bindings, $useReadPdo)`, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Connection.php:355`; `false` forces the write PDO); `$version = (int) $row->visibility_version`; `$settings->setRawAttributes([...$settings->getAttributes(), 'visibility_version' => $version], true)` so the DTO returns the RETURNED value, never a hydrated stale one (Eloquent's `increment()` would advance the in-memory attribute from the pre-lock hydration, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Eloquent/Model.php:1048-1074` — not used); (5) the stored settings event below, only when `$before !== $settings->blind_receiving`. `RETURNING` is valid on PG and on SQLite ≥ 3.35 (test lane); no other SQL differs per driver.
- **Stored settings event.** `ReceivingControlsChangedV1` (`compliance.receiving_controls.changed.v1`; `App\Modules\Compliance\Domain\Events`, extends `DomainEvent` like the settings-audit precedent `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Domain/Events/TerminalTrainingModeChanged.php:15-31`) is persisted through `StoredEventRepository::persist($event, $settings->id)` with `setAggregateRootVersion($version)` — the RETURNED value of step 4 (the §4.0 mechanism; aggregate = the settings row; the unique `(aggregate_uuid, aggregate_version)` at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_11_30_102448_create_stored_events_table.php:23` can no longer collide because versions are handed out under the row lock). Payload: `settingsId, companyId, tenantId, changedByUserId, blindReceivingBefore, blindReceivingAfter, visibilityVersion, occurredAt`. Not routed through `event()` (§4.0 double-store fact), so `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Listeners/DomainEventSubscriber.php:1057-1071` is not involved.
- **Carriage.** `ReceivingControlsReader::visibilityVersion(string $companyId): int` (Shared contract, §5.3) and `TransferIncomingVisibility::visibilityVersion(string $companyId): int` (delegating) expose it; one query per request. Emitted as `visibility_version` inside both transfer builders' output (beside `blind`), inside the fraud-settings DTO, and as `meta.visibility_version` on stock-matrix, POS stock-levels, POS stock-distribution, `GET /replenishment-requests` and `GET /pos/replenishment-requests`.
- **What the version guarantees (r4-B1 — withdrawn client guarantee).** NOTHING on the server depends on the client's stored version: every payload is built by the visibility builder for the requesting actor at request time (no server-side response cache exists on any of these controllers — no `Cache::`/`remember(` in `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockTransferController.php`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMatrixController.php`, `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosStockLevelController.php`, `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/StockDistributionController.php`). `visibility_version` is ADVISORY: a cache-invalidation hint. Clients use it best-effort (§8, §8b, §9): drop cached transfer/incoming/distribution/replenishment data for the company when a response's version differs from the stored one, and keep blind-relevant queries at `staleTime: 0`. No client path is required to be "the first versioned response"; no atomic cross-cache primitive is claimed.
- **Residual (honest, R4 in §5.0).** A client may keep showing data it fetched before the flip until its next successful response for that surface (web: next mount/focus fetch; POS: next successful pull for that cache — 60 s tick online, indefinitely offline; mobile: next sync). No server path ever returns a hidden figure after the flip; the exposure is bounded by data the client had already legitimately received under the previous setting.
- **Activation semantics (OD-4, §12).** Enabling blind receiving is effective server-side at commit; clients converge on their next successful responses.

---

## 5. API contract

Module `Inventory`; group middleware `['api','auth:sanctum',SetPermissionsTeam::class,EnforceTokenTenantClaim::class,'module:Inventory']` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:31` (rule 12); transfer routes at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:98-117`. Envelopes: every NEW error of this lane is `{error:{code,message,details}}` as the typed helpers at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockTransferController.php:359-402` build it (the pre-existing `INVALID_TRANSFER` body at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockTransferController.php:187-195` omits `details` and is not touched — r4 citation audit); 422 typed, 403 `LOCATION_ACCESS_DENIED`, 404 for invisible transfers (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockTransferController.php:110-114`). Any-of permission gates use the existing alias `require.any.permission` (`apps/api/bootstrap/app.php:120` → `apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php:14-19`, comma-separated permissions, `$user->can()` per name), whose 403 body is static (`apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php:22-31`; edit target: the message literal at `apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php:25` is specific to its first consumer, "view transaction destinations", and becomes the generic static sentence "You do not have permission to perform this action." — still no number, no transfer field).

### 5.0 Blind guarantee and surface closure (B1, B2, r3-B1, r4-B1)

**Guarantee (server-side) — DOCUMENT quantities (narrowed by owner ruling `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`, r9-B1).** With `blind_receiving` on, an actor for whom `canSeeExpected(transfer)` is false (§5.3) cannot obtain, from any **transfer, receipt, replenishment, movement/entry-exit or notification** surface, at request time, the sent, expected, remaining, per-line or per-lot quantity of any line or lot of a receivable transfer (`in_transit`/`partially_received`) — neither directly, nor as an aggregate **derived from transfer data** equal to it, nor as a sum of transfer-linked stock-movement rows another destination receiver posted while the transfer is still carrying (§5.10, r8-B1), nor as a replenishment quantity linked to the transfer, nor as free text that may restate it (sender-authored transfer `notes`; requester-authored replenishment `note` on a transfer-linked row — r6-M1) — and cannot cause the system to post a quantity on their behalf (no quantity-less completion; no close). Every payload is built for the requesting actor when the request is served; nothing is served from a stored response.

**What the guarantee deliberately does NOT cover (the owner's scope, r9-B1).** The control is documentary, exactly as the reference systems implement it (§0 row B4; benchmark `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:9,16`): the receiver is denied the quantities of the DOCUMENT being received so the count is not anchored. The owner ruled option (b), "the Oracle approach" (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`): "The goal is not to omit quantities everywhere … everywhere else it's okay." Destination on-hand, available and lot-stock POSITIONS therefore stay visible to a blind actor under the existing permission model, and the delta they expose is accepted residual R5 below. The alternative — masking every current-stock projection for a blind actor while any transfer is carrying — was REJECTED by the owner: no benchmark system hides a shop's own stock from its staff, and it would break POS selling, counting and replenishment for the blind role (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:16-18`).

**Accepted residuals**: R1 probing `OVER_RECEIPT` reveals an upper bound (owner Q2); R2 after the transfer is terminal, destination stock history shows what landed; R3 the coarse status `partially_received` (OQ-3 owner ACCEPT); **R4 (client-side, r4-B1)** data a client fetched or rendered BEFORE the flip may remain visible on that client until its next successful response for that surface — the version gate of §4.5 shortens the window best-effort and is not part of the guarantee; **R5 (current-stock delta inference, owner-ruled, r9-B1)** below.

**R5 — current-stock positions at the destination (ACCEPTED, owner ruling `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`).** A blind actor keeps the ordinary stock-position surfaces its existing permissions already reach; none of them is masked, re-gated, re-typed or cache-dropped by this lane. Each is named here with the code that emits the quantity, re-verified at this revision's HEAD:

1. **`GET /stock-levels` list and `GET /stock-levels/{product}/{location}` show** — both on `can:inventory.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:57-64`), which a blind receiver holds (T9 step 1). `StockLevelData` emits `quantity`, `reserved`, `available`, `incoming` and `projected_available` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/DTOs/StockLevelData.php:18-32`, populated at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/DTOs/StockLevelData.php:61-80`).
2. **Stock matrix `cells[].on_hand`** — `cellsForRows` accumulates `on_hand` straight from `stock_levels.quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:287-298`) into the zero-filled cell shape (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockMatrixQueryService.php:224-246`). Only `cells[].incoming` is masked by §5.0 row 7; `on_hand`, `reserved`, `available`, `min_quantity` and `max_quantity` are not.
3. **`GET /pos/stock-levels` `stock[].quantity`** — emitted beside `reserved` and `available` (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosStockLevelController.php:85-103`). Only `incoming[].incoming_transfer` is masked by §5.0 row 9.
4. **`GET /pos/products/{product}/stock-distribution` per-location and total `on_hand`** — emitted beside `incoming_transfer` (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/StockDistributionController.php:80-99`), derived from `stock_levels` as `quantity − reserved` per location and summed (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:113-135`, assembly and totals at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/LocationStockQueryService.php:167-207`). Only `incoming_transfer` is masked by §5.0 row 8.
5. **`GET /products/{product}/stock-levels`** — `can:products.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:53-64`); per-location `StockLevelData` rows (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:1064-1094`) and company totals `quantity`/`reserved`/`available`/`incoming`/`projected_available` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:1096-1139`).
6. **`GET /inventory/stock-matrix/rebalance`** — `can:inventory.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:73-75`); per-location `available` read from `stock_levels` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:36-49`) and emitted as deficit/surplus rows with `available`, `min_quantity`, `max_quantity` and `excess` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:61-115`).
7. **Counting reconciliation** — `can:inventory.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:311-314`); the payload emits `theoretical_qty` per item (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingReconciliationPayloadBuilder.php:61-80`). The dedicated COUNTER endpoints stay blind by construction and are untouched (§0 row B4).
8. **Batch/lot surfaces** — `GET /batches`, `GET /batches/{uuid}`, `GET /batches/{uuid}/stock`, `GET /products/{productId}/batch-stock` and `GET /pos/products/{productId}/batches` require authentication and the BatchExpiry module but no read permission (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php:12-48`). Show loads `batchStock.location` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:105-116`) and `BatchResource` emits per-location `quantity`, `reserved_quantity` and `available_quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Resources/BatchResource.php:55-60`); the stock endpoint emits the same three per location (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:264-281`) and product-batch-stock returns the resource per batch (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:329-356`). **The LIST emits the same per-location triple (r10-M1)**: `BatchRepository::getByCompany` eager-loads `batchStock` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Infrastructure/Persistence/BatchRepository.php:89-119`, the eager load at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Infrastructure/Persistence/BatchRepository.php:116-119`) and the controller returns `BatchResource::collection` over it (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:83-100`), so every listed lot carries `batch_stock[]` with `quantity`, `reserved_quantity` and `available_quantity` per location (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Resources/BatchResource.php:55-60`) beside the two roll-up members `total_quantity` and `available_quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Resources/BatchResource.php:39-40`). **The POS route emits a DIFFERENT envelope (r10-M1)**: `suggestions[].quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionDTO.php:27-41`, the quantity member at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionDTO.php:33`) inside `suggestions`, `fully_fulfilled`, `shortfall` and `total_quantity_suggested` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionResultDTO.php:23-35`), computed by FEFO from each lot's own `available_quantity` and capped at the requested amount (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:143-185`, the cap at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:176-178`); it REQUIRES a `location_id` and a positive `quantity` query parameter (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:295-306`), so a per-location lot balance is exactly what it discloses.
9. **The POS device types that mirror those shapes** stay non-nullable: `ServerStockRow.quantity/reserved/available` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:45-61`) and `StockDistributionRow.on_hand` with `totals.on_hand` (`apps/pos/src/types/stockDistribution.ts:6-29`). Only `incoming_transfer` becomes nullable (§8b).
10. **`GET /products/{product}` show (r10-M1)** — `can:products.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:58-60`), the same permission item 5 names and the same one `U_blind` holds. The controller adds `->withSum('stockLevels as stock_quantity', 'quantity')` to the product query (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:304-319`), so the response carries a COMPANY-WIDE stock aggregate for the product — every location summed, with no location filter and no separate gate. `ProductData::fromModel` copies it at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:131` through `stockQuantity()`, which formats the eager-loaded aggregate as a scale-4 decimal string (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:166-183`), and the whole DTO is returned at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:356-374`. Cost members are nulled for an actor without `pricing.view_cost_prices` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:145-155`, applied at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:360-366`), which `U_blind` lacks — so this surface discloses the stock position and not the unit cost.

**The inference R5 accepts, stated exactly.** A receipt posts through `StockAdjustmentService::receive`, which reads `quantity_before`, computes `quantity_after` and writes the destination `stock_levels` row inside the same transaction (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:133-158`), and records the per-lot batch movement for a lot line (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:168-176`). A blind actor who samples any surface above before and after another receiver posts therefore learns that receiver's posted delta exactly — and per lot, through the batch surfaces. **The multi-receiver composition is named, not glossed over**: receiver A samples the destination position, a SECOND destination receiver B posts the remainder of a line while a different line keeps the transfer `partially_received`, A samples again (the delta is B's posting), and A then submits a positive quantity for that line and draws the typed 422 `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` (§5.1 rule B10), which proves the line's remainder is zero — so `sampled_before + delta` is the line's SENT quantity while the transfer is still receivable. That composition is accepted under this ruling; §10.1 asserts it is REACHABLE (positive controls 6p–6u) rather than pretending it is closed. What stays closed is every transfer-derived path to the same number: §5.10 still nulls the three quantity keys on both movement feeds for carrying transfers, §5.9 still nulls the replenishment quantities and note, the receiver builder still omits every expected/remaining member, and the typed error bodies still carry no figure.

### 5.0b Blind response-shape audit (exact fields; every shape a `canSeeExpected() = false` actor can receive)

**Completeness note (r9 citation audit; corrected again by gate r10's citation audit).** Gate r9 marked the rev-9 version of this table WRONG/incomplete: it enumerated only the transfer-derived shapes and omitted the stock-level, product-stock, rebalance, counting-reconciliation and batch-stock response families that the same actor reaches with the permissions it already holds. Rev 10 added those families but claimed "Rev 10 lists them all", and gate r10 found the claim still WRONG/incomplete on three counts, each now recorded rather than asserted away: (a) `GET /products/{product}` was missing entirely although the same actor's `products.view` reaches it and its response carries the `withSum` aggregate `stock_quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:58-60`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:304-319,356-374`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:131,166-183`); (b) the batch family named five endpoints but described three — the LIST obtains `batch_stock[]` through the repository's eager load (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Infrastructure/Persistence/BatchRepository.php:89-119,116-119`, emitted at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Resources/BatchResource.php:39-40,55-60`); (c) the POS-batch route emits a distinct suggestion envelope, not the batch resource (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionDTO.php:27-41`, `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionResultDTO.php:23-35`). Rev 11 adds one row for each of the three, so the count of R5 rows below matches the count of R5 surfaces in §5.0 (items 1–10, table rows 19–25); the enumeration is stated as a checkable correspondence rather than as the sentence "lists them all". The table now has two kinds of row: the transfer-derived shapes, whose third column states what is absent by construction or masked and which are inside the §5.0 guarantee; and the rows prefixed **R5**, which are stock positions the owner ruled visible (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`) and which are therefore listed with nothing absent. An R5 row is not a gap in the audit — it is the audit recording an accepted residual.

The choice is PER TRANSFER, so one list page may mix both shapes; the web wire types are unions discriminated by `blind` (r7-M2, §8), never the full DTO alone | as above |

That suffix carries a digit — the COUNT OF REMAINING VALIDATION MESSAGES, a property of the submitted payload's shape, never of the transfer — so the envelope stays quantity-safe. Every message of the §5.1 validation table is a fixed sentence naming the field only (e.g. "Each transfer line may appear at most once per receipt."); the rule set is structural (shape, uniqueness, positivity, reason set) and never reads the transfer, so no sent/remaining quantity can enter a message or the summary | any transfer quantity, any transfer field |
