### 5.1 `POST /stock-transfers/{id}/receive` — `can:inventory.transfers.complete` + `canAccessLocation(destination)`

Request (`ReceiveStockTransferRequest`; rule 19: `numeric` + `regex:/^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/`, accepts JSON numbers and numeric strings — ingress contract `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:30-40`, quantity regex at `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:35`; clients send strings):
```json
{ "idempotency_key": "client key (required; a UUID by convention; MUST NOT start with sys:)", "notes": "string|null",
  "lines": [ { "transfer_line_id": "uuid", "quantity_received": "11.0000", "quantity_damaged": "1.0000",
               "discrepancy_reason": "damaged_in_transit", "discrepancy_note": "box crushed",
               "lots": [ { "batch_id": 812, "quantity_received": "11.0000", "quantity_damaged": "1.0000" } ] } ] }
```
Rules:
1. **Idempotency first.** `DB::transaction(attempts: 3)` → `lockTransfer` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:734-742`) → lookup by `(tenant_id, company_id, idempotency_key)`: same `transfer_id` + equal `payload_hash` → stored receipt, 200, `meta.replayed = true`, regardless of state; different transfer or hash → `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED`. Only then `canReceive()` else `TransferStateException`.
2. **Key race across transfers.** Catch `UniqueConstraintViolationException` outside the transaction, re-read the committed winner by key (precedent `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:170-190`), apply rule 1's discriminator.
3. **Canonical payload hash.** `ReceiptPayloadCanonicalizer`: drop `idempotency_key`; `notes`/`discrepancy_note` absent ≡ null; quantities → 4-dp via `bcadd($q,'0',4)`; lines sorted by `transfer_line_id`, lots by `batch_id`; recursive `ksort`; `json_encode(JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES|JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE|JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)` (precedent `apps/api/app/Modules/Fiscal/Application/Services/TerminalRegistrySnapshotService.php:540-558`); sha256.
4. **Valuation preflight (M3).** If Σ `quantity_damaged` > 0: `InventoryValuationModeResolver::requirePerpetual(companyId)` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryValuationModeResolver.php:79-88`) BEFORE any movement; its `UnsupportedValuationModeException` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Exceptions/UnsupportedValuationModeException.php:22`, no HTTP mapping today) → typed 422 `VALUATION_MODE_UNSUPPORTED`, no rows.
5. **Validation contract (r5-M1) — every payload the schema forbids is refused before any write.** Two layers, both complete before the first row/movement/event; a failure at either layer leaves zero `stock_transfer_receipts`/`_lines`/`_lots` rows, zero movements, zero `stored_events` and unchanged counters (T5 asserts each case). **Layer A — `ReceiveStockTransferRequest`** (structural; runs in the FormRequest before the controller, hence before rule 1; sibling `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Requests/StoreStockTransferRequest.php:65-109`; cross-field rules via `withValidator()` → `$validator->after()` exactly as `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Requests/StockAdjustmentLineRules.php:85-144` (`withValidator` is invoked by `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Foundation/Http/FormRequest.php:94-95`; `after` at `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Validation/Validator.php:440`); failures are the framework `VALIDATION_ERROR` 422 (`apps/api/bootstrap/app.php:325-334`) with static field messages). **Layer B — `StockTransferReceiptService`** (data-dependent; inside the transaction after rule 1, the header lock and all line product advisory locks taken in one sorted call as `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:326-339`; evaluated for every line before any write; failure = typed `TransferReceiptFailureReason` 422, `details = {transfer_line_id, batch_id}`). `is_lot_tracked` is NOT a request field: the service derives it as "the transfer line has ≥ 1 batch allocation" (the grain I3 binds the receipt to; §11 step 1e uses the same definition for the backfill) and stores it on the receipt line.

| # | Rule (layer) | Schema constraint it mirrors (§3.1/§3.2) | Failure |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | `lines` `required\|array\|min:1`; `lines.*.transfer_line_id` `required\|string\|uuid\|distinct` (`validateDistinct`, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Validation/Concerns/ValidatesAttributes.php:881`) | UNIQUE `(receipt_id, transfer_line_id)` | `VALIDATION_ERROR` `lines.{i}.transfer_line_id` |
| A2 | `lines.*.quantity_received`, `lines.*.quantity_damaged`, `lines.*.lots.*.quantity_received`, `lines.*.lots.*.quantity_damaged`: `required\|numeric\|regex:/^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/` (non-negative; ceiling per `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:35`) | CHECK all four ≥ 0 on lines and lots; `decimal(15,4)` scale | `VALIDATION_ERROR` `<field>` |
| A3 | `after()`: per line `bccomp(bcadd(received, damaged, 4), '0', 4) > 0` | CHECK `received + damaged + written_off + returned > 0` (a receipt writes only the first two) | `VALIDATION_ERROR` `lines.{i}.quantity_received` |
| A4 | `after()`: per lot row the same positivity | CHECK `stock_transfer_receipt_line_lots_positive` | `VALIDATION_ERROR` `lines.{i}.lots.{j}.quantity_received` |
| A5 | `lines.*.lots` `nullable\|array`; `lines.*.lots.*.batch_id` `required\|integer\|min:1`; `after()`: `batch_id` unique WITHIN each line (a closure as `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Requests/StockAdjustmentLineRules.php:118-144`, not the `distinct` rule, which scopes to the leading explicit path `lines` and would forbid the same batch on two lines) | UNIQUE `(receipt_line_id, batch_allocation_id)` (allocation ↔ `batch_id` is 1:1 per line, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_06_05_121000_create_stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations_table.php:28`) | `VALIDATION_ERROR` `lines.{i}.lots.{j}.batch_id` |
| A6 | `idempotency_key` `required\|string\|max:128\|not_regex:/^sys:/i` (`validateNotRegex`, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Validation/Concerns/ValidatesAttributes.php:2097`; static message "The idempotency key uses a reserved namespace." — r6-M2: `sys:` is the server namespace of §5.6 (`sys:complete:{transferId}`) and §11 step 1e (`sys:legacy-completion:{transferId}`), so a client can never reserve those keys under the company-wide unique; the same rule is in `CloseStockTransferRequest`); `notes` `nullable\|string\|max:5000`; `lines.*.discrepancy_reason` `nullable\|string\|max:32`; `lines.*.discrepancy_note` `nullable\|string\|max:1000` | column widths; reserved `sys:` namespace (§3.2, I6) | `VALIDATION_ERROR` (`errors['idempotency_key']` for the namespace case) |
| B1 | line id belongs to this transfer (and company) | FK `transfer_line_id` + document identity | `LINE_NOT_ON_TRANSFER` |
| B2 | `bccomp(bcadd(received, damaged, 4), line.remainingQuantity(), 4) <= 0` | CHECK `stock_transfer_lines_received_within_sent` | `OVER_RECEIPT` |
| B3 | line has allocations (`is_lot_tracked = true`) ⇒ `lots` present and non-empty | I3; CHECK `…lot_lines_have_no_parent_movement` (a lot line cannot carry parent movements, so it must carry lot rows) | `LOT_REQUIRED` |
| B4 | line has NO allocations (`is_lot_tracked = false`) ⇒ `lots` absent or empty | same CHECK read the other way (a lot-less line has no lot rows; movement ids live on the line) | `LOT_NOT_ALLOWED` |
| B5 | each lot `batch_id` ∈ the line's shipped allocations | FK `batch_allocation_id` | `UNKNOWN_LOT` |
| B6 | per lot `received + damaged ≤ allocation.remainingQuantity()` | CHECK `stock_transfer_line_batch_allocations_received_within_sent` | `LOT_OVER_RECEIPT` |
| B7 | `Σ lots.received == line.received` AND `Σ lots.damaged == line.damaged` (bccomp at 4) | I3 "lot sums = line grain"; I4 counters | `LOT_SUM_MISMATCH` |
| B8 | `product.requires_batch_tracking` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Domain/Product.php:46,150`) ⇒ line has allocations (initiate guarantees this, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:567-568,754-761`; a flag flipped after initiate is the only way to violate it and would create untracked stock for a tracked product) | `is_lot_tracked` consistent with the product | `LOT_TRACKING_MISMATCH` |
| B9 | `discrepancy_reason` present ⇔ `damaged > 0`; when present, `allowedFor(Receipt)` (`damaged_in_transit \| other`) | `discrepancy_reason` column semantics (§3.3) | `DISCREPANCY_REASON_REQUIRED` (absent with damage) / `DISCREPANCY_REASON_INVALID` (present without damage, or outside the set) |
| B10 | `bccomp(line.remainingQuantity(), '0', 4) > 0` for every submitted line | carrying invariant I5 | `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` (at receipt this is reachable only for a line already fully received; the request-level `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` of rev 5 is subsumed by A3 + `min:1`) |

Layer A never reads the transfer, so its messages cannot carry a sent/remaining figure; Layer B's messages are the static `message()` sentences. Rule 2's `UniqueConstraintViolationException` catch is narrowed accordingly: after the catch, the committed receipt is re-read by `(tenant_id, company_id, idempotency_key)`; if none exists the violation is NOT an idempotency race (every other unique is validated by A1/A5 up front) and is re-thrown as a 500 data bug, never mapped to a 422.
6. **Remainder vs discrepancy (r3-M4).** Omitted lines = open remainder. `discrepancy_reason` required iff `quantity_damaged > 0` (`DISCREPANCY_REASON_REQUIRED`), and must satisfy `allowedFor(Receipt)` = `damaged_in_transit | other` (`DISCREPANCY_REASON_INVALID`); the damage movement reason is always `MovementReason::Damage` whatever the declared reason (§6.1); `has_discrepancy = Σ damaged > 0`.
7. Writes: movements (§6) → counters → receipt rows (`sequence` = count + 1; `is_lot_tracked` = the line has allocations, rule 5 B3/B4/B8) → status (`completed` if Σ remainder = 0 else `partially_received`; on `completed`: `completed_by/at`, then §6.4 capitalisation) → stored events via `persist($event, $receiptId)` (§4.0) → GL buffer flush inside the root transaction (§6.2) → `afterCommit`: plain `StockTransferReceiptPosted`, and `StockTransferCompleted` when terminal.
8. Response 201 `{ data: { receipt: StockTransferReceiptData, transfer: <gated builder output> }, meta: { replayed: false } }`.

### 5.2 `POST /stock-transfers/{id}/close` — `can:inventory.transfers.reconcile` + `can:inventory.transfers.close` + `canAccessLocation(destination)` (r3-B2, r4-M4)

```json
{ "idempotency_key": "client key (MUST NOT start with sys:)", "disposition": "write_off | return_to_source", "reason": "lost_in_transit", "note": "string|null" }
```
Authority (owner-RULED, `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:108`; §12): `close` is a destructive terminal action and requires BOTH permissions, chained as two `can:` middlewares on the route (AND). `inventory.transfers.reconcile` supplies the visibility (`canSeeExpected`, §5.3 — the blind-safety argument of r3-B2 is unchanged: no caller reaches the service without being allowed to see expected quantities); the restored `inventory.transfers.close` supplies the write authority, so a reconciliation-only auditor cannot write off or return stock. A receiver (complete-only) or a reconcile-only user attempting close is refused by the `can` gate with the framework's static 403 body: authorization, not validation, so 403 rather than the "static 422" wording of the orchestrator brief; the body carries no quantity and no transfer field either way, and T9/T18 assert exactly that.
From `in_transit` or `partially_received`; rules 1–3 of §5.1 verbatim and rule A6 including the `sys:` rejection (`CloseStockTransferRequest`, r6-M2); `disposition` required (`DISPOSITION_REQUIRED`); `reason` required for both dispositions and must satisfy `allowedFor(Close)` (all four reasons). One disposition for the whole remainder (mixed = refused by design). Zero remainder → `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE`. `write_off` runs the §5.1 rule-4 preflight (M3). Writes one `kind = close` receipt whose lines carry `quantity_written_off = remainder` or `quantity_returned = remainder` per line/lot, the movements of §6.3 (write-off movement reason always `WriteOff`, §6.1), status `closed_with_writeoff` / `closed_returned`, `closed_*`, §6.4 capitalisation + `freight_uncapitalized`, `StockTransferClosedV1` + line events (`actorRole = closer`), alert (§7). `return_to_source` additionally requires `canAccessLocation(source)`. Response 201 `{ data: { receipt (with close quantities), transfer: <full builder> }, meta: { replayed } }`. Not a mobile action in M-1.

### 5.3 Visibility rule, builders, receipt DTO (r2-M5, r2-M10)

`ExpectedQuantityVisibility` (Inventory application service; constructor-injected — rule 13) implements the Shared contract **`App\Shared\Contracts\TransferIncomingVisibility`** (`canSeeIncomingAggregates(string $userId, string $companyId): bool`, `visibilityVersion(string $companyId): int`), bound in `InventoryServiceProvider::register` beside the existing `LocationStockReader` binding (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Providers/InventoryServiceProvider.php:63`). It reads the setting through a second Shared contract **`App\Shared\Contracts\Compliance\ReceivingControlsReader`** (`blindReceivingEnabled(string $companyId): bool`, `visibilityVersion(string $companyId): int`), implemented in Compliance over `CompanyFraudSettingsRepository::findByCompany` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Infrastructure/Repositories/CompanyFraudSettingsRepository.php:11-14`) and bound in `ComplianceServiceProvider::register` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Providers/ComplianceServiceProvider.php:57` style; the folder `apps/api/app/Shared/Contracts/Compliance/` already exists). Neither Inventory, POS nor Replenishment imports a Compliance model (the POS `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Application/Services/FraudSettingsResolver.php:7-8,14` direct import is prior debt, not a precedent).
- `canSeeExpected(user, transfer)` = setting off OR `inventory.transfers.reconcile` OR `canAccessLocation(source)` (unrestricted memberships return true at `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationContext.php:233-234`).
- `canSeeIncomingAggregates(user, company)` = setting off OR reconcile OR unrestricted (`getAllowedLocationIds` null, `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationContext.php:194-206`). Surfaces 7–9 and 17–18.
Builders (convention 11 — one class per concept, separate array literals):
- `TransferPayloadBuilder` — today's shape (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockTransferController.php:282-341`) + `quantity_received/damaged/written_off/returned/remaining` per line and allocation, `receipts[]`, `close_*`, `freight_uncapitalized`, `blind: false`, `visibility_version`.
- `TransferReceiverPayloadBuilder` — the §5.0b receiver shape. The guard comment lives in this PHP array literal — `// NEVER INCLUDE: notes, transfer_cost, transfer_cost_label, cancellation_reason, initiated_by, quantity, quantity_remaining, quantity_sent, quantity_received, unit_cost_snapshot, allocated_transfer_cost, batch_allocations[].quantity, receipts, close_*, freight_uncapitalized` — exactly as `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/InventoryCountingController.php:376`; the runtime key-scan test T9b enforces it. `my_receipts[]` = the actor's own posted lines — only ever what they typed, since `complete` and `close` are refused to them.
- `StockTransferReceiptData`: `id, receipt_number, kind, disposition|null, sequence, status, is_blind, has_discrepancy, received_by{id,name}, received_at, notes, lines[]{id, transfer_line_id, product_id, variant_id, is_lot_tracked, quantity_received, quantity_damaged, discrepancy_reason, discrepancy_note, lots[]{batch_id, batch_number, quantity_received, quantity_damaged}}`; for `kind = close` the `quantity_written_off/returned` members are present (the closer always has `canSeeExpected`).
- Clients never read `/fraud-settings` to decide the mode (`can:fraud-settings.view`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/routes.php:23-25`); they read `blind` and `visibility_version` from the payload.

### 5.4 `GET /stock-transfers/{id}/reconciliation` — `can:inventory.transfers.reconcile` + `canSeeTransfer` + endpoint access

Not `inventory.view` (granted to cashier/viewer/technician/operator: `apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:702,741,771,806`). Shape (vocabulary of `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingDiscrepancyReportService.php:43-56`): per line and lot `sent, received, damaged, written_off, returned, remaining, variance`, `discrepancy_reasons[]`, `receipts[]{receipt_number, kind (incl. legacy_completion), disposition, sequence, received_by, received_at, is_blind, has_discrepancy}`, `summary{lines, lines_with_discrepancy, total_sent, total_received, total_damaged, total_written_off, total_returned, total_remaining, freight_uncapitalized}`, `visibility_version`; quantities via `QuantityScale::formatForUnit` (`apps/api/app/Shared/Domain/QuantityScale.php:64-75`).

### 5.5 Company setting `blind_receiving`
`FraudSettingsController::update` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:108-123`) gains `'blind_receiving' => 'sometimes|boolean'` under `can:fraud-settings.update` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/routes.php:27-29`); NOT in `CASH_CONTROL_KEYS` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Presentation/Controllers/FraudSettingsController.php:30-35`). Both `update` and `reset` follow the five-step transactional write of §4.5 (insert-or-ignore, row lock, fill/save, atomic `RETURNING` bump, event on flip). All §3.1 surfaces in the same commit. Existing tenants: default `false`, `visibility_version = 1`, no data migration. The response DTO carries the RETURNED `visibility_version`.

### 5.6 `POST /stock-transfers/{id}/complete` — kept, `can:inventory.transfers.complete` (B1)
Order: permission → `canAccessLocation(destination)` → **`canSeeExpected(user, transfer)` else 422 `BLIND_REQUIRES_COUNTED_RECEIPT`** (before the idempotency lookup, so a blind actor can never obtain a stored full receipt through the replay path) → `StockTransferReceiptService::receiveAllRemaining(transfer, user, idempotencyKey: "sys:complete:{transferId}")`: one `kind = receipt` row with every remaining line/lot, zero discrepancy; the key is in the reserved server namespace (r6-M2) — rule A6 refuses any client key starting with `sys:` before the controller, so this key can only ever be written by this path and a receiver cannot pre-empt it (the namespace check is a FormRequest rule, not a service rule: the service accepts the key it is handed); `completeLocked` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:346-383`) deleted. Replay: `complete` twice by an allowed actor → 200 replayed. Response `{ data: <full builder output> }` (a blind actor never reaches it).

### 5.7 Incoming aggregates (surfaces 7–9; r2-M10)
`LocationStockReader::read(...)` and `stockDistributionForProduct(...)` (`apps/api/app/Shared/Contracts/LocationStockReader.php:24-31,45-51`) gain `bool $withTransferIncoming = true`; `LocationIncomingRowDTO::$incomingTransfer` and `StockDistributionRowDTO::$incomingTransfer` / `StockDistributionDTO::$totalIncomingTransfer` become `?string` (`apps/api/app/Shared/DTOs/LocationIncomingRowDTO.php:27`, `apps/api/app/Shared/DTOs/StockDistributionRowDTO.php:31`, `apps/api/app/Shared/DTOs/StockDistributionDTO.php:29`); JSON emits `null` (never `"0.0000"`, which would be a false statement). POS controllers inject `TransferIncomingVisibility` next to their existing Shared dependencies (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/StockDistributionController.php:29-33`, `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosStockLevelController.php:51-54`), pass its answer and emit `meta.visibility_version`. `StockMatrixQueryService::incoming` (Inventory) takes the same flag from `StockMatrixController` directly. The §3.5 remainder conversion is independent of the flag.

### 5.8 Permissions and route gates (D1; r3-B2; r4-M1; r4-M4)
TWO new permissions, defined next to the existing transfer block at `apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:196-200`:
- `inventory.transfers.reconcile` (D1 — visibility): gates reconciliation (§5.4), the "sees expected" disjunct (§5.3), and is one of the two close gates.
- `inventory.transfers.close` (restored, r4-M4 — write authority): required in addition to reconcile for `POST /{id}/close`.
Both granted to `manager` in the seeder's role map (`apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:585-602`, transfer permissions listed at `apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:602`) and to `admin` via `permissionNames()` (`apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:582`); grantable per role/user independently. Route gates after this lane (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:98-117`, edit targets):

| Route | Today | Rev 7 |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /stock-transfers` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:99-101`) | `can:inventory.transfers.view` | `require.any.permission:inventory.transfers.view,inventory.transfers.complete,inventory.transfers.reconcile` (r7-M3) |
| `GET /stock-transfers/{transfer}` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:107-109`) | `can:inventory.transfers.view` | same three-permission any-of |
| `GET /stock-transfers/{transfer}/receiver-view` | new | same three-permission any-of |
| `POST /stock-transfers/{transfer}/receive` | new | `can:inventory.transfers.complete` |
| `POST /stock-transfers/{transfer}/complete` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:111-113`) | `can:inventory.transfers.complete` | unchanged |
| `POST /stock-transfers/{transfer}/close` | new | `can:inventory.transfers.reconcile` + `can:inventory.transfers.close` |
| `GET /stock-transfers/{transfer}/reconciliation` | new | `can:inventory.transfers.reconcile` |
| `POST /stock-transfers`, `POST /{transfer}/cancel` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:103-105,115-117`) | create / cancel | unchanged |

Narrowing of the r4-M1 wording, with proof: `receive` is NOT put under the any-of gate. `inventory.transfers.view` is a read permission held by roles that never receive (e.g. the reconciliation-only auditor of r4-M4); an any-of gate on a write route would let a view-only actor post receipts and land stock — the same privilege collapse r4-M4 rejects for close. The complete-only receiver's problem was reachability of the READ routes (list/show), which the any-of gate solves; that receiver already holds `complete`. `require.any.permission` is the existing pattern (`apps/api/app/Modules/Company/routes.php:98-100`), so no new middleware or policy method is introduced.

**Third disjunct `inventory.transfers.reconcile` on the READ routes only (r7-M3).** `inventory.transfers.reconcile` is grantable independently of `inventory.transfers.view` (this section, above), and every canonical surface it authorises hangs off the two read routes: the reconciliation tab is nested inside the transfer detail page (§8), the Close action renders on that same page (§8), and `TransferDiscrepancyNotification` deep-links to `/inventory/stock-transfers/{id}` (§7), which the web bell navigates to directly (`apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:94-98`, inside the handler at `apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:85-98`). Without the third disjunct a reconcile-only (or reconcile+close) actor could call `GET /{id}/reconciliation` but could not open the page that renders it, nor the notification that points at it. Adding it is READ-only — no write route gains a disjunct (`receive` stays on `complete`, `close` stays on reconcile AND close) — and is blind-safe by construction: `canSeeExpected()` is true for any reconcile holder (§5.3), so the actor the disjunct admits is served the FULL builder, exactly as it would be if it also held `inventory.transfers.view`. Deploy: `tenants:seed RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`, `permission:cache-reset`, `php artisan permissions:export-frontend-map` (`apps/api/app/Console/Commands/ExportFrontendPermissionsMap.php:12-17`; drift guard `scripts/preflight.sh:136-149`).

### 5.9 Replenishment feeds (surfaces 17–18; r3-B1)
`ReplenishmentRequestResource` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Resources/ReplenishmentRequestResource.php:12-44`) gains a constructor flag `bool $withTransferQuantities = true` and a static `collectionFor(iterable $rows, bool $withTransferQuantities, Request $request): array` that resolves each row through `new self($row, $withTransferQuantities)`; the three collection call sites move to it (`apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Controllers/ReplenishmentRequestController.php:73,83`, `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosReplenishmentController.php:107`; the POS `store` echo at `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosReplenishmentController.php:83` is the actor's own pending request and has no `fulfillment_type`, so the rule below never masks it). Both controllers constructor-inject `TransferIncomingVisibility` (beside `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Controllers/ReplenishmentRequestController.php:29-34` and `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosReplenishmentController.php:25-28`) and compute `canSeeIncomingAggregates(userId, companyId)` once per request. Masking rule: when the flag is false and `fulfillment_type === ReplenishmentFulfillmentType::Transfer`, `requested_qty`, `suggested_qty` AND `note` are emitted as `null` (r6-M1: the note is arbitrary requester text — `nullable|string|max:2000`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Requests/CaptureReplenishmentRequest.php:38`; persisted verbatim, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Controllers/ReplenishmentRequestController.php:117-125`; emitted verbatim, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Resources/ReplenishmentRequestResource.php:32` — while the fulfilment copies the selected quantity into the transfer line, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Application/Services/ReplenishmentFulfillmentService.php:64-74`, and settlement links the row to that transfer, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Application/Listeners/SettleRequestsOnTransferInitiated.php:64-70`, so a note such as "send 7391.4517" restates exactly the figure the two structured fields mask; nulling it is the same rule as omitting sender `notes` from the receiver view); `fulfillment_id` (identity, not a quantity) and `request_count` (a count of requests) stay; rows not linked to a transfer keep their note. `note` is already a nullable wire value (the capture rule above), so neither client's types change. The rule applies to every transfer-fulfilled row regardless of transfer state — a superset of the orchestrator's `in_transit`/`partially_received` set, chosen so Replenishment needs no cross-module status read; terminal-transfer rows are R2 territory and pending rows never carry a transfer. Envelope: `meta.transfer_quantities_masked: bool`, `meta.notes_masked: bool` (always equal to it — both are the one flag `!canSeeIncomingAggregates`; the second key states the note masking explicitly, r6-M1) and `meta.visibility_version` (web, inside the existing `meta` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Controllers/ReplenishmentRequestController.php:74-75,84-91`; POS, beside `as_of`/`truncated` at `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosReplenishmentController.php:106-110`). `REALIGNMENT-LOG.md` entry (§11 step 5).

### 5.10 Movement feeds — transfer-linked quantity masking (surfaces 10–11; r8-B1)

**The defect this closes.** Rev 8 asserted that destination `TransferIn` rows "exist only after the actor's own receipt" and that these feeds expose "only the actor's own posted quantities". The code disproves it. `StockMovementController::index` filters on tenant and company (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:71-73`) and on the resolved location set (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:80`, resolver `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationScopeResolver.php:31-66`) and applies NO actor predicate; `formatMovement` emits `quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:218`), `quantity_before` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:220`), `quantity_after` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:221`), `reference_type` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:228`), `reference_id` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:232`) and the posting user (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:244`). `EntryExitNoteController::index` groups by `reference_type`/`reference_id`/`location_id`/direction/source type (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:44-72`) and `formatNote` emits every movement of the group (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:216-254`) with `quantity` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:247`), `quantity_before` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:249`) and `quantity_after` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:250`), so one note can combine two receivers' postings against the same transfer at the same location. Concretely: receiver A posts part of a line, receiver B posts its remainder while another line keeps the transfer `partially_received`, and A reads B's TransferIn through either feed; A then submits a positive amount for the now-full line and gets `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` (§5.1 rule B10), establishing zero remainder, so the sum of transfer-linked movements IS the sent quantity while the transfer is still receivable. **Filtering by `user_id` is not sufficient**, because `quantity_before` and `quantity_after` on the actor's OWN row encode the destination stock another receiver already landed.

**Predicate (who is masked).** The same company-scoped disjunction surfaces 7–9 and 17–18 use: `TransferIncomingVisibility::canSeeIncomingAggregates(string $userId, string $companyId): bool` (§5.3 — setting off OR `inventory.transfers.reconcile` OR an unrestricted location membership, `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationContext.php:194-206`). An actor for whom it returns **false** is masked. The per-transfer `canSeeExpected(user, transfer)` is deliberately NOT used here: both feeds are cross-transfer ledgers whose page can carry rows of many transfers, and evaluating a per-transfer predicate per row would make the response shape depend on a join the controllers do not have; `canSeeIncomingAggregates` is the strictly-safe superset (it is false only for actors `canSeeExpected` is also false for on every transfer they can reach, since reconcile and unrestricted membership are disjuncts of both). Both controllers constructor-inject `TransferIncomingVisibility` beside their existing dependencies (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:49-52`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:22-24`; rule 13, never `app()`), and compute the flag ONCE per request.

**Carrying set (which rows are masked).** A movement row is *transfer-linked and carrying* when either:
1. `reference_type = StockTransfer::class` (the value `markMovementAsTransfer` writes, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:745-752`) and `reference_id` is the id of a `stock_transfers` row whose `status` is in `StockTransfer::CARRYING_STATUSES` (§3.5) — the `TransferOut` at source and every `TransferIn` at destination, including the return-to-source restock of §6.1; or
2. `reference_type = StockMovementReferenceType::StockTransferReceipt->value` (`'stock_transfer_receipt'`, §3.3) and `reference_id` is a `stock_transfer_receipts` row whose `transfer_id` points at such a transfer — the receipt-linked destructive movements of §6.1 (the `Damage` issue at receipt and the `WriteOff` issue at close). A close makes the transfer terminal in the same transaction, so in practice only the receipt-linked `Damage` rows are ever masked through this second clause; it is specified anyway so no destructive receipt movement can be read against a still-carrying transfer.

Resolution is ONE extra query per page for each clause (one `SELECT id FROM stock_transfers WHERE id IN (:transfer_reference_ids_on_this_page) AND status IN (:carrying_statuses)`, and one `SELECT id, transfer_id FROM stock_transfer_receipts WHERE id IN (:receipt_reference_ids_on_this_page)` whose `transfer_id` values are matched against that same status set), the same one-query-per-source-kind-per-page shape the movements controller already uses for documents and countings (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:127-165`). Every other row — PO receipts, countings, adjustments, POS scrap, and every movement of a `completed`, `closed_with_writeoff`, `closed_returned` or `cancelled` transfer — is untouched for every actor: that is the accepted R2 residual, and it is what "keep legitimate terminal history intact" means here.

**Masked keys (exactly three, per feed).** For a masked actor, on a transfer-linked carrying row only:
- `GET /stock-movements` — `quantity`, `quantity_before`, `quantity_after` are emitted as JSON `null` (never `"0.0000"`, which would be a false statement, exactly as §5.7 argues for `incoming_transfer`). `quantity_decimals`, `movement_type`, `reason`, `reference`, `reference_type`, `reference_id`, `source_document_*`, `notes`, `user_id`, `user_name`, `reverses_movement_id`, `is_reversed`, `created_at`, product and location identity are unchanged: identity and provenance are not quantities, and the R2/R1 residuals already accept them.
- `GET /entry-exit-notes` — the same three keys inside `lines[]` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:247,249,250`) are `null`; `lines[].movement_id`, `lines[].product`, `lines[].quantity_decimals`, `lines[].movement_type`, `lines[].reason` and the note header (`id`, `direction`, `source_type`, `source_id`, `source_label`, `location`, `actor`, `timestamp`) are unchanged.
The actor's own posted quantities are NOT lost to them: `my_receipts[]` on `GET /stock-transfers/{id}/receiver-view` (§5.0b) and the `receipt` echo of the 201 receive response (§5.1 rule 8) return exactly what that actor typed, and neither can carry another receiver's figure.

**`meta` flag.** Both envelopes gain `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked: bool` — always the value `! canSeeIncomingAggregates(...)`, emitted on every page whether or not the page happens to contain a masked row, so a client can label the column rather than infer masking from a `null`. On `GET /stock-movements` it joins the existing pagination `meta` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:167-179`); on `GET /entry-exit-notes` it joins the existing `$meta` array (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:111-125`). **`visibility_version` is NOT carried on these two feeds**: they are not in the §4.5 carriage set, and adding it would widen the advisory-hint contract beyond the gate minimum. T9 rows 6m/6n therefore keep "not asserted" in their version column. **Corrected client-cache statement (r9-m1).** Rev 9 wrote that these feeds "hold no client-side cache to invalidate". That is FALSE for `apps/web`: all three consumers are TanStack queries with no per-query overrides — `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:144-171` (key `locationScopedKey(['stock-movements', …], scope)` at `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:145`), `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:85-101` (key `locationScopedKey(['product-movements', …], scope)` at `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:86`) and `apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:73-85` (key `tenantScopedKey(['entry-exit-notes', direction, 'all'])` at `apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:73`) — so today they inherit `staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5` and `refetchOnWindowFocus: false` from the client defaults (`apps/web/src/lib/queryClient.ts:3-9`). It IS true for `apps/pos` and for mobile, neither of which consumes either feed (§8b, §9). **The choice, stated (r9-m1):** these three queries take the ZERO-STALENESS option — `staleTime: 0, refetchOnWindowFocus: true`, the same behaviour §8 already declares for every blind-relevant hook — and their prefixes are deliberately NOT added to the `dropVisibilityCaches` removal set of §8, because these feeds carry no `visibility_version` (previous sentence) and a removal keyed on a version the response never returns would have nothing to compare against. Convergence is therefore best-effort under R4, exactly like the other blind-relevant surfaces: the first fetch after the flip re-renders the page masked.

**Scope note.** No permission, route, gate or write path changes. `can:inventory.view` still gates both feeds (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:78-80,82-84`); the location-scope fix on entry/exit notes (§5.0 row 11 fix (a), T17) is the only other change to them in this lane.

## 6. Discrepancy → movements + GL

### 6.1 Movements (all via `StockAdjustmentService`: `receive` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:115-129`, `issue` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:253-267`; inside the receipt transaction, under the up-front product locks; r3-M4: the movement reason is a function of the ACTION column, never of the declared discrepancy reason)

| Action / quantity | Movement(s) | Movement reason / GL |
|---|---|---|
| receipt — received (good) | destination `receive(qty, reference = transfer_number, batchId)` + `markMovementAsTransfer(TransferIn)` — as `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:358-383` | `TransferIn`: no GL (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/MovementReason.php:86-92,111`); WAC untouched |
| receipt — damaged (`discrepancy_reason ∈ {damaged_in_transit, other}`) | same receive + mark, then destination `issue(qty, reference = receipt_number, batchId, reason: MovementReason::Damage, unitCost: Product::resolveMovementUnitCost() (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Domain/Product.php:315-325`), referenceType: StockTransferReceipt, referenceId: receipt id)` | **always `Damage`** (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/MovementReason.php:26,103`): GL, Shrinkage → Dr Shrinkage / Cr Inventory; the declared reason is stored on the receipt line and event only |
| close `write_off` (any of the four reasons) | receive + mark, then `issue(... reason: MovementReason::WriteOff ...)` | **always `WriteOff`** (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/MovementReason.php:28,105`): GL, Shrinkage |
| close `return_to_source` | **source** `receive(qty, reference = transfer_number.'-RETURN', batchId)` + mark `TransferIn` — the cancel restock loop `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:452-481` extracted into `restockAtSource(line, qty, lots)` shared by cancel and return | `TransferIn`: no GL (benchmark note `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-transfer-discrepancy-gl.md:7-13`) |

Movement identity (r3-M3): lot-less line → one movement per kind with quantity > 0, id stored on the receipt line; lot-tracked line → one movement per lot row per kind with quantity > 0 (each `receive`/`issue` call carries that lot's `batchId`), ids stored on the lot rows, parent columns NULL (CHECK, §3.2).

Land-then-scrap rationale: the remainder is derived, not a `stock_levels` row; the TransferIn gives the destination row the units the scrap removes (net on-hand 0, one destructive movement, one GL leg — I2).

### 6.2 GL bridge (M3 r1/r2)
The receipt service is a composite root of the D-28 buffer: `InventoryGlPostingBuffer::mark()` at entry, `enqueue(MovementGlContext)` per destructive movement, `flushIfOutermost()` inside the root transaction after the body. Buffer behaviour: at `DB::transactionLevel() > 1` it registers a leak alarm and returns `[]` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingBuffer.php:56-64` — `flushIfOutermost` declared at 56, the nested-level branch at 60-64); it throws only outside any transaction (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingBuffer.php:66-67`); so the receipt service MUST be the outermost transaction (T13 asserts no leak alarm). `rollbackTo(marker)` on earlier failure (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingBuffer.php:39-45`). Direct `postFor*` calls fail PHPStan (`apps/api/app/PHPStan/Rules/InventoryGlPostingViaBufferOnly.php:17-42`).
- **Lot-less line**: `MovementGlKind::Exit` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/MovementGlKind.php:9`) → `postForExit` → `postMovement` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:26-28,130-207`): `requirePerpetual` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:158`), `hasInventoryMovementAccounts` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:161`), counter `InventoryShrinkageExpense` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:185-187`). Context as `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Application/Services/ReturnScrapWriteOffService.php:169-189` minus `batchNumber/productId`.
- **Lot line**: `MovementGlKind::BatchWriteOff` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Enums/MovementGlKind.php:12`) with the real `batchNumber` and `productId`, one context per lot movement (`postForBatchWriteOff` throws without them, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:95-96`; accounts at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:99`). This path performs no valuation-mode check (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryGlPostingService.php:89-127`), which is why §5.1 rule 4 preflights `requirePerpetual` at the receipt root for BOTH paths; `postForBatchWriteOff` is not modified.
- Non-positive resolved unit cost → warn and post nothing (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Application/Services/ReturnScrapWriteOffService.php:135-141`).
- `BatchWriteOffService::writeOff` (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/BatchWriteOffService.php:44-57`) is NOT used.

### 6.3 Return-to-source — no GL (OQ-1)
Stock movement pair only; company-owned unchanged (I2). §6.4 runs on the terminal transition.

### 6.4 Freight — CONFIRMED default (owner, `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:106`; benchmark `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-owner-decisions-po-revert-freight-attribution.md`; algorithm preserved verbatim from rev 3/4)
**Today.** `capitalizeTransferCost` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:631-707`) weights on shipped `quantity` (`computeAllocationWeights`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:714-728`), allocates 100 % of `transfer_cost` with the last line absorbing the 4-dp residual (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:671-707`), and capitalises each share via `recordCostAdjustment` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:745-831`), which writes a quantity-0 `Adjustment` movement (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:812-830`) and **no journal**, and no-ops when company-owned quantity is 0 (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/WeightedAverageCostService.php:771-775`). Transfer freight has no GL leg in this module.

**Confirmed algorithm.** At the terminal transition (`completed` or `closed_*`), once per transfer:
1. `goodTotal = Σ lines.quantity_received`, `sentTotal = Σ lines.quantity` (4-dp strings).
2. Working scale `w = max(ALLOCATION_SCALE, s + 4)` where `ALLOCATION_SCALE = 6` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:65-70`) and `s = CurrencyScaleResolverInterface::getScaleSafe($company->currency_code, 3)` (rule 19; the terminal transition may run without `CompanyContext`).
3. `pool = CurrencyScale::bcformat(bcmul(transfer_cost, bcdiv(goodTotal, sentTotal, w), w), 4)` — one rounding, at the stored scale of `transfer_cost` (truncation, `docs/architecture/precision-contract.md:25-26`).
4. `freight_uncapitalized = bcsub(transfer_cost, pool, 4)` → `stock_transfers.freight_uncapitalized` (`decimal(15,4)`; exact by construction; I7).
5. The existing allocation loop runs on `pool` instead of `transfer_cost`, with **landed weights**: `ProRataQuantity` → `quantity_received`; `ProRataValue` → `quantity_received × unit_cost_snapshot`; `EqualPerLine` → `1` for lines with `quantity_received > 0`, else `0`. Zero-weight lines are skipped (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:661-663`); the last cost-bearing line absorbs the residual so Σ `allocated_transfer_cost = pool` exactly (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:671-707`). `recordCostAdjustment` per line as today.
6. Damaged, written-off and returned units are all outside `goodTotal`, so their freight share is the residual: never capitalised, no journal, surfaced in reconciliation and carried on the header events.

**Worked example** (`transfer_cost = 120.0000`, `pro_rata_quantity`, TND `s = 3`, `w = 7`). Lines: A 8 sent, B 4 sent. Receipt: A 5 good, B 4 good; close `write_off` of A's remaining 3. `goodTotal = 9`, `sentTotal = 12`; `bcdiv('9','12',7) = 0.7500000`; `pool = bcformat(90.0000000, 4) = 90.0000`; `freight_uncapitalized = 120.0000 − 90.0000 = 30.0000`. Allocation on landed weights 5 and 4 (total 9): A `bcmul('90.0000', bcdiv('5','9',7), 7) = 49.9999995` → 4 dp `49.9999`; B (last) = `90.0000 − 49.9999 = 40.0001`. Persisted: `A.allocated_transfer_cost = 49.9999`, `B = 40.0001`, `freight_uncapitalized = 30.0000`; `49.9999 + 40.0001 + 30.0000 = 120.0000` (I7). Single-line T4 (12 sent, 5 good, 7 written off): `pool = 50.0000`, `freight_uncapitalized = 70.0000` = 7/12 of 120. T3 (11 good + 1 damaged of 12): `pool = 110.0000`, residual `10.0000`.

Rejected alternatives (recorded, not open): (a) "expense with shrinkage" — Dr `InventoryShrinkageExpense` / Cr a transfer-freight clearing account; would need a new `SystemAccountPurpose`, provisioning in `InventoryVarianceAccountProvisioner` and every seeded country chart, and would double-hit P&L unless the clearing account is reconciled against the freight invoice; (b) mode-consistent pool ratio — identical to the default under `pro_rata_quantity`.

Precision (rule 19): quantities numeric strings; `bccomp/bcadd/bcsub` at `QTY_SCALE = 4`; no float; `ForbidFloatCastOnDecimalProperty` guards models.

## 7. Notifications (T-4 minimum; r3-M5; r4-M5)

Both extend `Illuminate\Notifications\Notification implements ShouldQueue` (precedent `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Notifications/UserInvitation.php:8-16`; tenancy on the worker via `QueueTenancyBootstrapper`, `apps/api/config/tenancy.php:38-43`), `databaseType()` + `toDatabase()` as `apps/api/app/Modules/Treasury/Application/Notifications/TreasuryAlertNotification.php:22-40`, `afterCommit = true`, default queue (`apps/api/config/horizon.php:201-217`; `apps/api/tests/Unit/Config/HorizonQueueCoverageTest.php`). Recipients resolved in the listener, in the request, inside `DB::afterCommit` — never in the worker (rule 20).
- **Identity.** `$this->id = Uuid::uuid5(NS_TRANSFER_NOTIFICATIONS, "{type}:{receipt_or_transfer_id}:{user_id}")`; Laravel keeps a preset id (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/NotificationSender.php:150-154,220-228`); `notifications.id` is the uuid PK (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_12_110000_create_notifications_table.php:13-20`).
- **Idempotent delivery (inside the queued job).** The job runs `ChannelManager::sendNow` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/SendQueuedNotifications.php:116-118`), which calls `shouldSendNotification` → the notification's own `shouldSend($notifiable, $channel)` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/NotificationSender.php:156-158,195-200`). Both notifications implement `shouldSend()` = `! DatabaseNotification::query()->whereKey($this->id)->exists()` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/DatabaseNotification.php:9,26`, non-incrementing uuid key): a replayed job finds the row, skips, and completes. Because the stock `DatabaseChannel::send` is a relation `create()` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/Channels/DatabaseChannel.php:17-21`) that would throw on a concurrent duplicate, `via()` returns `[IdempotentDatabaseChannel::class]` — a project channel (`App\Shared\Infrastructure\Notifications\IdempotentDatabaseChannel extends DatabaseChannel`) whose `send()` inserts through `insertOrIgnore` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Query/Builder.php:4132`; PG `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Query/Grammars/PostgresGrammar.php:371`). **Columns (r4-M5) — every column the stock path writes, plus the timestamps Eloquent would add:** `id, type, data` and `read_at` from `buildPayload()` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/Channels/DatabaseChannel.php:31-43`; `data` json-encoded because the query builder bypasses the model's `data => array` cast at `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/DatabaseNotification.php:47-48`); `notifiable_type = $notifiable->getMorphClass()`, `notifiable_id = $notifiable->getKey()` (what the `uuidMorphs('notifiable')` relation would set, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_12_110000_create_notifications_table.php:16`); **`created_at` and `updated_at` = `(new DatabaseNotification)->freshTimestampString()`** (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Eloquent/Concerns/HasTimestamps.php:117-129` — `now()` formatted with the model's date format, i.e. the same value and precision Eloquent writes for the `timestampsTz()` columns, which are nullable by construction, `apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/Schema/Blueprint.php:1300-1304`). A nullable `created_at` would reach the API as `created_at: null` (ordering `orderByDesc('created_at')` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Notification/Presentation/Controllers/NotificationController.php:20`; the `created_at` member emitted at `apps/api/app/Modules/Notification/Presentation/Controllers/NotificationController.php:35` — line 34 emits `read_at`; r5 citation audit) and break the web contract (`created_at: string`, `apps/web/src/features/notifications/api/notificationsApi.ts:4-10`; unconditional `localeCompare` at `apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:81-83`) — T10 asserts the contract. The PK is the last line of defence; it is never allowed to fail the job. `databaseType()` is honoured by `buildPayload()` (`apps/api/vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Notifications/Channels/DatabaseChannel.php:35-37`), so the bell's `type` switch is unchanged.
- `TransferInitiatedNotification` — `inventory.transfer.initiated`; data `{transfer_id, transfer_number, source_location_name, destination_location_id, destination_location_name, line_count, initiated_by_name, blind, deep_link}` (no quantities, no notes); `deep_link = /inventory/stock-transfers/{id}/receive`. Recipients: destination-accessible memberships (`apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationContext.php:194-206`) with `inventory.transfers.complete`, minus the initiator. Listener on plain `StockTransferInitiated`.
- `TransferDiscrepancyNotification` — `inventory.transfer.received_with_discrepancy` (on `StockTransferReceiptPosted` with `kind = receipt` and `hasDiscrepancy`) and `inventory.transfer.closed` (`kind = close`); data `{transfer_id, transfer_number, receipt_id, receipt_number, kind, disposition, destination_location_name, lines_with_discrepancy, received_by_name, deep_link}`; `deep_link = /inventory/stock-transfers/{id}`. Recipients: `inventory.transfers.reconcile` holders with destination access (they can act on it — reconcile; close if they also hold `inventory.transfers.close`). **Reachability (r7-M3):** the deep link resolves for a reconcile-only recipient because `inventory.transfers.reconcile` is a disjunct of the backend list/show any-of gate (§5.8) and of the web detail route guard (§8); the bell navigates straight to it (`apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:94-98`), so a notification is never sent to an actor who would land on a 403. A plain partial receipt notifies nobody.
- Web: `apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx` switch (`apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:45-71`) + `KNOWN_TYPES` (`apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:20-26`) gain the three types; navigation by `deep_link` (`apps/web/src/features/notifications/components/NotificationPanel.tsx:94-98`); `notifications` namespace en/fr/ar.

## 8. Web (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/`)

- Types (r5-m1 — shadow entity types only). `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts` (109 lines today) is split, not replaced. **Replaced by generated DTOs**: the backend-owned entity projections `StockTransferStatus`, `StockTransferType`, `TransferCostDistribution`, `StockTransferLine`, `StockTransferLineBatchAllocation`, `StockTransfer` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts:12-72`, plus the "hand-written on purpose" header comment at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts:1-10`) → `StockTransferData`, `StockTransferLineData`, `StockTransferLineBatchAllocationData`, `TransferStatus`/`TransferType`/`TransferCostDistribution` enums, `StockTransferReceiptData`, `StockTransferReceiptLineData`, `StockTransferReceiptLineLotData`, `TransferReceiverViewData`, `TransferReconciliationData`, `CompanyFraudSettingsData.blind_receiving/visibility_version` via `php artisan typescript:transform` (convention 11 rule 4, `docs/conventions/11-ONE-SURFACE-PER-CONCEPT.md:44-47`). **Stay in the file (request/query transport types with no generated counterpart)**: `CreateStockTransferLineInput`, `CreateStockTransferLineBatchAllocationInput`, `CreateStockTransferInput` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts:74-95`; consumed by `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/CreateStockTransferPage.tsx` and `useCreateStockTransfer` at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:31-41`), `StockTransferListFilters` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts:97-103`, `useStockTransferList` at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:11-16`), `StockTransferListResponse` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts:105-108`; today `data: StockTransfer[]` at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/types/index.ts:106`), re-pointed at the generated entity types (`status?: TransferStatus | 'all'`, and — r7-M2 — **`data: (StockTransferData | TransferReceiverViewData)[]`**); **added beside them**: `ReceiveStockTransferInput` (the §5.1 body; quantities as strings), `ReceiveStockTransferLineInput`, `ReceiveStockTransferLotInput`, `CloseStockTransferInput` (§5.2 body), **`StockTransferReceiveResponse = { receipt: StockTransferReceiptData; transfer: StockTransferData | TransferReceiverViewData }`** — the `data` member ONLY — with the raw envelope beside it, **`StockTransferReceiveEnvelope = { data: StockTransferReceiveResponse; meta: { replayed: boolean } }`** and **`StockTransferCloseEnvelope = { data: StockTransferCloseResponse; meta: { replayed: boolean } }`** where `StockTransferCloseResponse = { receipt: StockTransferReceiptData; transfer: StockTransferData }` (r8-m2 — the close response always carries the full builder, §5.2), **`StockTransferShowResponse = StockTransferData | TransferReceiverViewData`** (r7-M2), `StockTransferListResponse.meta.visibility_version`. **Envelope choice, stated (r8-m2).** `apiPost` returns `response.data.data` and discards the top-level `meta` (`apps/web/src/lib/api.ts:422-425`), so a type that puts `meta.replayed` on the unwrapped shape is a false contract. Receive and close therefore use the RAW `api.post` envelope type, not `apiPost`, **because the caller consumes `meta.replayed`**: the receive page renders an idempotent replay (200 with `meta.replayed === true`) as success rather than as a duplicate post (§8 detail bullet, §5.1 rule 1), and the close dialog does the same on a replayed close. Every other transfer mutation keeps `apiPost` — `complete` and `cancel` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/stockTransferApi.ts:45-46,49-50`) have no `meta` member to lose. No claim is made that a builder comment reaches the generated type (r3-m1); the guard is the backend key-scan test T9b.
- **List and show are unions, not the full DTO (r7-M2).** §5.0 row 2 has the server pick `TransferPayloadBuilder` or `TransferReceiverPayloadBuilder` PER TRANSFER on both read routes, so a web type of `StockTransferData` alone is a false contract for the very actor the r4-M1 any-of gate admits. The two response types above are therefore discriminated unions over the server's builder choice, and `blind` is the discriminant: `TransferPayloadBuilder` emits `blind: false` and `TransferReceiverPayloadBuilder` emits `blind: true` (§5.3), both always present. One narrowing helper lives beside the types — `export const isReceiverTransfer = (t: StockTransferData | TransferReceiverViewData): t is TransferReceiverViewData => t.blind === true` — and is the only place the discriminant is read. Shape differences the narrowing must cover, from §5.0b: the receiver shape carries nested `source_location{id,name}` / `destination_location{id,name}` and `initiated_at` where the full shape carries flat `source_location_name` / `destination_location_name` / `created_at`, and it carries no `transfer_cost`, `transfer_cost_label`, `transfer_cost_distribution`, `initiated_by_name`, `completed_by_name`, `cancelled_by_name`, `cancellation_reason` or `notes` member at all. Because the union members differ structurally, `pnpm typecheck` fails on every un-narrowed read of a full-only member — that compiler failure is the mechanism, not a convention.
- **Route guards (r3-M1; API side r4-M1 in §5.8).** `RequirePermission` accepts `permissions?: Permission[]` with any-of semantics by default (`apps/web/src/features/auth/components/RequirePermission.tsx:7-11,40,60-62`). Edit targets in `apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx`: list route (`apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1402-1411`, today `permission="inventory.transfers.view"` at `apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1405`) and detail route (`apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1422-1430`, today `apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1425`) become `permissions={['inventory.transfers.view', 'inventory.transfers.complete', 'inventory.transfers.reconcile']}` — the same three-permission any-of set as the backend read gate (§5.8), with `inventory.transfers.reconcile` added by r7-M3 so that a reconcile-only holder can open the detail page that hosts the reconciliation tab and the Close action, and the page the discrepancy notification deep-links to; new route `stock-transfers/:id/receive` → `ReceiveTransferPage` with `permission="inventory.transfers.complete"`; all three plus `stock-transfers/new` (`apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1412-1420`) get `moduleKey="inventory"` as the inventory hub routes (`apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1086,1096`). The sidebar item stays on `inventory.transfers.view` (`apps/web/src/components/organisms/Sidebar/Sidebar.tsx:239`; its `permission` prop is single-valued, `apps/web/src/components/organisms/Sidebar/Sidebar.tsx:110,120`) — a complete-only receiver, and equally a reconcile-only supervisor (r7-M3), enters through the bell deep link (§7) or the list URL; a sidebar any-of prop is out of scope (§1). Reconciliation tab keeps `inventory.transfers.reconcile`; the Close action renders only with BOTH `inventory.transfers.reconcile` and `inventory.transfers.close` (r4-M4).
- API (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/stockTransferApi.ts:37-50`, `apiPost` unwraps once — rule 14): `receive`, `close`, `receiverView`, `reconciliation`; **`receive` and `close` are RAW-envelope calls (r8-m2)** — `receive: async (id: string, body: ReceiveStockTransferInput): Promise<StockTransferReceiveEnvelope> => (await api.post<StockTransferReceiveEnvelope>(\`/stock-transfers/${id}/receive\`, body)).data` and `close` the same with `StockTransferCloseEnvelope`, because `apiPost` returns `response.data.data` and would drop `meta.replayed` (`apps/web/src/lib/api.ts:422-425`); this is the pattern `list` already uses to keep `meta` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/stockTransferApi.ts:31-33`). `receiverView` and `reconciliation` stay on `apiGet` (no envelope member is consumed). **`show` is re-typed to the union (r7-M2)** — `show: async (id: string): Promise<StockTransferShowResponse> => apiGet<StockTransferShowResponse>(...)` (edit target; today `show: async (id: string): Promise<StockTransfer>` returning `apiGet<StockTransfer>` at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/stockTransferApi.ts:37-38`), and `list` keeps `api.get<StockTransferListResponse>` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/stockTransferApi.ts:31-33`, the paginated pattern that preserves `meta`) with the union now inside `StockTransferListResponse.data`; `complete` and `cancel` keep the full type (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/stockTransferApi.ts:45-46,49-50`) because a blind actor never reaches either (§5.6, §3.4); hooks with `tenantScopedKey` keys (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:11-20`, edit targets); invalidation of transfer, list, `stock-levels`, `stock-movements` (pattern at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:43-51`) and notifications keys.
- **Cache hygiene / visibility version — best-effort (r4-B1).** Transfer detail/list, receiver-view, stock-matrix (with incoming) and replenishment hooks set `staleTime: 0, refetchOnWindowFocus: true` (overriding the client defaults `staleTime: 5 min` at `apps/web/src/lib/queryClient.ts:6` and `refetchOnWindowFocus: false` at `apps/web/src/lib/queryClient.ts:8`). **The three movement-feed queries join that list by r9-m1** — `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:144-171`, `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:85-101` and `apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:73-85` — but NOT the removal set below, because neither endpoint carries `visibility_version` (§5.10); the movement-feeds bullet of this section states that choice in full. The R5 stock-position surfaces (§5.0 rows 19–24) are not blind-relevant at all: they set nothing and are not dropped. **Real cache keys (r5-M3).** `tenantScopedKey` appends tenant and company as SUFFIXES (`apps/web/src/lib/tenantScopedKey.ts:29-35`), and React Query matches a filter key positionally from the front (`partialMatchKey`, `node_modules/.pnpm/@tanstack+query-core@5.90.11/node_modules/@tanstack/query-core/build/modern/utils.js:94-104` — declared at 94, the recursive front-position match at 101-103; `removeQueries` at `node_modules/.pnpm/@tanstack+query-core@5.90.11/node_modules/@tanstack/query-core/build/modern/queryClient.js:119`), so a filter of `tenantScopedKey(['stock-transfers'])` = `['stock-transfers', tenant, company]` never matches a leaf — the repo documents exactly this at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/__tests__/queries.test.tsx:40-46` and `apps/web/src/lib/locationScopedKey.ts:18-22`. The leaves this feature and its blind-relevant neighbours register are: `['stock-transfers', 'list', filters, tenant, company]` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:13`), `['stock-transfers', 'detail', id, tenant, company]` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:20`), new `['stock-transfers', 'receiver-view', id, tenant, company]` and `['stock-transfers', 'reconciliation', id, tenant, company]` (same `namespace` constant, `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:9`); `['inventory-stock-matrix', search, page, perPage, { locScope }, tenant, company]` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/pages/StockByLocationPage.tsx:22` via `locationScopedKey`, `apps/web/src/lib/locationScopedKey.ts:24-30`; today that page requests `includeIncoming: false`, `apps/web/src/features/inventory/pages/StockByLocationPage.tsx:22`, so the web matrix cache carries no transfer incoming until a later lane turns it on — the prefix is dropped regardless) and `['inventory', 'stock-matrix', 'rebalance', { locScope }, tenant, company]` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/hooks/useRebalanceSuggestions.ts:15`); `['replenishment', 'open', filters, tenant, company]` and `['replenishment', 'history', filters, tenant, company]` (`apps/web/src/features/replenishment/api/queries.ts:10-11`). Notifications (`['notifications', userId, 'unread-count' | 'list', tenant, company]`, `apps/web/src/features/notifications/hooks/useNotifications.ts:23,34`) carry no quantity and are not dropped. **Mechanism.** `dropVisibilityCaches(queryClient)` in `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/visibilityVersionGate.ts` runs, in order, `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: ['stock-transfers'] })`, `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: ['inventory-stock-matrix'] })`, `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: ['inventory', 'stock-matrix'] })`, `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: ['replenishment'] })` — BARE literal prefixes, the same form the existing mutations already use for invalidation (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/api/queries.ts:36,48`, `apps/web/src/features/replenishment/api/queries.ts:14`), which match every leaf above whatever the trailing tenant/company. A bare prefix also removes another company's entries in the same browser session; for `removeQueries` that is harmless (nothing is refetched until a mount asks) and deliberately preferred over the suffix-scope predicate form of `apps/web/src/features/inventory/_invalidation.ts:24-38` (which is the correct shape if a lane ever needs scoping: `k[0] === 'stock-transfers' && k[k.length - 2] === tenantId && k[k.length - 1] === companyId`). `useVisibilityVersionGate()` keeps the last seen version under `tenantScopedKey(['visibility-version'])` (`queryClient.setQueryData`); each blind-relevant hook passes its response through it in `select`: on a differing (or absent) stored version it FIRST stores the new version, THEN calls `dropVisibilityCaches` — so the one refetch of the triggering surface that removal provokes sees a matching version and cannot loop. The fraud-settings mutations (`apps/web/src/features/compliance/pages/FraudSettingsPage.tsx:86-111`; edit target — today `onSuccess` invalidates only the fraud-settings predicate at `apps/web/src/features/compliance/pages/FraudSettingsPage.tsx:89-91,104-106`; `apps/web/src/features/compliance/api/fraudApi.ts:28-38` only performs HTTP calls) store `data.data.visibility_version` then call the same helper. No claim is made that these steps are atomic across caches or that a particular response is "first"; the residual is R4 (§5.0). The lines table derives its columns from the payload discriminator (`blind` + presence of `quantity`).
- **Detail page narrows on `blind` before reading any full-only member (r7-M2).** The summary section reads them unconditionally today — `transfer.source_location_name` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:131`), `transfer.transfer_cost` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:136`), `transfer.transfer_cost_distribution` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:139`), `transfer.initiated_by_name` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:141`) and the completed rows through `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:146-154` — all inside the `<dl>` opened at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:128` and closed at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:176`. **Corrected edit range (r8 citation audit):** the block does not end at line 152 — the cancelled rows (`cancelled_by_name`, `cancelled_at`, `cancellation_reason`) run `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:155-167` and the notes row runs `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:168-175`, both reading full-only members too, so the real edit target is the whole section `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:124-176`. After the union it splits once, `if (isReceiverTransfer(transfer))`: the receiver branch renders transfer number, status, `transfer.source_location.name`, `transfer.destination_location.name` and `transfer.initiated_at`, and nothing else (no cost row, no cost-distribution row, no initiated-by/completed-by/cancelled-by rows, no notes — those members do not exist on the receiver shape, §5.0b); the full branch is today's block verbatim. No `?? '—'` fallback on a member the union does not carry: an absent concept is an absent row, not an em dash on a full-shape label.
- Detail page (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx`): `canComplete` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:38`) replaced by `TRANSFER_ACTIONS: Record<TransferStatus, { receive: boolean; close: boolean; cancel: boolean }>` (exhaustive; precedent `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/components/StockTransferStatusBadge.tsx:5-10`). **"Receive all" (today's Complete button, `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferDetailPage.tsx:98-107`) renders iff `TRANSFER_ACTIONS[status].receive && transfer.blind === false`** — `blind` comes from the server's builder choice (B1); the receiver shape has no such button. **Receive** → link to `ReceiveTransferPage` (`QuantityInput` strings at `unit.decimal_places`; `Select` for `discrepancy_reason` limited to `damaged_in_transit | other`, enabled when damaged > 0; lot rows; one `crypto.randomUUID()` key per page mount; replay 200 = success). **Close** `ConfirmDialog` with disposition radio, reason `Select` (all four), note `Textarea` — rendered only with reconcile AND close (r4-M4). **Reconciliation** tab (`RequirePermission permission="inventory.transfers.reconcile"`), variance cells in design tokens (rule 18).
- List page (r2-M8): `STATUS_OPTIONS` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferListPage.tsx:15-21`) is replaced by
  ```ts
  const TRANSFER_STATUS_FILTERS = { draft: true, in_transit: true, partially_received: true, completed: true,
  ```
