  ```ts
  const STATUS_OPTIONS = ['all', ...(Object.keys(TRANSFER_STATUS_FILTERS) as (keyof typeof TRANSFER_STATUS_FILTERS)[])] as const
  ```
  (precedent `apps/web/src/features/import/errorCodes.ts:3-19`). A generated case missing from the object literal fails `pnpm typecheck`; badge `Record` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/components/StockTransferStatusBadge.tsx:5-10`) forces the three new variants.
- **List rows narrow on `blind` too (r7-M2).** Four cells read full-only members unconditionally today: `tx.source_location_name` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferListPage.tsx:125`), `tx.destination_location_name` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferListPage.tsx:128`), `tx.transfer_cost` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferListPage.tsx:134`) and `tx.created_at` (`apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferListPage.tsx:137`), inside the row map at `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/StockTransferListPage.tsx:114-137` (edit target). After the union each row computes its cells once through `isReceiverTransfer(tx)`: the two location cells fall back to `tx.source_location.name` / `tx.destination_location.name` on a receiver row (the same rendered string, a different member), and the cost and created-at cells render the em dash `'—'` for a receiver row — the table keeps one column set for both shapes, since a receiver may hold transfers of both kinds in one page when only some of them satisfy `canSeeExpected`. Transfer number, the `Link` and `StockTransferStatusBadge` read members both shapes carry and are untouched.
- Replenishment (r3-B1): `ReplenishmentLine.requested_qty` is already `string | null` (`apps/web/src/features/replenishment/types/index.ts:4`); the history list (`apps/web/src/features/replenishment/pages/ReplenishmentQueuePage.tsx:147,194`, today renders `matrix.requested_no_qty` for null) renders `t('replenishment:matrix.requested_masked')` when `meta.transfer_quantities_masked && line.fulfillment_type === 'transfer'`; the response `meta` is read through `api.get` (the paginated pattern — `apiGet` drops `meta`).
- i18n: `stock-transfers` (`apps/web/src/lib/i18n.ts:52,108,218,275`) keys `receive.*`, `close.*`, `reconciliation.*`, `status.{partially_received,closed_with_writeoff,closed_returned}`, `discrepancyReason.*`, `errors.<code>` for every `TransferReceiptFailureReason` case plus `INVALID_TRANSFER_STATE` in en/fr; Arabic keeps the existing English fallback for this namespace (`apps/web/src/lib/i18n.ts:474-477`) — stated. `notifications`, `compliance` and `replenishment` get the new keys.
- **Movement feeds — nullable quantities on the two web consumers (r8-B1, §5.10).** Both masked feeds have exactly one web consumer family each; there is no other reader in `apps/web` (search over `apps/web/src` for `/stock-movements` and `/entry-exit-notes`). (a) `GET /stock-movements` is read by `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:145,162` and by `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:96`, both bound to the exported row type `StockMovement` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/types.ts:29-57`; the endpoint's envelope at `apps/web/src/features/inventory/types.ts:60-63`). `quantity` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/types.ts:38`), `quantity_before` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/types.ts:40`) and `quantity_after` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/types.ts:41`) become `string | null`, and `StockMovementsResponse.meta` becomes `OffsetPaginationMeta & { transfer_movement_quantities_masked: boolean }` (an intersection at the call site, not a new member on the shared `OffsetPaginationMeta`, which other endpoints share — `apps/web/src/features/inventory/types.ts:60-63`). The four read sites must then narrow: `bccomp(movement.quantity, '0')` and `formatQuantity(movement.quantity, …)` at `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:267,299,302` and `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:234,260`, and the before/after cells at `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:312,319` and `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:264,267` — a `null` renders the em dash `'—'` (no sign, no `formatQuantity` call, never `parseFloat` — rule 19) and the column header carries `t('inventory:stockMovements.maskedHint')` when `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked` is true. `pnpm typecheck` fails on every un-narrowed read: that compiler failure is the mechanism. (b) `GET /entry-exit-notes` is read by `apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:73,81`; its local `EntryExitNoteLine` members `quantity` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:28`), `quantity_before` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:30`) and `quantity_after` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:31`) become `string | null`, its `EntryExitNoteResponse.meta` (`apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:54-59`) gains the same boolean, and the single render site `apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:136` renders the em dash without the `'-'`/`'+'` direction prefix when the line's quantity is `null`. i18n: `inventory` namespace keys `stockMovements.maskedHint` and `entryExitNotes.maskedHint` in en/fr (rule 11). **(c) Zero staleness on the three movement queries (r9-m1) — edit targets.** Because §5.10 makes these feeds blind-relevant, each of the three `useQuery` calls gains `staleTime: 0, refetchOnWindowFocus: true`, overriding the client defaults `staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5` and `refetchOnWindowFocus: false` (`apps/web/src/lib/queryClient.ts:3-9`): `apps/web/src/features/inventory/StockMovementsPage.tsx:144-171` (today the options are `enabled` and `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` only — `keepPreviousData` is retained, it re-renders the PREVIOUS PAGE of the same key family while the next page loads and is scoped to one tenant/company by `usePlaceholderScopeGuard`, so it cannot resurrect a pre-flip response for a different setting), `apps/web/src/features/inventory/components/ProductMovementsTab.tsx:85-101` (today `enabled` only) and `apps/web/src/features/inventory/EntryExitNotesPage.tsx:73-85` (today `enabled` only). Their key prefixes — `['stock-movements', …]` and `['product-movements', …]` through `locationScopedKey`, `['entry-exit-notes', …]` through `tenantScopedKey` — are deliberately NOT added to `dropVisibilityCaches` (§8 cache-hygiene bullet): the two endpoints carry no `visibility_version` (§5.10), so there is no version for the gate to compare, and zero staleness plus focus refetching is the whole convergence mechanism for them. Residual R4 is unchanged and covers the window before that first refetch.
- Settings: `apps/web/src/features/compliance/components/CashDrawerControlsSection.tsx:8-15,47-54` pattern — `Checkbox` "Blind receiving" in a new `ReceivingControlsSection` on `apps/web/src/features/compliance/pages/FraudSettingsPage.tsx` (`apps/web/src/features/compliance/pages/FraudSettingsPage.tsx:397-403`).
- Tests: Vitest for the receive page, close dialog gating (no Close without reconcile AND close), blind rendering (no expected column and no "Receive all" when `blind: true`), reconciliation tab, route guard (complete-only user reaches list/detail/receive; view-only user does not reach receive; **r7-M3 as an EXECUTABLE acceptance contract, corrected for the two-permission close default (r8-M1) — two distinct named cases in `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/pages/__tests__/StockTransferDetailPage.permissions.test.tsx`:** (i) `"reconcile-only user opens the detail page and sees the reconciliation tab without Close"` — the actor holds `inventory.transfers.reconcile` and NEITHER `inventory.transfers.view`, `inventory.transfers.complete` nor `inventory.transfers.close`; the case asserts the detail route renders (not a `Navigate` to `/dashboard`), `getByRole('tab', { name: t('reconciliation.tab') })` resolves, and `queryByRole('button', { name: t('close.action') })` is `null`. **First failing assertion against today's code:** the reconciliation-tab query, because the detail route is guarded by `permission="inventory.transfers.view"` (`apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1425`) and `RequirePermission` redirects the actor to `/dashboard` before the page mounts (`apps/web/src/features/auth/components/RequirePermission.tsx:37-75`, the any-of/all-of branch at `apps/web/src/features/auth/components/RequirePermission.tsx:60-62`). (ii) `"reconcile+close user sees the reconciliation tab and the Close action"` — the same actor plus `inventory.transfers.close`; both queries resolve. The two cases together pin the applied default of §5.2 and §5.8 (Close needs BOTH permissions) and agree with T18's reconcile-only `close` 403 — the rev-8 wording that required a reconcile-only user to SEE Close is deleted as unsatisfiable. A third case, `"user with none of the three read permissions is blocked on list and detail"`, asserts the redirect on both routes**), **movement-feed masking (r8-B1): `apps/web/src/features/inventory/__tests__/StockMovementsPage.masked.test.tsx` renders a fixture page whose `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked` is `true` and whose rows mix one transfer-linked row with `quantity`/`quantity_before`/`quantity_after` all `null` and one PO row with real strings — the masked row's three cells render the em dash and no `NaN`, `undefined` or `'0'`, the PO row renders its formatted quantities, and the masked-hint header text is present; `apps/web/src/features/inventory/__tests__/EntryExitNotesPage.masked.test.tsx` does the same for a note whose `lines[0].quantity` is `null` (em dash, no `'-'`/`'+'` prefix) beside a line with a real quantity**, **receiver-shaped payload rendering (r7-M2): the list and detail Vitest fixtures include a REAL receiver payload built from the §5.0b receiver shape (nested `source_location`/`destination_location`, `initiated_at`, `blind: true`, no `transfer_cost`, `created_at`, `initiated_by_name`, `notes` or `transfer_cost_distribution` member) mixed in the same list page beside a full payload; the list renders both rows with the two location names and an em dash in the cost and created-at cells of the receiver row, the detail page renders the receiver summary with no cost/initiated-by row and no "Receive all" button, and neither test throws or prints `undefined`; a type-level test asserts `isReceiverTransfer` narrows `StockTransferShowResponse` in both directions and that reading `transfer_cost` off the un-narrowed union does not compile (`@ts-expect-error`)**, the best-effort version gate (r5-M3: the test seeds REAL leaf-shaped keys exactly as `apps/web/src/features/stock-transfers/__tests__/queries.test.tsx:47-58` does — `['stock-transfers', 'list', {}, 'tenant-1', 'company-1']`, `['stock-transfers', 'detail', 'transfer-1', 'tenant-1', 'company-1']`, `['inventory-stock-matrix', '', 1, 25, { locScope: 'all' }, 'tenant-1', 'company-1']`, `['inventory', 'stock-matrix', 'rebalance', { locScope: 'all' }, 'tenant-1', 'company-1']`, `['replenishment', 'open', {}, 'tenant-1', 'company-1']`, plus `['notifications', 'u', 'list', 'tenant-1', 'company-1']` as the must-survive control — and asserts `queryClient.getQueryData(key)` is `undefined` for the five and defined for the control after a transfer response whose version differs, that a same-version response leaves all six, and that a fraud-settings mutation behaves like the differing response), and a type-level test that `TRANSFER_STATUS_FILTERS` compiles against the generated union.

## 8b. POS device client (`apps/pos`) — surfaces 8–9, 18 and the best-effort version gate (B2, r3-B1, r4-B1). Every line here is an edit target; "today" states the current code.

- Types: `incoming_transfer` becomes `string | null` in `apps/pos/src/types/stockDistribution.ts:12,28` (row and totals; today `string`) and in `ServerIncomingRow` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:55-61`; today `string`). `LocationStockRow` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:40`) and `LocationStockDisplay` (`apps/pos/src/lib/stock/gridStock.ts:33-37,42-46,95-100`) stay `string`. `ServerReplenishmentRow.requested_qty/suggested_qty` are already `string | null` (`apps/pos/src/api/replenishmentApi.ts:17-18`) and the feed validator already accepts null (`apps/pos/src/lib/replenishment/replenishmentSyncService.ts:154-159`); the cache columns are nullable (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/migrations.ts:1888`) — no POS replenishment type change. **POS equivalent of the web prefix removal (r5-M3).** The POS keeps no TanStack cache for surfaces 8, 9 or 18: `@tanstack/react-query` is imported by `apps/pos/src/App.tsx` (the provider) and two tests only; the device caches are the SQLite tables `location_stock` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/migrations.ts:1589`), `product_stock_distribution_cache` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/crossLocationStockRepository.ts:18-24`) and `open_replenishment_cache` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/openReplenishmentRepository.ts:40-47,89`), and `dropVisibilityCaches(db)` below is their removal step.
- Persistence normalisation at the single write site: `replaceIncoming` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:191-230`) binds `r.incoming_transfer ?? '0'` at `apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:216` (today binds the raw value); the SQLite column stays `TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0'` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/migrations.ts:1589`). Step 1 of the function zeroes every incoming column before the upsert (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:195-199`), so exact values are replaced on every successful `pullLocationStock` (incoming is complete on page 1, `apps/pos/src/lib/sync/syncService.ts:1035-1036,1122-1128`; written under `withWriteTransaction('sync', (tx) => replaceIncoming(tx as unknown as Database, incoming))` at `apps/pos/src/lib/sync/syncService.ts:1146-1148`).
- **Version hint (best-effort, r4-B1).** Each of the three version-bearing pulls — `pullLocationStock` (page 1 `meta.visibility_version`), the distribution fetch (before `upsertDistribution`, `apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/crossLocationStockRepository.ts:43-62`) and the replenishment pull (`apps/pos/src/lib/replenishment/replenishmentSyncService.ts:97-124`, before `replaceOpenRequests`, `apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/openReplenishmentRepository.ts:40-47`) — compares the response version with `getSyncMetadata(db, 'visibility_version')` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/syncLogRepository.ts:19-26`; table `sync_metadata`, `apps/pos/src/lib/db/migrations.ts:162-166`). On a difference (or no stored value) the pull, inside its own write transaction, runs the shared helper `dropVisibilityCaches(db)` — `UPDATE location_stock SET incoming_transfer = '0'` (the same zeroing statement `replaceIncoming` runs at `apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/locationStockRepository.ts:195-199`), `DELETE FROM product_stock_distribution_cache` (new `clearAllDistribution` in `apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/crossLocationStockRepository.ts`) and the scoped `DELETE FROM open_replenishment_cache` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/openReplenishmentRepository.ts:89`) — then `setSyncMetadata(db, 'visibility_version', v)` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/syncLogRepository.ts:28-35`), then its own upsert. One helper, three callers, but no claim that the device's caches are consistent as a set after any single response: a cache another pull has not yet refreshed is empty/zero until that pull runs. Residual R4: a device offline since before the flip keeps its cache until its first successful pull; the 60 s tick (`apps/pos/src/lib/sync/syncService.ts:1019-1024`) bounds the online window.
- Renderers: `apps/pos/src/components/organisms/ProductGrid/ProductTable.tsx:115-124` and `apps/pos/src/components/molecules/ProductCard/ProductCard.tsx:233-250` read the normalised row (unchanged arithmetic — masked transfer incoming reads as 0, so "arriving" derives from `incoming_po` alone). `apps/pos/src/components/organisms/CrossLocationStockSection/CrossLocationStockSection.tsx:104-117` (today formats the value, no masked branch) renders `t('crossLocationStock.masked')` ("—") when `row.incoming_transfer === null` / `data.totals.incoming_transfer === null`; keys added to `apps/pos/src/locales/en/pos.json` (block `crossLocationStock` at `apps/pos/src/locales/en/pos.json:323-333`) and `apps/pos/src/locales/fr/pos.json` (block at `apps/pos/src/locales/fr/pos.json:323`). `RequestRefillSheet` already treats a null `suggested_qty` as "no suggestion" (`apps/pos/src/components/organisms/RequestRefillSheet/RequestRefillSheet.tsx:76-78`). The distribution cache stores the payload as opaque JSON (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/crossLocationStockRepository.ts:18-24`), so `null` round-trips unchanged.
- Fixture `apps/pos/src/pages/ThemePreviewPage.tsx:784` unchanged. Tests: Vitest for `replaceIncoming` with `null` → row `'0'`; `dropVisibilityCaches` from each of the three callers (mismatch drops all three caches and stores the version; match leaves them); `CrossLocationStockSection` masked render; `pnpm typecheck` in `apps/pos`.
- **Movement feeds: no POS consumer (r8-B1).** Neither `GET /stock-movements` nor `GET /entry-exit-notes` is called anywhere in `apps/pos/src` (search over `apps/pos/src` for both paths returns no match), and no POS SQLite table caches either feed — the device caches are `location_stock` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/migrations.ts:1589`), `product_stock_distribution_cache` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/crossLocationStockRepository.ts:18-24`) and `open_replenishment_cache` (`apps/pos/src/lib/db/repositories/openReplenishmentRepository.ts:40-47,89`). The §5.10 masking therefore requires no POS type, renderer, cache or test change, and `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked` has no POS reader.
- `REALIGNMENT-LOG.md` entries: nullable `incoming_transfer`, `meta.incoming_transfer_masked`, `meta.visibility_version` on both POS stock endpoints; masked quantities + `transfer_quantities_masked` + `visibility_version` on the POS replenishment feed (§11 step 5).

## 9. Mobile (M-1 later) — contract commitments only (`/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile` @ `51e3445`)
- **The mobile receipt screen is the PRIMARY receiving surface, and for a blind actor it must be blind BY CONSTRUCTION (owner, `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`).** The owner's ruling names "the mobile app (the intended primary receiving surface)" as the place blind receiving must hold, alongside the web receipt surfaces. Three commitments follow, and they are contract statements only — **the mobile screen itself is still M-1 (later) and is NOT in this lane** (§1 "Out of scope" is unchanged; nothing below asks for a screen to be built here). (1) **Server-driven receiver projection**: the screen renders exclusively what `GET /stock-transfers/{id}/receiver-view` returns, built by `TransferReceiverPayloadBuilder` for the requesting actor at request time (§5.0 row 1, §5.3) — the omission is the server's, never a client-side hide of a field the response carried. (2) **It never fetches or caches the full transfer shape**: no call to `GET /stock-transfers/{id}` or `GET /stock-transfers` from the receive flow, and nothing derived from a full payload is persisted in a mobile cache that the receive screen reads — so there is no local copy of a sent/expected/remaining quantity for a blind actor to reach, online or offline. (3) **A NEVER-INCLUDE guard on the generated types**, in the exact shape counting already ships: `my_count` carries only the counter's own prior figure and the type carries the literal comment `// NEVER INCLUDE: theoretical_qty, count_1_qty, count_2_qty, count_3_qty` (`/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src/features/counting/api/countingApi.ts:193-197`). The transfer equivalent is the `TransferReceiverLine` guard of the bullet below. **The web receipt surface is under the same rule**: `ReceiveTransferPage` (§8) renders the receiver projection, the detail page narrows on `blind` before reading any full-only member, and "Receive all" is not rendered in blind mode (§8, OD-1 in §12). Neither surface relies on R5 staying invisible: R5 is a stock POSITION and is out of the guarantee by the same ruling (§5.0).
- Receive screen uses ONLY `/receiver-view` and `/receive` with a client key per submission; offline replay resends the same key (`/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src/features/counting/services/pendingCountSyncService.ts:58-68`) and treats 200 + `meta.replayed` as success.
- `/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src/features/receiving/types.ts:58-73,98-109` get a SUPERVISOR-SHAPE comment; new `TransferReceiverLine` / `PurchaseOrderReceiverLine` carry `// NEVER INCLUDE: quantity, quantity_ordered, quantity_remaining, quantity_sent, notes`. T-3b adds `GET /purchase-orders/{id}/receiver-view` under the same setting and masks PO incoming.
- Version hint (best-effort; r5-M3 mobile equivalent): mobile keys are key factories without a tenant suffix — `receivingKeys.all = ['receiving']` with leaves `['receiving', 'list']`, `['receiving', 'detail', id]`, `['receiving', 'receipt-status', id]` (`/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src/features/receiving/queries.ts:5-11`), invalidated by the same factories (`/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src/features/receiving/queries.ts:45-48`). M-1 adds `transferReceivingKeys.all = ['transfer-receiving']` (leaves `'list'`, `'receiver-view'`/id) and stores `visibility_version` per tenant+company in the account store; on a mismatch it stores the new version, then runs `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: receivingKeys.all })` and `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: transferReceivingKeys.all })` (bare prefixes match every leaf), then caches the response; residual R4 applies (a cached view persists until the next sync).
- **Movement feeds: no mobile consumer either (r8-B1).** A search over `/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/erp-mobile/src` for `/stock-movements` and `/entry-exit-notes` returns no match, so the §5.10 masking adds no mobile contract commitment; the mobile NEVER-INCLUDE guards stay as stated above.
- No "receive all" action exists on mobile; `close` is not a mobile action.

The receipt-linked `Damage` row (`reference_type = 'stock_transfer_receipt'`, §6.1) posted against T1 is masked too, and the one posted against T2 is not. `GET /entry-exit-notes` → the same three keys `null` inside `lines[]` for the T1 note (whose group deliberately contains BOTH receivers' movements, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:44-72,216-254`), intact for the T2 note, `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked === true`. **Reconcile actor** (`canSeeIncomingAggregates = true`): the same two requests return every quantity, including T1's, with `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked === false` — the liveness half, so a masking bug that nulls everything fails here. **Setting OFF**: the blind actor's own two requests return every quantity with the flag `false`. **Terminal transition**: after the transfer is closed `write_off`, the same blind actor re-issues both requests and T1's rows are no longer masked (accepted R2). Second company: B's blind actor sees B's rows only and A's masking state does not affect B. Both DB lanes |

### 10.1 T9 procedure — `TransferBlindLeakOracleTest` (r5-M2; r6-M1; r6-M3; r8-B1; **r9-B1 — scope of absence narrowed to transfer-derived surfaces, R5 positive controls added**; `apps/api/tests/Feature/Inventory/TransferBlindLeakOracleTest.php`, trait `apps/api/tests/Support/AssertsBlindPayloads.php`)

**Scope of absence (r9-B1, owner-ruled).** Gate r9 proved the rev-9 oracle unsatisfiable: it required the sentinel `7391.4517` to be absent from the whole stock-matrix and POS bodies, while the fixture itself drives the destination `stock_levels` row for product P at L2 to exactly that value and those bodies emit it as `on_hand`/`quantity`. Under the owner's ruling (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`) the oracle is split in two, and both halves are executable:

- **Absence — transfer-derived surfaces ONLY.** `assertNoForbiddenKeys`, `assertNoSentinelValues`, `assertNullOnTransferRows` and `assertNullOnTransferMovements` run on the transfer, receipt, replenishment, movement/entry-exit and notification surfaces: steps 6a–6o, the corresponding halves of step 7 and step 8. That is the §5.0 guarantee, unchanged in strength from rev 9.
- **Presence — stock-position surfaces (R5).** New steps 6p–6u assert with `assertSentinelsPresent` that the blind actor DOES receive the current-stock figures on the R5 surfaces of §5.0 rows 19–25 (rows 19–24 from rev 10, row 25 `GET /products/{product}` added by rev 11 under r10-M1; the sub-steps 6s-c/6s-d, 6t-c and 6u-d/6u-e are likewise rev-11 additions, and 6t-b became a value assertion instead of a bare 200). These are positive controls, not leak assertions: they fail if a future change silently masks a stock position (which would break POS selling, counting and replenishment for the blind role) and they fail if the R5 acceptance was never real. **This is what makes the acceptance falsifiable**: the residual is written into the test as an expected observation rather than left as prose.
- **Mixed bodies are pruned, never scanned whole.** Three surfaces carry BOTH a masked transfer member and an R5 stock-position member in one body: stock matrix (`cells[].incoming` masked, `cells[].on_hand` R5), `GET /pos/stock-levels` (`incoming[].incoming_transfer` masked, `stock[].quantity` R5) and `GET /pos/products/{P}/stock-distribution` (`incoming_transfer` masked, `on_hand` R5). For those, the value scan runs on a PRUNED copy of the body with the R5 member keys removed (helper below), and the same body is then asserted PRESENT-ful on exactly those members. Rev 9's "every response body is scanned WHOLE" therefore holds for every surface except these three, where it is replaced by the stated prune-then-assert pair — an explicit, reviewable exception rather than a silent one.

**Assertion helpers (trait).** `assertNoForbiddenKeys(array $json, array $forbiddenKeys, string $surface): void` — explicit recursive descent over every array (assoc and list) at any depth; fails with `"$surface: forbidden key <key> at <json.path>"` (`array_walk_recursive` is NOT used: it visits leaves only and never sees keys of nested arrays). `assertNoSentinelValues(array $json, array $sentinels, string $surface): void` — recursive descent over every scalar: a numeric scalar (int, float or numeric string) fails when `bccomp((string) $value, $sentinel, 4) === 0` for any numeric sentinel; a string fails when it CONTAINS any sentinel as a substring (catches `"send 7391.4517"` inside a note or a message as well as the notes sentinel). `assertSentinelsPresent(array $json, array $sentinels, string $surface): void` — the inverse (each SUPPLIED sentinel found at least once, by the same numeric/substring matching) — the liveness proof for the two scanners; it is only ever called with a subset from table P below, never with the whole of `S` (r6-M3). `assertCellEquals(array $json, string $path, string $expected): void` — `bccomp` at 4 dp on one addressed value (the derived matrix cell of table P). `assertVisibilityVersion(array $json, string $path, int $expected): void`. `assertNullOnTransferRows(array $rows, array $fields): void` (replenishment rows with `fulfillment_type = 'transfer'`: each named key present and `null`). **`assertNullOnTransferMovements(array $rows, string $transferId, array $fields): void` (r8-B1)** — for the movement feeds: every row whose `reference_type` is `App\Modules\Inventory\Domain\StockTransfer` with `reference_id === $transferId`, or whose `reference_type` is `'stock_transfer_receipt'` and whose receipt belongs to that transfer, has each named key PRESENT and `null`; every other row in the same payload is left to the caller's own assertions, so a blanket-null bug is caught by the control row rather than passing. The entry/exit variant descends into `data[].lines[]` before applying the same rule, keyed on the note's `source_id`/`source_type`. **`withoutKeys(array $json, array $keys): array` (r9-B1)** — returns a deep copy of the body with every occurrence of each named key, at any depth, removed; it is the pruning step the three mixed bodies use before `assertNoSentinelValues`, and it is asserted to have actually removed something (a prune that removes nothing means the member was renamed and the surface must be re-audited, so the helper fails rather than passing vacuously).

**Sentinels (`S`) — DOCUMENT quantities** that occur nowhere else in the fixture and cannot be produced by arithmetic on the actor's own postings; these are the values the §5.0 guarantee forbids on transfer-derived surfaces: `S = { '7391.4517' (line-1 sent = replenishment requested qty of the P request), '5000.0000' (line-1 remainder after the blind receipt — and, from step 5b on, the quantity the SECOND destination receiver posts on line 1, r8-B1), '2617.2500' (line-2 sent, batch-allocated), '1017.2500' (the lot quantity the SECOND destination receiver posts on line 2 against batch B1 at step 5b — r9-B1), '1600.0000' (line-2 remainder after step 5b), '613.7000' (transfer_cost), '41.3300' (unit_cost_snapshot), 'SENT-SENTINEL-7391' (transfer notes) }`. The P replenishment request's note is `'send 7391.4517'` — authored by `U_other`, not by the blind actor, and carrying the first sentinel as a substring (r6-M1). The blind actor's own posting is `2391.4517` (allowed to appear in the receipt echo, `my_receipts` and notes — but NOT, from rev 9 on, in a movement-feed `quantity`/`quantity_before`/`quantity_after`, which §5.10 nulls for this actor on every transfer-linked carrying row including their own). PO incoming for product P at L2 is `12.0000` (a confirmed PO line, still outstanding — proves PO incoming stays visible; not a sentinel). **Movement control value (r8-B1)** `'88.0000'` — a stock-adjustment receive of product PC at L2, on no transfer at all: it MUST stay visible on both movement feeds for the blind actor, so a blanket-null implementation fails the test as loudly as a leaking one.

**R5 control values (`R`) — STOCK POSITIONS the blind actor MUST still receive (r9-B1, owner ruling `docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`).** These are asserted PRESENT, never absent, on the §5.0 rows 19–24 surfaces: `R = { '7391.4517' (product P's on-hand at L2 after step 5b: 0 → 2391.4517 → 7391.4517 — numerically equal to line-1 sent, which is exactly the accepted delta inference and the reason this control exists), '1017.2500' (product PB's on-hand at L2, and batch B1's lot quantity at L2, after step 5b), '88.0000' (product PC's on-hand at L2 from the non-transfer adjustment) }`. `R` gains no member in rev 11: the surfaces added by r10-M1 all emit values already in `R`. The other figures rev 11 introduces are deliberately outside BOTH sets so they can never satisfy or break an existing assertion — the thresholds `100.0000` (P at L3, `min_quantity`) and `1000.0000` (P at L2, `max_quantity`), the derived rebalance `excess` `6391.4517`, and the POS-batch request quantity `2000.0000` with its `shortfall` `982.7500`. `R` and `S` deliberately OVERLAP on `7391.4517` and `1017.2500`: the same number is forbidden on transfer-derived surfaces and required on stock-position surfaces. That overlap is the whole content of the owner's ruling, and writing it as two named sets is what makes the design reviewable — a future change that masks stock positions fails the `R` assertions, and a future change that leaks the figure through a transfer surface fails the `S` assertions.

**Why the second receiver matters (r8-B1, extended by r9-B1).** After step 5b, `U_blind2` has posted `5000.0000` of line 1 and `1017.2500` of line 2's batch-B1 allocation, so line 1 is fully received, **line 2 still has `1600.0000` open, and the transfer therefore stays `partially_received` and still receivable** — the exact composition gate r8 described, now also exercised at lot grain. The destination `stock_levels` row for product P runs `0 → 2391.4517 → 7391.4517` and the `batch_stock` row for B1 at L2 runs `0 → 1017.2500`. Two consequences, and rev 10 treats them differently because the owner ruled them different: (1) an unmasked MOVEMENT feed would hand the blind actor the other receiver's `quantity` (`5000.0000`, `1017.2500`) and a `quantity_after` (`7391.4517`) that is exactly the sent quantity, and step 5c's `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` confirms line 1's remainder is zero — that path is transfer data and stays CLOSED by §5.10, asserted by rows 6m/6n; (2) the same figures remain readable as STOCK POSITIONS through the R5 surfaces, and steps 6p–6u assert exactly that, so the residual is documented by an executable observation instead of a promise.

**Forbidden-key sets.** `K_transfer = { notes, transfer_cost, transfer_cost_label, cancellation_reason, initiated_by, quantity, quantity_remaining, quantity_sent, quantity_sent_snapshot, quantity_written_off, quantity_returned, unit_cost_snapshot, allocated_transfer_cost, receipts, close_disposition, close_reason, close_note, closed_at, closed_by_user_id, freight_uncapitalized }` (transfer surfaces; `quantity_received`/`quantity_damaged` are allowed because `my_receipts[]` legitimately carries the actor's own — the sentinel scan covers values). `K_receipt = { quantity_sent_snapshot, quantity_written_off, quantity_returned, in_movement_id, scrap_movement_id, return_movement_id }` (the 201 `receipt`). `K_notification = { quantity, quantity_sent, notes, requested_qty }`. `K_none = {}` for `GET /stock-movements` and `GET /entry-exit-notes`: the three quantity keys stay PRESENT on every row of both feeds (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:218,220,221`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:247,249,250`), so a key scan is the wrong instrument — **rev 8's justification that those values "are the actor's own movement" was FALSE (r8-B1): neither controller has an actor predicate** (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMovementController.php:60-80`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/EntryExitNoteController.php:44-72`). These two surfaces are therefore covered by the value scan PLUS the new null-key set below. **Null-key set (r8-B1)** `N_movement = { quantity, quantity_before, quantity_after }` — enforced by `assertNullOnTransferMovements` on every row linked to transfer T (whichever actor posted it, the blind actor's own row included) on both feeds, with the `'88.0000'` control row asserted intact in the same payload. **Null-key set (r6-M1)** `N_replenishment = { requested_qty, suggested_qty, note }` — on the replenishment feeds the keys stay present (the resource shape, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/Resources/ReplenishmentRequestResource.php:25,26,32`) and must be `null` on every transfer-linked row; enforced by `assertNullOnTransferRows`, with the value scan covering the rest of the row. **No key set at all on the R5 surfaces (r9-B1).** Steps 6p–6u run neither `assertNoForbiddenKeys` nor `assertNoSentinelValues`: a stock position carries no forbidden key by definition under the owner's ruling, and asserting sentinel absence there is exactly the unsatisfiable claim gate r9 rejected. Their only assertions are the 200 and `assertSentinelsPresent(body, P[surface])` with the `R` subset.

**Positive-control table P (r6-M3; corrected and extended by r9-B1)** — the value subset each surface is contractually capable of containing. Two kinds of row, and the difference is now explicit:

- **Transfer-derived rows (6a–6o, reconciliation)** carry the `S` subset the surface's FULL shape (setting OFF, or `U_reconcile` with the setting ON) can hold. `assertSentinelsPresent` is called per surface with exactly `P[surface]`, as `U_reconcile` in step 7 and as `U_blind` after the flip in step 8 — never as `U_blind` while the setting is on. EXCLUDED means the surface never emits those values for any actor; the blind assertions of step 6 still cover it, with absence.
- **R5 rows (6p–6u), new in rev 10 and completed in rev 11** carry the `R` subset the surface emits for EVERY actor, blind included. Rev 11 adds no letter to this block: every surface gate r10 found missing is a SUB-step of an existing letter — 6s-c/6s-d (counting reconciliation and its counter-facing pair, in the stock-level family because `theoretical_qty` is a snapshot of `stock_levels.quantity`), 6t-c (`GET /products/{P}`, in the product family) and 6u-d/6u-e (the batch list and the POS suggestion envelope, in the batch family) — so "6p–6u" continues to name the whole presence block wherever this document uses it. One of those sub-steps, **6s-d**, is an ABSENCE row inside the presence block and is marked as such in the table: it is the counter-facing half of the counting control, issued as `U_other` rather than `U_blind`, and it states the values that must NOT appear. It is listed here rather than among the 6a–6o rows because it is the paired half of 6s-c and only means anything beside it — the same document, the same three snapshots, two different readers. `assertSentinelsPresent` is called with `P[surface]` **as `U_blind` while the setting is ON** (step 6) and again as `U_reconcile` (step 7); there is no absence assertion on these surfaces at all.

**Column correction (r9-B1).** Rev 9 labelled the first column "P after step 5" but read it after steps 5b and 5c, when line 1 is already fully received — so its `5000.0000` remainders and its `'5012.0000'` derived matrix cell were stale by the time steps 6–8 ran. The column below is the state steps 6, 7 and 8 actually observe: **after step 5b**. Line 1: sent `7391.4517`, received in full, remainder `0.0000`. Line 2: sent `2617.2500`, received `1017.2500` against batch B1, remainder `1600.0000`. Destination positions at L2: P `7391.4517`, PB `1017.2500` (all of it in batch B1), PC `88.0000`. PO incoming for P at L2: `12.0000`.

| Surface | P after step 5b (the state steps 6–8 observe) | P pre-receipt (step 3) | Why this subset |
|---|---|---|---|
| 6a receiver-view | EXCLUDED | EXCLUDED | always `TransferReceiverPayloadBuilder` (§5.0 row 1) — no quantity, cost or note member for anyone |

| **6q** `GET /pos/stock-levels` `stock[]` (R5) | `7391.4517` (P `stock[].quantity`, and `available` with `reserved = 0`), `1017.2500` (PB), `88.0000` (PC) — PRESENT for `U_blind` | `88.0000` only | the terminal's own positions (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosStockLevelController.php:85-103`); masking them would stop the terminal selling (§5.0 R5) |

The counter side is the shipped blind-counting rule (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/InventoryCountingController.php:334-377`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:54-74`), re-asserted here on a document whose theoretical quantities are known sentinels; existing precedents `apps/api/tests/Feature/Inventory/BlindCountingTest.php:187-202,207-219,261-276` |

All three PRESENT for `U_blind` | `88.0000` on PC's row for 6t-a; 6t-b returns `{data: []}` before step 5d writes the thresholds and creates the L3 row (no cell carries a threshold yet, and P's only in-scope cell reads `0.0000`, so the no-threshold branch produces a receiver but no POSITIVE donor and the product is skipped — `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:78-94`; PB is skipped for the same reason and PC for the mirror one, a positive donor with no receiver); 6t-c reads `0.0000` for P and is asserted only for its 200 | product stock levels on `can:products.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:53-64`; rows at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:1064-1094`, totals at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:1096-1139`); rebalance on `can:inventory.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:73-75`) reading its thresholds from the `stock_levels` row itself (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:36-49`) and emitting a product only on a deficit/surplus pair among the in-scope cells (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:61-115`, the pair requirement at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:92-94`), which the L2/L3 membership and the step-5d thresholds make deterministic; product show on `can:products.view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:58-60`) with the aggregate computed at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/ProductController.php:304-319` and emitted at `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Application/DTOs/ProductData.php:131,166-183` |

PRESENT for `U_blind`. This is the lot-grain half of the accepted residual: the figure equals exactly what the second receiver posted against that allocation | `0.0000` for B1 at L2, so no R5 sentinel; the first four routes are still asserted 200 to prove reachability without a read permission, and the POS route returns `suggestions: []` with `shortfall: '2000.0000'` because the lot holds nothing yet | no read permission on any of the five (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php:22,24,42,45,48`, group gate `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php:12`); per-location lot quantities at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:264-281`, `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:105-116` with `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Resources/BatchResource.php:55-60`, and `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:329-356`; the list's eager load at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Infrastructure/Persistence/BatchRepository.php:116-119` with the collection returned at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/Controllers/BatchController.php:83-100`; the POS suggestion envelope at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionDTO.php:27-41` and `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Application/DTOs/BatchSuggestionResultDTO.php:23-35`, computed at `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:143-185`; the lot movement is written at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:168-176` |
| `GET /stock-transfers/{T}/reconciliation` | `7391.4517` (line-1 `sent`), `2617.2500` (line-2 `sent`), `1600.0000` (line-2 `remaining`), `1017.2500` (line-2 `received`) — line-1 `remaining` is `0.0000` after step 5b | n/a (not requested before step 5) | §5.4 shape has no cost or notes member; `summary.total_sent = 10008.7017` is not a sentinel |

**Procedure.**
1. Seed company A — created through the normal company setup with **`allow_cross_location_stock_view => true`** (r7-M1; the column is `$fillable` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Domain/Company.php:305` and cast `boolean` at `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Domain/Company.php:355`; the precedent for setting it on the factory in a feature test is `apps/api/tests/Feature/POS/StockDistributionEndpointTest.php:53`) — with locations L1 source, L2 destination `pos_enabled`, **L3 (r10-M1) — a second in-scope destination-side location of company A that receives NO movement of any kind for the whole test** (it exists only so the rebalance surface of step 6t-b can produce the deficit/surplus pair its algorithm requires, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockRebalanceQueryService.php:92-94`; because nothing is ever posted there it adds no row to either movement feed and no note group, so steps 6m and 6n are unaffected), lot-less product P (unit 4 dp, cost `41.3300`) seeded at L1 with EXACTLY `7391.4517` and batch-tracked product PB with one batch B1 seeded at L1 with EXACTLY `2617.2500` (so the TransferOut of initiation, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:571-595`, draws both L1 rows to `0.0000` and the company-wide sums asserted in steps 6t-a and 6t-c are the L2 positions alone — r10-M1), B1 carrying a FUTURE `expiry_date` with `is_active = true` and `is_recalled = false` so the FEFO predicates of step 6u-e return it (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Domain/Services/FEFOInventoryService.php:81-112`), a confirmed PO for P at L2 with `12.0000` outstanding, and — **r8-B1** — a third lot-less product PC that is on NO transfer, brought into L2 by one posted stock-adjustment receive of `88.0000` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Domain/Services/StockAdjustmentService.php:115-129`) so both movement feeds carry a non-transfer control row. Company A has the `BatchExpiry` module enabled, which the batch routes require alongside authentication (`apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php:12-48`) — without it steps 6u and its step-7 repeat would 403 on module gating rather than exercising the R5 residual. Users: `U_blind` — membership restricted to **L2 and L3** (rev 10 had L2 alone; the second membership is what makes step 6t-b deterministic, because `GET /inventory/stock-matrix/rebalance` intersects the request with `LocationScopeResolver::resolve` — `apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Services/LocationScopeResolver.php:31-67`, applied at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockMatrixController.php:51-61` — and a single in-scope location can never yield both a deficit and a surplus for one product. L3 is NOT the transfer's source: `U_blind` still cannot see L1, so nothing about the source side is widened, and every step that names L2 explicitly is unchanged), permissions `inventory.transfers.complete`, `inventory.view`, `replenishment.view`, `pos.operate_terminal`, **`pos.view_cross_location_stock`** (r7-M1) and **`products.view`** (r9-B1 — the gate on `GET /products/{product}/stock-levels`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/routes.php:53-64`, required for R5 step 6t-a; it is an ordinary catalogue read permission a receiver holds, not a transfer permission, so it widens nothing the guarantee covers); NOT `inventory.transfers.view`, `inventory.transfers.reconcile`, `inventory.transfers.close`, `fraud-settings.view`, `replenishment.create` (read gates: `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:70-84`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/routes.php:14-16`, `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/routes.php:142,148,161-162`). The batch routes need no read permission at all, which is itself part of what R5 accepts (§5.0 row 23). **Why both (r7-M1):** `GET /pos/products/{product}/stock-distribution` is DOUBLE-gated — `Gate::authorize('pos.view_cross_location_stock')` (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/StockDistributionController.php:37`) and then `abort(403, 'Cross-location stock view is disabled for this company.')` unless `companies.allow_cross_location_stock_view` (`apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/StockDistributionController.php:40-41`), a column whose migration default is `false` (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_06_14_100000_add_allow_cross_location_stock_view_to_companies.php:14`). Without both, steps 6j, 7 and 8 receive 403 and neither the blind scan nor the positive control ever sees that response shape; the permission is otherwise seeded only to `manager` (`apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:405,648`), and these fixture users are permissioned explicitly. `U_other` — a second destination user (membership restricted to L2) with `replenishment.create` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Replenishment/Presentation/routes.php:17-19`) and nothing else — the author of the replenishment notes (r6-M1). **`U_blind2` (r8-B1) — a SECOND destination RECEIVER: membership restricted to L2, permissions `inventory.transfers.complete` and `inventory.view`, and none of `inventory.transfers.view`, `inventory.transfers.reconcile`, `inventory.transfers.close`. It exists to post the hidden sentinel of step 5b; it is a distinct user from `U_blind`, so the leak the register describes (A reads B's posting) is reproduced rather than assumed away.** `U_reconcile` — `U_blind`'s membership and permissions + `inventory.transfers.reconcile`. `U_admin` — seeded admin.
2. As `U_other`, capture two replenishment requests at L2: P `7391.4517` with note `'send 7391.4517'`, PB `2617.2500` with note `'second lot please'`. As `U_admin`, `POST /replenishment-requests/actions/create-transfer` for both → one transfer T (`fulfillment_id = T` on both rows), with `notes = 'SENT-SENTINEL-7391'`, `transfer_cost = '613.7000'`, line 2 allocated wholly to B1. Then, as `U_other`, capture a third request for P at L2 with note `'PENDING-NOTE'` (stays pending, no transfer). Setting is OFF.
3. **Scanner liveness (setting OFF).** As `U_blind`: `GET /stock-transfers/{T}`, `GET /inventory/stock-matrix?include=incoming&location_ids[]=L2`, `GET /replenishment-requests?status=fulfilled` → `assertSentinelsPresent` with the pre-receipt column of table P (6b: the five values incl. the notes; 6h: `2617.2500` + `assertCellEquals` `'7403.4517'` on P's parent-row cell `incoming`; 6k: `7391.4517` — present in `requested_qty` AND in the note — and `2617.2500`). **The R5 scanners are proven live in the same step (r9-B1):** the matrix body's `cells[].on_hand` for PC at L2 reads `88.0000` and `GET /stock-levels` returns the same figure, so `assertSentinelsPresent` on the R5 members has found its subset before any R5 assertion of step 6 runs. A scanner that cannot find its subset here fails the test before any blind assertion runs.
4. As `U_admin`, `PATCH /fraud-settings {blind_receiving: true}` → `v = data.visibility_version` (T19 covers the +1).
5. As `U_blind`, `POST /stock-transfers/{T}/receive` line 1 `quantity_received: '2391.4517'` (fresh key) → 201; `assertNoForbiddenKeys(data.receipt, K_receipt)`, `assertNoForbiddenKeys(data.transfer, K_transfer)`, `assertNoSentinelValues(body, S)`, `assertVisibilityVersion(body, 'data.transfer.visibility_version', v)`. Line-1 remainder is now `5000.0000`, line 2 untouched.
5b. **Second destination receiver posts the hidden sentinels (r8-B1; lot grain added by r9-B1).** As `U_blind2`, ONE `POST /stock-transfers/{T}/receive` (its own fresh key) → 201, carrying two lines: line 1 `quantity_received: '5000.0000'`, and line 2 with `lots: [{ batch_id: B1, quantity_received: '1017.2500' }]` and the matching line total. Line 1 is now fully received; **line 2 keeps `1600.0000` open, so T stays `partially_received` and remains receivable** — the exact composition gate r8 describes, now also at lot grain so the batch-stock inference gate r9 identified is reproduced rather than assumed. Assert on `U_blind2`'s own 201 body the same helpers as step 5 (`K_receipt`, `K_transfer`, and `assertNoSentinelValues(body, S \ {'5000.0000', '1017.2500'})`: those two ARE its own submitted values and are echoed legitimately, so the exclusion is stated here rather than being a silent hole). The destination `stock_levels` row for P is now `7391.4517`; PB's is `1017.2500`, all of it in batch B1's `batch_stock` row at L2.
5c. **`NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` is exercised (r8-B1).** As `U_blind`, `POST /stock-transfers/{T}/receive` line 1 `quantity_received: '0.5000'` (fresh key) → 422 `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` (§5.1 rule B10), `assertNoSentinelValues(body, S)`, `array_keys(error.details) ⊆ {transfer_line_id, batch_id}`, `error.message` static. This is the probe that turns any surviving movement figure into an exact quantity, so it runs BEFORE the movement scans of step 6 and its 422 is asserted, not merely tolerated.
5d. **Deterministic fixtures for the two R5 surfaces that needed one (r10-M1).** Both are written AFTER step 5b so they observe the post-receipt positions P `7391.4517`, PB `1017.2500`, PC `88.0000` at L2. **(a) Rebalance thresholds.** As `U_admin`, two `PUT /inventory/stock-levels/thresholds` calls (`can:inventory.adjust`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:66-68`; controller `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/StockLevelController.php:82-99`; writer `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockThresholdService.php:15-67`): `{product_id: P, location_id: L3, min_quantity: '100.0000'}` — the L3 row does not exist yet and the writer CREATES it at `quantity '0.0000'` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockThresholdService.php:45-51`), which is the deficit cell — and `{product_id: P, location_id: L2, max_quantity: '1000.0000'}`, which makes L2 the surplus cell. Only ONE bound is written per row, so the request-level `max_quantity ≥ min_quantity` check never fires (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Requests/UpdateStockThresholdsRequest.php:54-64`). PB and PC receive no threshold, which is what keeps them out of the step-6t-b payload. **(b) Counting document.** As `U_admin`, `POST /inventory/countings` (`can:inventory.adjust`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:272-274`) with `scope_type: 'location'`, `scope_filters: {location_ids: [L2]}`, `include_zero_stock: false`, `requires_count_2: false`, `requires_count_3: false` and `count_1_user_id: U_other` (the request rules are `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Requests/CreateCountingRequest.php:37-87`; `count_2_user_id` is required only when `requires_count_2` is true, hence the explicit false) → counting **C**. `InventoryCountingService::create` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:73-120`) generates the items (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:133-169`) from seeds that copy `stock_levels.quantity` into `theoretical_qty` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:277-302`, the copy at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:297`) over the `quantity > 0` rows of L2 (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:505-559`, the predicate at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:558`), and registers `U_other` as counter 1 (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:564-578`). C therefore holds exactly three items whose theoretical quantities ARE the `R` set. **No activation is performed and none is needed**: `getItemsForCounter` has no status predicate (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:1053-1073`), the reconciliation endpoint only resolves the counting for the company (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:185-209`, the lookup at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:189`), and `POST /inventory/countings/{C}/activate` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:299-301`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/InventoryCountingService.php:699`) is deliberately NOT called — leaving C outside `pending_review` also keeps `replay_preview` `null` on every item (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/CountingReplayPreviewService.php:50-59`), so the reconciliation body carries no computed quantity beyond the snapshots. Creating C posts NO stock movement (movements are written only at finalize), so both movement feeds and every earlier assertion are untouched.
6. As `U_blind`, one request per row, in this order, applying the helpers listed. Every response body is scanned WHOLE (including `error` and `meta`), with exactly three stated exceptions — 6h, 6i and 6j, whose bodies are pruned of their R5 members by `withoutKeys` before the value scan (r9-B1) — and rows 6p–6u carry no absence assertion at all, being the R5 positive controls — with ONE stated exception introduced in rev 11, **6s-d**, which is the counter-facing half of the counting control and is therefore an ABSENCE assertion, and the only row of the whole step issued as an actor other than `U_blind` (it runs as `U_other`, the counter assigned in step 5d, because the counter-facing endpoints require assignment, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/InventoryCountingController.php:347-350`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:46-48`):

| Step | Request | Status | Keys | Values | Surface-specific | Version |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 6a | `GET /stock-transfers/{T}/receiver-view` | 200 | `K_transfer` | `S` | `blind === true`; `lines[1].lots[0].batch_number` present, no `quantity` beside it | `data.visibility_version == v` |
| 6b | `GET /stock-transfers/{T}` | 200 | `K_transfer` | `S` | receiver shape (`blind === true`) | `data.visibility_version == v` |
| 6c | `GET /stock-transfers?status=all` | 200 | `K_transfer` | `S` | T listed (any-of gate) | `meta.visibility_version == v` |
| 6d | `POST /stock-transfers/{T}/complete` | 422 | — | `S` | `error.code == BLIND_REQUIRES_COUNTED_RECEIPT`, `error.details == []`, no `data` key | not asserted |
| 6e | `POST /stock-transfers/{T}/close {disposition: write_off, reason: lost_in_transit}` | 403 | — | `S` | static body, no `data` key | not asserted |
| 6f | `POST /receive` line 1 `'9999.0000'` (fresh key) | 422 | — | `S` | `OVER_RECEIPT`; `array_keys(error.details) ⊆ {transfer_line_id, batch_id}` | not asserted |
| 6g | `POST /receive` with line 1 twice (fresh key) | 422 | — | `S` | `VALIDATION_ERROR`; `error.errors` keys only, static messages | not asserted |
| 6h | `GET /inventory/stock-matrix?include=incoming&location_ids[]=L2` | 200 | `K_none` | `S` on `withoutKeys(body, ['on_hand','reserved','available','min_quantity','max_quantity'])` — the R5 members are pruned FIRST (r9-B1), because `cells[].on_hand` for P at L2 legitimately reads `7391.4517` under the owner ruling and a whole-body scan would be unsatisfiable | cell for P at L2: `incoming == '12.0000'` (PO only, line 1 fully received); PB cell `incoming == '0.0000'` (transfer half masked); `meta.incoming_transfer_masked === true`. R5 half in step 6p on the SAME response | `meta.visibility_version == v` |
| 6i | `GET /pos/stock-levels` (L2 terminal) | 200 | `K_none` | `S` on `withoutKeys(body, ['quantity','reserved','available'])` — the R5 `stock[]` members are pruned FIRST (r9-B1); `incoming[]` has no key of those names, so the prune leaves the masked half fully scanned | every `incoming[].incoming_transfer === null`; `meta.incoming_transfer_masked === true`. R5 half in step 6q on the SAME response | `meta.visibility_version == v` |
| 6j | `GET /pos/products/{P}/stock-distribution` | 200 | `K_none` | `S` on `withoutKeys(body, ['on_hand'])` — the R5 member is pruned FIRST (r9-B1): the L2 row's `on_hand` and `totals.on_hand` legitimately read `7391.4517` | the status is asserted 200 FIRST — a 403 here means the step-1 preconditions (`pos.view_cross_location_stock` on the actor and `allow_cross_location_stock_view` on company A) regressed, and the masking assertions below would silently pass on an error body (r7-M1). Then: every row and `totals`: `incoming_transfer === null`; `meta.incoming_transfer_masked === true`. R5 half in step 6r on the SAME response | `meta.visibility_version == v` |

7. **Positive control (r6-M3; extended to the movement feeds by r8-B1; corrected and extended to R5 by r9-B1).** As `U_reconcile` (full builder — `canSeeExpected` via reconcile; and `canSeeIncomingAggregates = true`, so §5.10 does not mask it): 6b, 6c, 6h, 6i, 6j, 6k, 6l, **6m, 6n** and `GET /stock-transfers/{T}/reconciliation` → 200 each (6j included: `U_reconcile` is defined as `U_blind`'s permissions plus reconcile, so it carries `pos.view_cross_location_stock` and `products.view`, and the company switch is A's — r7-M1; a 403 on 6j fails the positive control rather than skipping it), `assertSentinelsPresent(body, P[surface])` with the "after step 5b" column (6b: `7391.4517`, `2617.2500`, `1600.0000`, `613.7000`, `41.3300`, `SENT-SENTINEL-7391`; 6c: `613.7000` + notes; 6h: `1600.0000` on PB's cell `incoming` + `assertCellEquals` `'12.0000'` on P's cell `incoming`; 6i: `1600.0000` on PB's `incoming_transfer`; 6j: EXCLUDED for sentinels — P's `incoming_transfer` is `0.0000` after step 5b — but the request is still made and `meta.incoming_transfer_masked === false` asserted; 6k: `7391.4517` — in `requested_qty` and in the note — and `2617.2500`, with `meta.transfer_quantities_masked === false` and `meta.notes_masked === false` inside `meta`; 6l: the same two values with `transfer_quantities_masked === false` and `notes_masked === false` **top-level beside `as_of`/`truncated`** (r8-m3, `apps/api/app/Modules/POS/Presentation/Controllers/PosReplenishmentController.php:106-110`); **6m/6n: `5000.0000`, `7391.4517` and `1017.2500` present as `U_blind2`'s TransferIn `quantity`, `quantity_after` and lot `quantity`, with `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked === false` and the `'88.0000'` control row intact — the r8-B1 liveness proof for the two surfaces the blind run must find nulled**; reconciliation: `7391.4517`, `2617.2500`, `1600.0000`, `1017.2500`), `blind === false` on 6b, `meta.incoming_transfer_masked === false` on 6h–6j, version `v` on every version-bearing row. 6a is re-issued as `U_reconcile` too and must still pass its step-6 key/value assertions (receiver builder for everyone). Any surface whose `P` subset is not found fails: the scanners are proven live on the very shapes the blind run must find empty. **R5 half (r9-B1; sub-steps completed in rev 11 under r10-M1):** 6p, 6q, 6r, 6s-a, 6s-b, **6s-c**, 6t-a, **6t-b**, **6t-c** and **6u-a/6u-b/6u-c/6u-d/6u-e** are re-issued as `U_reconcile` and must return the SAME `R` values as they did for `U_blind` in step 6 — identical bodies on the stock-position surfaces for both actors, including the counting reconciliation payload, the single deterministic rebalance row (`U_reconcile` shares `U_blind`'s L2+L3 membership, so the resolver hands it the same two cells) and all five batch shapes. **6s-d is the one row NOT re-issued here**, and deliberately so: it is an absence control on the counter-facing endpoints, which require the requester to be an assigned counter (`apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/InventoryCountingController.php:347-350`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Controllers/CountingItemController.php:46-48`); `U_reconcile` is not assigned to C and would receive a 403, which would prove nothing. Its liveness comes from the other direction — the same three values ARE found by 6s-c on the admin-side payload of the same document, so a scanner that could not see them would fail there first. That equality is the executable statement of the owner's ruling: blind receiving changes what a transfer surface says, and changes nothing about what a stock-position surface says.
8. **Flip case.** `PATCH /fraud-settings {blind_receiving: false}` → `v+1`; as `U_blind` re-issue 6a–6c and 6h–6n: `assertSentinelsPresent(body, P[surface])` for every non-EXCLUDED row of the "after step 5b" column (6b the six values, 6c header, 6h PB's `1600.0000` + the derived P cell `'12.0000'`, 6i PB's `1600.0000`, 6j EXCLUDED for sentinels with `meta.incoming_transfer_masked === false` asserted, 6k/6l request quantities and note, **6m/6n the second receiver's `5000.0000`, `7391.4517` and `1017.2500` with `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked === false` — r8-B1: turning the setting off restores the movement quantities to this same actor**) and version `v+1` on the version-bearing rows (6m/6n carry none); 6a keeps its step-6 key/value assertions (EXCLUDED — the receiver builder is unconditional). `PATCH {blind_receiving: true}` → `v+2`; re-issue 6a–6o: every row passes its step-6 assertions with version `v+2` on the version-bearing rows. This is the server-side statement "no request made after the flip returns a hidden figure"; no client cache is asserted (R4). **R5 invariance across the flip (r9-B1; sub-steps completed in rev 11 under r10-M1):** 6p, 6q, 6r, 6s-a, 6s-b, **6s-c**, 6t-a, **6t-b**, **6t-c** and **6u-a/6u-b/6u-c/6u-d/6u-e** are re-issued after EACH of the two flips and must return the same `R` values every time — the counting `theoretical_qty` snapshots, the rebalance row's `surpluses[0].available` and `excess`, `stock_quantity` on product show and the five batch shapes included — with no `meta` masking flag appearing on any of those surfaces in any state. **6s-d is re-issued as `U_other` after each flip too**, and must stay empty of all three `R` values: the counter-side control is a property of the counting document and must not become reachable because `blind_receiving` moved. A stock position that changed when `blind_receiving` flipped would mean the lane had silently taken option (a) — the branch the owner rejected — and this assertion is what catches it.
9. Both DB lanes run every step; there is no PG-only assertion in this test.

Reviewers: `inventory-costing-reviewer` + `stock-gl-interaction-reviewer` + `tenancy-authz-reviewer` + `frontend-conventions-reviewer` (web + POS client).

## 11. Migration and rollout

1. Migrations (tenant path; `tenants:migrate` per tenant DB, `.claude/context/architecture.md:70`; each written with the §3.1 recipe, each one PG transaction, logged only on success): (a) four counters + named CHECKs on lines and allocations; (b) three receipt tables (PK and NOT NULL columns inside `Schema::create`; named FKs/CHECKs and indexes in the guarded passes); (c) `stock_transfers.closed_*` + `freight_uncapitalized`; (d) `company_fraud_settings.blind_receiving` + `visibility_version`; (e) **backfill, idempotent by predicate, in chunks of 500 through the Query Builder (portable to the SQLite lane; hashes computed in PHP)**: for every `stock_transfers` row with `status = 'completed'` and no `stock_transfer_receipts` row — insert one receipt `kind = legacy_completion`, `receipt_number = 'TRR-LEGACY-' || transfer_number`, `sequence = 1`, `status = posted`, `is_blind = false`, `has_discrepancy = false`, `idempotency_key = 'sys:legacy-completion:' || id` (reserved server namespace, r6-M2), `payload_hash = sha256(idempotency_key)`, `received_by_user_id = COALESCE(completed_by_user_id, initiated_by_user_id)` (initiator is `restrictOnDelete`, `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_05_28_120000_create_stock_transfers_table.php:55`), `received_at = COALESCE(completed_at, updated_at)`; one receipt line per transfer line with `quantity_received = quantity_sent_snapshot = quantity`, `is_lot_tracked` = whether the line has allocations, `in_movement_id` (lot-less lines only) = the single `stock_movements` row with `reference_type = StockTransfer::class, reference_id = transfer id, movement_type = transfer_in, location_id = destination, product_id` — null when zero or several match (documented; `markMovementAsTransfer` writes those columns, `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Application/Services/StockTransferService.php:745-752`); one lot row per allocation with its `in_movement_id` resolved the same way plus `batch_id`; then `UPDATE stock_transfer_lines SET quantity_received = quantity WHERE transfer_id IN (SELECT id FROM stock_transfers WHERE status = 'completed') AND quantity_received = 0`, same for allocations. in_transit/cancelled untouched (M2). Recovery: a rolled-back (unlogged) migration is re-run by the next `tenants:migrate`; post-commit drift is repaired by a new forward migration (§3.1), never by rollback. **Interruption recovery of this backfill is PostgreSQL-only (r6-m2):** on PG the migration transaction discards every chunk of a failed run, so a header without lines cannot persist and the rerun re-selects the transfer; on SQLite (no migration transaction) an interruption between a header insert and its lines/lots would leave a row the predicate skips — no convergence after interruption is claimed there, T11 on SQLite asserts single-pass idempotency (clean rerun adds nothing) only, and the PG lane carries the forced-failure case.
2. Readers switch to `REMAINDER_SQL` + `CARRYING_STATUSES` in the SAME merge as the backfill.
3. No feature flag: endpoints are new; `complete` keeps its contract for non-blind actors; `blind_receiving` defaults off.
4. Permissions: seeder rows for `inventory.transfers.reconcile` and `inventory.transfers.close`, `tenants:seed RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`, `permission:cache-reset`, `permissions:export-frontend-map`; `docs/handoff/PROMOTION-CHECKLIST-*` gets the steps plus the §3.1 manual-recovery line.
5. `docs/03-ERP-INTEGRATION/REALIGNMENT-LOG.md` (root rule 9): new endpoints, `TransferStatus` value set, `complete` 422 in blind mode, close under reconcile + close (403 otherwise), any-of gate on transfer list/show, nullable `incoming_transfer` + `meta.incoming_transfer_masked` + `meta.visibility_version` on the POS feeds, masked replenishment quantities + `transfer_quantities_masked` + `visibility_version` on both replenishment feeds (inside `meta` on the web feed, TOP-LEVEL beside `as_of`/`truncated` on the POS feed — r8-m3), **nullable `quantity`/`quantity_before`/`quantity_after` on transfer-linked carrying rows plus `meta.transfer_movement_quantities_masked` on `GET /stock-movements` and `GET /entry-exit-notes` (r8-B1, §5.10), and the location scope now applied to `GET /entry-exit-notes`**, `visibility_version` on transfer payloads and the fraud-settings DTO (advisory), nullable `incomingTransfer` on the Shared DTOs.
6. `docs/glossary.md` rows (§2), `packages/shared/types/generated.d.ts`, frontend permission map, `apps/pos` types regenerated/updated in the same PR.

---

## 12. Owner rulings applied (none open)

No item of this lane is awaiting an owner line. The one item gate r9 raised under "Owner decisions required" — the current-stock residual — was RULED on 2026-09-09 (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`) and is recorded among the applied rulings below as **R5**. The three items rev 8 carried as "still open" were RULED on 2026-09-09 evening (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:108`), each as the orchestrator default that was already applied throughout this spec, and the owner added the framing "we are still in a green field, nothing to worry about like previous clients" — so **no legacy-device or legacy-tenant compatibility claim is required anywhere in T-2/T-3**, for activation, for the backfill, or for either client cache. Nothing in the spec asks for one.

**Applied rulings (not reopened):** **R5 — current-stock residual, option (b) "the Oracle approach"** (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:110`): blind receiving hides the expected/sent/remaining quantities of the SPECIFIC DOCUMENT being received only — on the mobile app, "the intended primary receiving surface" (§9), and on the web receipt surfaces (§8). In the owner's words, "The goal is not to omit quantities everywhere … everywhere else it's okay": destination on-hand, available and lot stock stay visible under the existing permission model (§5.0 rows 19–24), and current-stock delta inference — including the multi-receiver composition in which a second receiver's posting moves the position while `NOTHING_TO_RECEIVE` proves the line is exhausted — is an ACCEPTED residual. Consequences applied throughout: the §5.0 guarantee is stated over DOCUMENT quantities on transfer, receipt, replenishment, movement/entry-exit and notification surfaces; §5.10's movement masking is unchanged because a movement row is transfer data; §5.0b records every stock-position shape as an R5 residual; T9 asserts absence only on transfer-derived surfaces and adds the R5 presence controls 6p–6u (§10.1). Benchmark basis: Oracle Retail Store Inventory Operations Cloud hides the expected inbound quantity on the receiving document and restricts no inventory dialog (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:9`), and no reference system hides a site's own on-hand from its staff (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:16`) — *rejected alternative (recorded, not open): option (a), mask every current-stock projection (stock levels, matrix `on_hand`, POS stock and distribution, product stock, rebalance, counting reconciliation, batch/lot stock) for a blind actor while any transfer is carrying, because it would hide a shop's own stock from the people who work there and break POS selling, counting and replenishment for the blind role, which no benchmark system does* (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-09-benchmark-blind-receiving-on-hand-visibility.md:16-18`); **OD-1 — blind refuses quantity-less `complete`** (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:108`): `POST /{id}/complete` returns 422 `BLIND_REQUIRES_COUNTED_RECEIPT` whenever `canSeeExpected()` is false; the web "Receive all" button is not rendered in blind mode (server-authoritative `blind`); `complete` stays as a delegate for non-blind / reconcile / source-side actors (§5.6, §8) — *rejected alternative (recorded, not open): allow a blind receiver to `complete` as an explicit "everything arrived, nothing damaged" attestation, because the server would then post the hidden quantity on the receiver's behalf, which defeats the control*; **Close authority (r4-M4)** (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:108`): close requires BOTH `inventory.transfers.reconcile` (visibility) AND a separate `inventory.transfers.close` (write), both seeded to manager + admin and grantable independently (§5.2, §5.8) — *rejected alternative (recorded, not open): `inventory.transfers.reconcile` alone authorises close (rev-4 shape, one permission fewer), because a reconciliation-only auditor would gain a terminal stock/GL mutation; blind safety is identical under both*; **OD-4 — activation semantics** (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:108`): enabling blind receiving is effective server-side at commit; every payload is built for the requesting actor at request time; `visibility_version` is an advisory cache hint; a client converges on its next successful response per surface and may show pre-flip data until then (R4, §4.5) — *rejected alternative (recorded, not open): "effective only after every registered device re-baselines", because one offline device would hold the whole company in non-blind mode*; D1 reconcile permission seeded to manager + admin, grantable (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:98`; §5.4, §5.8); D2 reason only when damaged > 0, shortness at close, receiver-note ticket (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:99`; §1, §5.1 rule 6); OQ-1 `disposition` with no GL on return (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:100`; §5.2, §6.3); OQ-2 leave settled, notify, requester re-raises (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:101`; §4.3); OQ-3 coarse `partially_received` (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:102`; §5.0 R3); pattern detection via stored per-line events + reference query (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:103`; §4.2, §4.4); **freight OQ-4 default CONFIRMED** — capitalisable pool = `transfer_cost × Σ good ÷ Σ sent`, landed-weight allocation, residual persisted as `stock_transfers.freight_uncapitalized decimal(15,4)`, no journal (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:106`; §3.1, §6.4, I7); **multi-receiver attribution CONFIRMED** — per-line events attribute exactly what each receiver posted, a close shortage is a transfer-level fact carried by the closer, the query is per company and never counts supervisors' own actions (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:106`; §4.2, §4.4); accountant out of the PO-revert seed (`docs/handoff/OWNER-QUESTIONS-consolidated-2026-09-06.md:106`; not this lane). Orchestrator decisions recorded, not re-asked: replenishment quantities masked for actors without incoming-aggregate visibility (§5.9); the transactional-migration + logging statement replaces every self-healing promise (§3.1); `receive` stays under `inventory.transfers.complete` rather than the any-of gate (§5.8).
